# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [An asymptotically solvable model of many-body critical phases: mobility edges, scars, and inverted scars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00157)
*Yi-Ting Tu,Zi-Jian Li,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个一维最近邻相互作用自旋链的渐近可解模型，其空间结构诱导出层级镜像多体共振，在热力学极限下得到满足/违反弱本征态热化假设（ETH）的两个相，类似于MBL和ETH相但存在罕见的"疤痕"和"反转疤痕"本征态，两相之间可由有限温度相变（热力学多体迁移率边）分隔，并暗示准周期MBL中存在非局域化稀有区域，挑战了无雪崩不稳定性的普遍信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机MBL系统的预热区由偶然多体共振主导，而确定性大尺寸系统中由势结构引发的另一类共振可导致单粒子临界相，但其在相互作用系统中的后果尚不清楚。

Method: 构造一个一维最近邻相互作用自旋链的渐近可解模型，其空间结构诱导层级镜像多体共振，通过分析热力学极限下的本征态热化性质来区分两个相。

Result: 得到两个热力学相（分别满足和违反弱ETH），存在罕见的"多体疤痕"和"反转疤痕"本征态；两相可由有限温度相变（热力学多体迁移率边）分隔；结果还暗示相互作用Aubry-André模型中存在非局域化稀有区域。

Conclusion: 结构诱导的镜像多体共振可产生不同于传统认识的量子相结构，包括被普遍认为不可能的热力学多体迁移率边，并挑战了准周期MBL无雪崩不稳定性的信念。

Abstract: While the prethermal regime of random many-body localized (MBL) systems is dominated by accidental many-body resonances, another class of resonances, originating from the underlying potential structure, is expected in large-size deterministic systems. It is known that this class of resonances can lead to single-particle critical phases that are neither localized nor extended, but the consequences in interacting systems remain unclear. In this work, we construct an asymptotically solvable model of a one-dimensional nearest-neighbor interacting spin chain, whose spatial structure induces a hierarchy of mirror-like many-body resonances. We derive two phases in the thermodynamic limit, characterized by the satisfaction and violation of a version of the weak eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). While these two phases are similar to the usual MBL and ETH phases, there exist rare eigenstates that behave like the opposite phase, interpreted as many-body scars and inverted scars. Surprisingly, the two phases can be separated by a finite-temperature phase transition, corresponding to a thermodynamic many-body mobility edge, which was often believed to be impossible. Our results also suggest the existence of delocalized rare regions in an otherwise-localized interacting Aubry-André model, even if there are no low-disorder regions like those in random systems. This challenges the common belief that there is no avalanche instability in quasiperiodic MBL.

</details>


### [130] [Neural Quantum States for Nuclear Magnetic Resonance Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00178)
*Bharadwaj Chowdary Mummaneni,Bo Xing*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用神经量子态（NQS）作为变分量子态的替代表示来预测核磁共振（NMR）谱。通过在2至5自旋的分子上测试TDVP和p-tVMC两种传播方法，结果表明TDVP能以<10^-3的谱均方误差精确复现所有谱线位置和强度。为克服大体系面临的积分步数随谱带宽急剧增长的问题，作者提出在化学位移哈密顿量的相互作用系中传播，将积分步数减少8倍至一个数量级以上，并成功实现了14自旋分子（蔗糖）的精确传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一性原理预测NMR谱需要对2^N维量子态进行传播，当自旋数N增大时计算变得不可行。作者旨在探索神经量子态作为这一问题的替代表示，以突破传统方法的规模限制。

Method: 使用两类变分量子态传播方法：（1）时间相关变分原理（TDVP）和（2）投影时间相关变分蒙特卡洛（p-tVMC），在实验参数化的2至5自旋分子上计算^1H谱。此外，通过在化学位移哈密顿量的相互作用系中传播来减少所需积分步数。

Result: TDVP以平均谱均方误差<10^-3复现所有线位置和强度；p-tVMC也能复现相同特征，但精度取决于每步优化参数。相互作用系传播方法在保持精度的同时，将积分步数减少8倍至一个数量级以上，并实现了14自旋蔗糖分子的精确蒙特卡洛传播。

Conclusion: 神经量子态结合相互作用系传播方法能够高效准确地预测NMR谱，大幅扩展了可处理的体系规模（从5自旋扩展至14自旋），为第一性原理NMR谱预测提供了一种有前景的可扩展方案。

Abstract: Predicting a nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectrum from first principles requires propagating a quantum state of dimension $2^N$ for $N$ coupled spins, which becomes intractable beyond larger $N$. We benchmark Neural Quantum States (NQS), a class of variational quantum states expressed as an artificial neural network, as an alternative representation for this problem. Using two propagation methods, the Time-Dependent Variational Principle (TDVP) and projected time-dependent Variational Monte Carlo (p-tVMC), we compute the $^1$H spectra of four ($2 \to 5$ spins) experimentally parameterized molecules. TDVP reproduces all line positions and intensities with average spectral mean squared errors of $<10^{-3}$; p-tVMC reproduces the same features, with accuracy determined by its per-step optimization parameters. One dominant obstacle to larger systems is the steep growth of the number of integration steps with spectral bandwidth, which can be removed by propagating in the interaction frame of the chemical-shifted Hamiltonian. Retaining the same accuracy, this reduces the number of integration steps roughly eightfold for the 3-spin system and by at least an order of magnitude for the 4- and 5-spin systems, and it enables a 14-spin molecule (sucrose) to be accurately propagated via Monte Carlo sampling.

</details>


### [131] [Spin Quantum Hall Effect: the Critical Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00849)
*Hrant Topchyan,Win Nuding,Andreas Klümper,Ara Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文利用近年发展的S-矩阵方法对自旋量子霍尔效应（SQHE）网络模型进行了高精度数值计算，确定了局域化长度临界指数ν和边界临界指数μ，为检验无序拓扑系统与共形场论中的精确临界指数提供了重要依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQHE是少数已知精确临界指数的安德森局域化转变范例，是检验无序拓扑系统和共形场论理论的重要试验场；通过高精度数值计算其临界指数，有助于验证理论预测并深入理解该相变的普适性。

Method: 采用近年开发的S-矩阵方法对随机网络进行计算，取代Chalker-Coddington模型中的随机U(1)相位为随机SU(2)矩阵，构造属于Altland-Zirnbauer分类中C类对称性的SQHE网络模型，并通过高精度数值模拟计算局域化长度指数ν和边界临界指数μ。

Result: 通过S-矩阵方法的高精度数值计算，得到了SQHE网络模型的局域化长度临界指数ν和边界临界指数μ的精确估计值。

Conclusion: 该研究为SQHE临界转变提供了高精度数值参考，验证了该模型在对称类C中的临界行为，并为无序拓扑系统与共形场论的精确指数预测提供了有力的数值检验。

Abstract: The spin quantum Hall effect (SQHE) provides one of the few examples of an Anderson localization transition for which exact critical exponents are known, making it an important testing ground for theories of disordered topological systems and conformal field theory. The corresponding network model, obtained by replacing the random $U(1)$ phases of the Chalker--Coddington model with random $SU(2)$ matrices, belongs to symmetry class C of the Altland--Zirnbauer classification and is believed to describe quasiparticle transport in two-dimensional disordered superconductors with broken time-reversal symmetry. In this work, we perform high-precision numerical calculations of the localization-length exponent ($ν$) and the boundary critical exponent ($μ$) for the SQHE network model using the recently developed $S$-matrix approach to random networks.

</details>


### [132] [Predictive Formulas for Scattering Mean Free Path for General Disordered Dielectric Media Beyond the Long-Wavelength Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01016)
*Jaeuk Kim,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文推导了统计均匀两相介质在维度d=1,2,3下散射平均自由程ℓ_s的预测性解析公式。不同于仅适用于相同圆形或球形散射体的Mie理论估计，该公式适用于任意形状、多分散颗粒介质及非颗粒介质，微观结构通过谱密度引入。公式基于有效动态介电常数的精确强对比展开，并在五种非超均匀和超均匀模型上验证，部分结果与FDTD模拟一致。对k_1/s≲1区域预测与模拟吻合良好，且与适用处Mie理论一致；对超均匀介质预测ℓ_s∼k_1^{-(d+1+α)}，隐形超均匀介质在有限波数区间内透明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mie散射理论仅适用于相同圆形或球形散射体，无法处理任意形状、多分散颗粒以及非颗粒形态的随机介质。散射平均自由程是描述波在无序介质中传输的关键物理量，因此需要一种能够将微观结构（通过谱密度）与波散射行为联系起来的一般性预测框架。

Method: 基于精确强对比展开（strong-contrast expansion）推导有效动态介电常数，由此获得散射平均自由程的解析公式。微观结构信息通过谱密度χ̃_V(k)编码，适用于d=1,2,3维度。选取五种非超均匀和超均匀模型进行验证，并使用有限差分时域（FDTD）模拟进行数值对比。

Result: 在k_1/s≲1区域（入射波数与比表面积之比较小），预测结果与FDTD模拟吻合良好，且与适用范围内的Mie理论一致，并在二维横磁（TM）极化下精度优于Mie估计。在k_1/s≳1区域，Mie估计更精确。对超均匀介质（χ̃_V(k)∼k^α，小k区域），理论预测ℓ_s∼k_1^{-(d+1+α)}；隐形超均匀介质在有限波数区间内完全透明。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条基于微观结构（谱密度）预测和设计一般无序介电材料中波传输的通用路径，克服了Mie理论在散射体几何形状上的局限性，为隐身超均匀介质的波传输设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We derive predictive formulas for the scattering mean free path $\ell_s$ of statistically homogeneous two-phase dielectric media in dimensions $d=1,2,3$. Unlike Mie-based estimates limited to identical circular or spherical scatterers, the formulas apply to arbitrarily shaped and polydisperse particulate media as well as nonparticulate media, with microstructure entering through the spectral density. The formulas are based on the exact strong-contrast expansion for the effective dynamic dielectric constant. We apply them to five nonhyperuniform and hyperuniform models and validate selected cases using finite-difference time-domain simulations. For $k_1/s \lesssim 1$, where $k_1$ is the incident wavenumber and $s$ is the specific surface, the predictions agree well with simulations and are consistent with Mie theory where applicable, while improving accuracy for two-dimensional transverse-magnetic polarization. Mie estimates become more accurate for $k_1/s \gtrsim 1$. For hyperuniform media with $\widetildeχ_V(k)\sim k^α$ at small $k$, the theory predicts $\ell_s\sim k_1^{-(d+1+α)}$; stealthy hyperuniform media are transparent over a finite wavenumber interval. These results provide a microstructure-based route to predict and design wave transport in general disordered dielectric materials.

</details>


### [133] [Griffiths phase in clique percolation in random geometric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01487)
*Vasilii Tiselko,Olga Valba,Alexander Gorsky*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有不同几何约束核函数的随机几何图系综中的团渗流现象。对于锐截止核，发现了具有幂律行为的广义Griffiths相；对于幂律核，识别出团大小依赖的ER态与几何态之间的边界；对于指数核也进行了分析，并讨论了潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解几何约束（以不同核函数形式体现）如何影响随机几何图中的团渗流行为，特别是Griffiths相的存在及其边界条件，为复杂网络中团结构涌现提供理论框架。

Method: 系统比较三种核函数（锐截止、幂律、指数）下的随机几何图系综，通过解析和数值手段分析团渗流的临界行为，识别Griffiths相的边界及其与ER系综渗流临界点的关系。

Result: 锐截止核下发现宽Griffiths相（扩展临界性），其边界分别对应于大但有限团簇内渗流的ER广义临界点和整体团簇系统可渗流的参数点；幂律核下发现更丰富的、团大小依赖的ER/几何态边界，Griffiths相仍存在；指数核模式也被分析。

Conclusion: 不同核函数导致的几何约束显著影响随机几何图的团渗流相图，Griffiths相在不同约束类型下均存在但结构各异，研究结果对理解实际复杂网络中的团簇结构和渗流行为具有应用价值。

Abstract: In this study, we discuss the clique percolation in the ensembles of random geometric graphs with different kernels that quantify the geometrical constraints. For the sharp cut-off we find the wide Griffiths phase of extended criticality with the power-law behavior. One boundary of the Griffiths phase is the generalization of a percolation critical point for the ER ensemble when the percolation within the large but finite cluster emerges. The second boundary corresponds to the point in the parameter space when the percolation in the entire clustered system becomes available. For the power-law kernel, richer behavior with a clique-size-dependent boundary between the effective ER and geometric regimes has been identified. The Griffiths phase in this case exists as well. Finally, the pattern with the exponential kernel has been analyzed. We briefly discuss the possible applications of our findings.

</details>


### [134] [Tunneling the Loss Landscape: Bypassing Memorization with Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01833)
*Lai Shun Chan,Xiaotian Zhang,Yue Shang,Ge Zhang,Entao Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络训练中的"顿悟"（grokking）现象，将其类比为统计物理中的玻璃弛豫过程，并通过一个三组件框架（参数迁移性PM、复制相关性RC、分形维度FD）直接验证了训练动态与玻璃动力学的定量一致性。基于此发现，作者提出了受交换蒙特卡洛算法启发的SAM-Swap优化插件来加速泛化，并揭示了加速泛化与参数空间随机探索之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于grokking的理论大多从经典机器学习机制（如权重范数）或统计物理的宏观理论层面进行解释，但缺乏对训练动态的直接经验验证。作者希望弥合理论框架与实证数据之间的鸿沟，直接在微观训练动态层面验证玻璃弛豫理论。

Method: 作者引入三组件框架来直接刻画训练动态：1) 参数迁移性（PM）衡量参数变化能力；2) 复制相关性（RC）和3) 分形维度（FD）是玻璃动力学中的经典度量。同时提出了SAM-Swap优化插件，借鉴玻璃动力学中广泛使用的交换蒙特卡洛算法，通过参数交换来加速泛化。

Result: 实验表明标准优化表现出清晰的玻璃动力学特征——grokking网络被动力学禁锢在记忆化状态中，具有坍缩的迁移性、强历史依赖性和通道状运动模式。对比SAM-Swap、权重衰减和高斯梯度噪声，发现加速泛化始终与参数空间的随机探索相关联，类似于物理学中的扩散过程。

Conclusion: 研究首次在训练动态层面直接经验验证了grokking的玻璃弛豫理论，提供了定量证据表明标准优化确实将网络困在动力学受阻的亚稳态。SAM-Swap作为实际优化工具能加速泛化，且揭示了参数空间随机探索（类似物理扩散）是加速泛化的关键机制，为grokking的深度理解和新优化方法设计提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Grokking is a striking phenomenon in neural network training, where a model can undergo a prolonged period of pure memorization before abrupt generalization. While previous works have attempted to interpret it through classical machine learning mechanisms like weight norm, recent research draws an analogy from statistical physics, framing grokking as a form of computational glass relaxation. This theory defines the initial memorization as a result of `fast cooling' where the training loss is reduced so quickly that a glass state is formed, followed by a `slow relaxation' towards final generalization. Although providing a unifying framework for representative grokking theories, this perspective has remained largely at the theoretical on macroscopic level without direct empirical validation on training dynamics. Here we introduce a three-component framework to directly characterize the training dynamics via parameter mobility (PM), and two representative measurements from glassy dynamics: replica correlation (RC) and fractal dimension (FD). We demonstrate that standard optimization presents clear signatures of glass dynamics and inherently traps the grokking network in a kinetic arrested memorization state with a collapsed mobility, strong history dependence, and channel-like motions. This quantitative agreement motivates us to introduce State-Aware Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping (SAM-Swap), an optimization plug-in that can accelerate generalization, inspired by swap Monte Carlo algorithm widely used in glass dynamics. Comparing SAM-Swap, weight decay, and Gaussian gradient noise, we find that accelerated generalization is consistently associated with random exploration in the parameter space, similar to diffusion in physics.

</details>


### [135] [Bayesian Inference with Structured Signal: Static Replica Symmetry Breaking on the Nishimori Line in the Planted Spin Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02373)
*Andrea Vincenzo Dell'Abate,Louise Budzynski*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了信号先验中存在相关性对贝叶斯推断的影响，使用随机正则图上的植入自旋玻璃模型，发现根据先验相态的不同，信号中的结构可以促进或阻碍推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论贝叶斯推断模型通常假设信号的分量是独立同分布的，但真实信号往往具有相关性。作者希望研究信号先验中的相关性如何影响推断的相变行为和解码性能，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用最小模型：定义在随机正则图上的植入自旋玻璃，信号从耦合参数为κ的Ising模型中采样。通过分析先验的不同相（顺磁、铁磁、复制对称破缺态），计算重建阈值并分析后验的静态RSB转变。

Result: 在顺磁区，信号相关性降低重建阈值，较弱信号即可恢复；在铁磁区，先验本身已可实现部分恢复，作者确定了观测提供额外信息的阈值；当先验处于RSB相时，在Nishimori条件下检测到后验中的静态RSB转变，为非可分离相关先验导致贝叶斯最优推断中静态RSB提供了首个实例。

Conclusion: 信号先验的相关性对推断有复杂影响：可降低重建阈值（顺磁相）或本身提供部分恢复能力（铁磁相），但在RSB相下会引入玻璃态，可能损害信念传播等算法的性能，需要进一步研究其算法后果。

Abstract: A common assumption in theoretical models of Bayesian inference is that the signal has i.i.d. components. To study the effect of correlations in the signal prior, we consider a minimal model: the planted spin glass on random regular graphs, where the signal is sampled from an Ising model with coupling $κ$. Depending on the phase of the prior, we find that adding structure in the signal can either help or hinder inference. In the paramagnetic regime, correlations in the signal lower the reconstruction threshold, so that weaker signal strength is sufficient for recovery. In the ferromagnetic regime, the prior alone already enables partial recovery, and we identify the threshold above which the observations provide additional information. When the prior itself is in a replica symmetry breaking (RSB) phase, we detect a static RSB transition in the posterior under Nishimori conditions. This provides an example where a non-separable, correlated prior leads to static RSB in a Bayes-optimal inference problem. We discuss the consequences of this glassy phase for algorithmic performance, in particular for Belief Propagation.

</details>


### [136] [Anomalous Localization in Magnetically Doped Two-Dimensional Topological Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24373)
*Felippe Amorim,Washington F. dos Santos,Mauro S. Ferreira,Alexandre Reily Rocha,Caio Lewenkopf*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维拓扑绝缘体（2DTIs）在有限浓度磁性杂质下的边缘输运性质，提出了一个解析框架并辅以数值模拟，预测了安德森局域化的出现，发现了以电导亚指数衰减为特征（ln G ∝ -√L）的异常局域化机制，并证明输运表现出仅由有效杂质浓度控制的普适标度行为。模型应用于Mn掺杂HgTe量子阱与实验数据高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然孤立稀释磁性杂质对二维拓扑绝缘体边缘输运的影响已被充分理解，但在高密度、无序磁性杂质系综下的输运性质仍不清楚，这限制了人们对磁性掺杂拓扑相中反常局域化现象的理论理解。

Method: 开发了一个解析理论框架，并辅以大规模数值模拟，来刻画具有有限浓度磁性杂质的二维拓扑绝缘体的边缘输运行为。该框架考虑了磁性杂质破坏时间反演对称性导致的反向散射效应。

Result: 预测了安德森局域化的出现，发现了一个异常局域化机制，表现为电导的亚指数衰减（ln G ∝ -√L）；证明输运呈现仅由有效杂质浓度决定的普适标度行为；将模型应用于Mn掺杂HgTe量子阱时与实验数据取得极好的一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解磁性掺杂拓扑相中的反常局域化现象提供了理论基础，填补了从稀释杂质到高密度杂质系综之间的理论空白。

Abstract: Two-dimensional topological insulators (2DTIs) harbor spin-polarized edge states that are topologically protected by time-reversal symmetry against non-magnetic structural disorder. However, coupling to magnetic impurities breaks this symmetry, inducing backscattering and destroying perfect quantization. While the impact of isolated dilute magnetic impurities is well understood, the transport properties in the presence of dense, disordered ensembles of magnetic moments remain poorly understood. In this work, we develop an analytical framework, supported by extensive numerical simulations, that captures the behavior of edge transport in two-dimensional topological insulators (2DTIs) with a finite concentration of magnetic impurities. We predict the onset of Anderson localization and uncover an anomalous localization regime characterized by a sub-exponential decay of the conductance, scaling as $\ln {\cal G} \propto -\sqrt{L}$, where $L$ is the system length. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the transport exhibits a universal scaling behavior governed solely by the effective impurity concentration. Applying our model to Mn-doped HgTe quantum wells, we find excellent agreement with experimental data. These findings provide a theoretical foundation for understanding anomalous localization phenomena in magnetically doped topological phases.

</details>


### [137] [Conservation laws determine what physical learning remembers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00097)
*Bijaya Dangol*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究物理学习规则（EP、CL、AL）在电阻网络中的守恒量K（电导质量）及其对训练的影响。证明EP与CL在单输出场景下轨迹等价且守恒K，而AL不守恒并以两倍损失速率耗散K。在线性电路中守恒量无功能后果，但非线性（如二极管）电路中，初始化尺度会显著影响学习结果（达40%），保守规则永久保留这种记忆而耗散规则部分擦除。耗散规则训练更快但泛化更差，惩罚与耗散质量相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解物理学习规则中守恒量如何影响归纳偏置、初始化记忆、训练速度和泛化性能，从而指导物理学习机的设计。

Method: 通过理论分析（轨迹等价证明、守恒量推导、向量场齐次性分析）结合数值实验（线性电路与二极管电路对比），系统考察三种学习规则在不同初始化尺度下的行为差异。

Result: EP和CL单输出轨迹等价且守恒K；AL不守恒并以两倍损失速率耗散K。线性电路中所有规则齐次于电导，选项不受初始化尺度影响；二极管电路中学习结果依赖初始化尺度（最高约40%差异），保守规则永久保留记忆而耗散规则部分擦除。耗散规则训练快但泛化差，惩罚与耗散质量相关且在大电路中消失。

Conclusion: 物理学习规则的守恒结构决定了其初始化记忆、训练速度和泛化行为，应被视为物理学习机器的一个重要设计参数。

Abstract: Physical learning rules such as equilibrium propagation (EP), coupled learning (CL), and adjoint coupled learning (AL) train resistive networks through local measurements. In the small-nudge limit EP and CL exactly conserve the conductance mass K = (1/2) sum_e kappa_e^2, a property that stabilizes training. We show that conservation also governs the inductive bias of these rules. For a single output we prove that EP and CL are trajectory equivalent, so single-output experiments cannot distinguish what the two rules learn. We prove that AL does not conserve the mass but dissipates it at exactly twice its own loss. In linear circuits we prove that the conserved mass has no functional consequence: all three vector fields are homogeneous in the conductances, so the selected solution is independent of the initialization scale. Fixed nonlinear elements break this protection. In diode circuits the learned input-output function depends on the initialization scale by up to about forty percent, an effect absent in linear controls, and the conservative rules retain this memory permanently while the dissipative rule partially erases it. At matched training loss the dissipative rule typically generalizes worse than the conservative rules, although it reaches low training loss faster; the penalty correlates with the mass dissipated en route and fades in larger circuits, where little mass is lost. The conservation structure of a local learning rule thus sets its initialization memory, its training speed, and, where dissipation is appreciable, its generalization; it should be treated as a design parameter of physical learning machines.

</details>


### [138] [Optimal Decoding for Measurement-Based GHZ State Preparation: The Maximum-Utility Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00160)
*Misha Yutushui,Theo Haas,Simon Trebst*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出最大效用解码器（MUD）框架，将测量型基于GHZ态制备中的解码问题定义为最小贝叶斯风险推断，取代传统面向二元逻辑恢复的MWPM和MLD解码器。该框架通过最大化解码态期望效用实现最优解码，并设计了可扩展的两阶段解码器（MWPM+CNN精修）。研究发现仅改进第一阶段的匹配（感知规范选择）就能在高达256×256的系统中接近最优性能，弥合了裸MWPM与最优解码阈值之间87%的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观GHZ态是实现量子计量、加密和容错码的基础资源。当前最先进的基于测量的协议虽具有高效低深度执行的优势，但其性能受限于传统解码器（如MWPM或MLD），这些解码器优化的是二值逻辑恢复，而非最大化GHZ态在二维几何中特有的连续长程有序特征。

Method: 将解码问题重新表述为最小贝叶斯风险推断，引入最大化解码态期望效用的通用范式。具体实现包括：(1) 构造可实现每射解码量子有序度最高值的算法，确立测量型GHZ态制备的最优解码策略；(2) 设计可扩展的两阶段解码器：第一阶段将综合症编码为MWPM的边权重，第二阶段用卷积神经网络（CNN）训练以最大化期望效用，成本仅为最优解码器的一小部分。

Result: 第一阶段的改进本身（使匹配算法感知规范选择而无额外成本）在最大研究规模N=256×256下表现接近最优，弥合了裸MWPM与最优解码阈值之间高达87%的差距。

Conclusion: 最大效用解码器（MUD）推广了MWPM和MLD，建立了一个多功能的通用框架，可通过重新定义效用函数来针对特定实验的操作需求进行显式定制。

Abstract: The meticulous preparation of macroscopic Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states provides a foundational resource for quantum technologies such as metrology, cryptography, and fault-tolerant codes. While state-of-the-art measurement-based protocols offer efficient low-depth execution, their performance can be bottlenecked by conventional decoders, such as minimum weight perfect matching (MWPM) or even maximum-likelihood decoding (MLD), which optimize for $binary$ logical recovery and fail to maximize the $continuous$ long-range order characteristic of a GHZ state for two-dimensional geometries. Here we overcome this limitation by framing the decoding problem as minimum Bayesian risk inference, introducing a general paradigm that maximizes the expected ${utility}$ of the decoded state. Implementing this maximum-utility approach, we construct an algorithm that achieves the highest possible per-shot decoded quantum order and thereby establish an optimal decoding strategy for measurement-based GHZ state preparation. To improve its computational efficiency, we design a scalable two-stage decoder, which first encodes the syndromes into the edge weights of MWPM and then refines the result with a convolutional neural network trained to maximize the expected utility, at a fraction of the cost of the optimal decoder. Remarkably, we find that the first stage alone$\unicode{x2014}$which makes the matching aware of the gauge choice at no cost beyond bare MWPM$\unicode{x2014}$already performs near-optimally up to the largest sizes we study, $N=256\times256$, closing up to $87\%$ of the gap between the bare-MWPM and optimal decoding thresholds. Generalizing MWPM and MLD, the maximum-utility decoder (MUD) establishes a versatile framework that can be explicitly tailored to the operational demands of specific experiments by redefining the utility function.

</details>


### [139] [Marked vertex search on disordered graphs with Rosenzweig-Porter phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00224)
*Sabyasachi Chakraborty,Tilen Cadez,Sonjoy Majumder,Rohit Kishan Ray*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究无序随机图上的标记顶点搜索问题，通过在Erdős-Rényi图上引入Rosenzweig-Porter模型构建双随机系统，发现搜索性能直接追踪底层量子相，且局域化相比遍历相能实现更高的搜索成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子标记顶点搜索算法在规则图上已被证明优于经典算法，但在无序系统中其行为尚未被充分探索。理解无序对量子搜索的影响对于实际量子计算应用至关重要。

Method: 在Erdős-Rényi(ER)图上实现Rosenzweig-Porter(RP)随机矩阵模型，构建具有图连通性和RP无序双重随机性的系统。利用RP模型中可调的人本征、非遍历扩展和局域化相来研究连续时间量子行走搜索。

Result: 证明了RP谱系在稀疏到稠密图约束下仍保持Wigner-Dyson-to-Poisson谱交叉特征，并推导了随图边概率p系统移动的有限尺寸局域化边界解析估计。发现搜索性能直接追踪底层量子相：遍历相支持快速输运但搜索成功率低，而局域化相搜索成功率高但耗时显著更长。

Conclusion: 建立了随机矩阵无序与连续时间量子行走搜索性能之间的直接定量联系，表明无序可以作为一种可调参数被利用于量子搜索协议，而非仅仅是障碍。

Abstract: Quantum marked vertex search algorithms are known to outperform their classical counterparts, yet their behavior in the presence of disorder remains largely unexplored. Here, we address this gap by studying marked vertex search on disordered random graphs. To introduce disorder, we implement the Rosenzweig-Porter (RP) model, a random matrix ensemble with tunable ergodic, non-ergodic extended, and localized phases, on Erdős-Rényi (ER) graphs. This produces a doubly random system where ER graph connectivity randomizes which interactions exist, while RP disorder controls their strength and `on-site' potentials, providing a two-parameter framework to study quantum dynamics on disordered networks. First, we show that the characteristic Wigner-Dyson-to-Poisson spectral crossover of the RP ensemble survives under graph constraints across the sparse-to-dense range, and we derive an analytical estimate for the finite-size localization boundary that shifts systematically with the graph edge probability $p$, consistent with a resonant-hybridization argument. Thereafter, using this disordered graph ensemble, we study the marked vertex search problem and find that search performance tracks the underlying quantum phase directly. Counterintuitively, the ergodic phase, despite supporting fast transport, yields lower success probability than the localized phase, which achieves high success probability at the cost of significantly longer search times. These results establish a direct and quantitative link between random matrix disorder on graphs and the performance of continuous-time quantum walk search, and suggest that disorder, rather than being merely an obstacle, can be exploited as a tunable parameter in quantum search protocols.

</details>


### [140] [Temperature-driven inversion and nonlinear dynamics in ChatGPT-like AIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00939)
*Neil F. Johnson,Frank Yingjie Huo,Bella Xinrui Li*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文发现ChatGPT类AI的decoder温度升高时，其输出熵并不像普通多态系统那样单调增加，而是先经过熵极大值后进入粒子数反转（population inversion），表现出冻结态、循环、间歇性和噪声诱导有序等非线性动力学特征。作者提出存在一个隐藏坐标作为有效非线性映射的状态变量，其轨迹平均值能强预测输出重复性，表明这类AI是可测量和可扰动的可控非线性物理系统而非"随机鹦鹉"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学认为升高温度会增加系统可访问状态的范围从而增加熵，但作者在ChatGPT类AI中发现相反现象，即提高解码温度虽扩大next-token选择范围，但长期输出的人口分布却经历熵极大值后进入人口反转。这一反直觉发现促使作者将其视为非线性物理系统进行深入研究。

Method: 作者对11个AI模型进行了12,000个续写（continuations）的实验，通过自回归反馈驱动长时间输出，观察输出群体的演化行为。他们分析了熵随温度的变化轨迹，识别出冻结态、周期循环、间歇性等非线性动力学特征，并利用轨迹平均来构建隐藏坐标作为有效非线性映射的状态变量，以预测输出重复性。

Result: 发现自回归反馈使长时间输出经过熵极大值后进入粒子数反转，该转变伴随冻结状态、循环、间歇性和噪声诱导有序现象。隐藏坐标的轨迹平均值能强预测独立测试轨迹中的输出重复性，证实了该隐藏坐标作为有效非线性映射状态变量的作用。

Conclusion: ChatGPT类AI不应被视为"随机鹦鹉"，而应被视为一类可控的非线性物理系统，其内部动力学可以被测量和扰动。这一视角为理解和操控大语言模型行为提供了新的物理框架。

Abstract: Increasing the temperature of an ordinary many-state system increases access to a wider range of states and hence increases its entropy. We find the opposite in ChatGPT-like AIs, even though raising the decoder temperature likewise increases access to a wider range of states (next-token choices). Across 12,000 continuations from 11 AIs, autoregressive feedback drives the long-time output population through an entropy maximum and into population inversion. The transition features frozen states, cycles, intermittency and noise-induced ordering. We present evidence of a hidden coordinate that acts as the state variable of an effective nonlinear map. Its trajectory average strongly predicts output repetition in separate test trajectories. ChatGPT-like AIs therefore behave not as `stochastic parrots', but as a new class of controllable nonlinear physical systems whose internal dynamics can be measured and perturbed.

</details>


### [141] [Local network growth: How simple rules drive network complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01853)
*Alexei Vazquez*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本书提出一个核心论点：真实网络的全局规律并非由"优先连接"（富人愈富）等全局规则自上而下强加，而是源自纯粹的局部规则——每个新节点仅基于其已到达的节点及其直接邻居做出决策。通过逐机制论证，作者展示了冲浪者、朋友介绍、基因复制等局部行为如何在聚合层面自发产生真实复杂系统的完整特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去二十年，网络科学的普遍规律被归因于"优先连接"（富者愈富），但这规则在字面意义上要求每个新节点在连接前先勘察整个网络，这在现实中不合理。作者旨在提供一个更简单、更自然的解释框架。

Method: 全书以"一机制一个案例"的方式进行论证，用通俗语言叙述核心思想，将数学推导放在可跳过的方框中。逐一展示引用图、万维网、社交关系、蛋白质相互作用和项目进度表等各类网络如何通过同一套局部规则，在每次局部决策中逐步"自生长"出全局模式。

Result: 论证了从纯局部规则出发，在不依赖全局信息的条件下，能够在聚合层面重现真实复杂系统的完整标志性特征，包括巨型枢纽、短路径、密集局部聚类、社区结构和冗余路径等。

Conclusion: 真实网络的全局规律是从底层涌现的，而非自上而下施加的。局部规则足以构建出真实复杂系统的全部特征，这为理解网络自组织提供了一种更简洁、更符合物理直觉的替代框架。

Abstract: The Internet, a living cell, a circle of friends, a billion-dollar construction project: these systems share almost nothing -- yet, drawn as networks, they look astonishingly alike. Each has a few giant hubs among a multitude of sparsely connected nodes, short paths between any two parts, dense local clustering, communities, and many redundant routes. For two decades such patterns have been credited to "preferential attachment," the rich getting richer -- a rule that, taken literally, asks every newcomer to survey the whole network before it links. This book makes a simpler case, and defends it one mechanism at a time: the global regularities of real networks are not imposed from above but emerge from purely local rules, in which each new node acts only on a node it has reached and that node's immediate neighbours. A surfer following links, a friend introducing a friend, a gene copied with its connections -- none consults the network as a whole, yet each builds, in the aggregate, the full and unmistakable signature of a real complex system. Written for the curious reader as much as the specialist, with the ideas told in plain language and the mathematics set aside in boxes that can be skipped, it shows how citation graphs, the web, social ties, protein interactions, and project schedules all grow themselves from the same handful of local rules -- one local decision at a time.

</details>


### [142] [Spread complexity as a probe in generalized and long-range Aubry-Andre-Harper models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02451)
*Triyas Sapui,Tanoy Kanti Konar,Subinay Dasgupta,Aditi Sen De*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义和长程Aubry-Andre-Harper(AAH)模型中量子淬火的扩展复杂度。在具有能量依赖迁移边的广义AAH模型中，长时平均扩展复杂度在势能跨越迁移边时表现出非解析行为，可精确识别迁移边转变。作者推导了矩和Lanczos系数的解析表达式，发现Lanczos系数在不同迁移边情形下呈现定性不同的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展复杂度(spread complexity)是量子动力学中刻画态演化的关键指标，但在存在迁移边(mobility edges)的准周期系统中尚未被系统研究。理解量子淬火后扩展复杂度的行为，有助于揭示迁移边对Dynamics的影响，并为识别迁移边转变提供新的诊断工具。

Method: 论文在广义AAH模型（支持能量依赖迁移边）和长程AAH模型中研究量子淬火的扩展复杂度。采用Krylov空间的Lanczos算法计算扩展复杂度，分析长时平均行为与局域态密度的展宽对应关系，并解析地推导了淬火矩阵元的矩(moments)及对应的Lanczos系数表达式。

Result: (1) 广义AAH模型中，长时平均扩展复杂度在初态能量对应的迁移边被跨越时呈现非解析行为，能精确识别迁移边转变；(2) Lanczos系数在存在迁移边时表现为初始平台后随Krylov基指标衰减，而常规无迁移边AAH模型为近似常数；(3) 长程跳跃下，从局域相到扩展相淬火时系数随Krylov指标衰减，反向淬火则与AAH结果一致。

Conclusion: 扩展复杂度可作为识别迁移边转变的有效探测工具，其Lanczos系数的行为特征为区分有无迁移边的准周期系统提供了清晰的判据，揭示了迁移边对量子淬火动力学的深刻影响。

Abstract: We investigate the spread complexity of quantum quenches in generalized and long-range Aubry-Andre-Harper (AAH) models, encompassing regimes with and without mobility edges. In particular, in the generalized AAH models supporting energy-dependent mobility edges, we demonstrate that the long-time averaged spread complexity exhibits nonanalytic behavior when the post-quench quasiperiodic potential crosses the mobility edge associated with the energy of the initial eigenstate, thereby accurately identifying the mobility-edge transition. Such a behavior is supported by the spreading of local density of states. We further derive analytical expressions for the moments and the corresponding Lanczos coefficients for quenches between the limits of vanishing and strong quasiperiodic potentials. The Lanczos coefficients display qualitatively distinct behavior depending on the presence of mobility edges - they exhibit an initial plateau followed by a decay with the Krylov basis index, in contrast to the nearly constant behavior of the conventional AAH model without mobility edges. For LR hopping, the coefficients decay with the Krylov basis index for quenches from the localized to the extended phase, while they coincide with the AAH results for quenches in the opposite direction.

</details>

### [143] [Rhombohedral Graphene: A Tale of Many Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00167)
*Ahmed Abouelkomsan,Filippo Gaggioli,Daniele Guerci,Liang Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过非相互作用能带色散导出一个简单指示器，用于识别菱面体石墨烯相图中强关联区域，并开发了结合Hartree-Fock理论的神经网络变分蒙特卡洛方法求解相互作用基态，发现多种无经典类比的新型电子晶体相：维格纳晶体、自掺杂维格纳晶体以及"反晶体"（电子液体中的空穴晶格）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱面体石墨烯展现出极其丰富的关联量子相（从手性超导体到电子晶体），但现有理论方法难以在载流子密度和位移场的大参数空间中系统预测这些强关联相的边界和性质。需要一种简单有效的指示器来识别强关联区域，并发展能准确求解相互作用基态的计算方法。

Method: 1）从非相互作用能带色散推导出一个简单指示器，用于在载流子密度-位移场相图中定位强关联区域；2）开发神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（NN-VMC）方法，并与Hartree-Fock理论结合，用于求解相互作用基态。

Result: 计算揭示了多种无经典类比的电子晶体相，随密度增加依次出现：维格纳晶体、自掺杂维格纳晶体（self-doped Wigner crystal），以及"反晶体"（anticrystal）——即在电子液体中形成的空穴晶格。并讨论了这些相的实验可观测信号及其与超导电性的可能联系。

Conclusion: 菱面体石墨烯中存在一系列超出经典预期的强关联电子晶体相，且这些相可能与超导电性存在潜在关联，为理解这一材料体系中丰富的量子相变和超导机制提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Experiments on rhombohedral graphene have uncovered an extraordinary wealth of correlated quantum phases - from chiral superconductors to electronic crystals - all within a single family of atomically thin materials. Here, we introduce a simple indicator, derived from the noninteracting band dispersion, that identifies strongly correlated regions in the phase diagram of rhombohedral graphene as a function of carrier density and displacement field. We develop a neural-network variational Monte Carlo method, combined with Hartree-Fock theory, to solve the interacting ground states. Our calculation reveals a variety of electron crystals with no classical analog. These include, at increasing density: Wigner crystal, self-doped Wiger crystal, as well as ''anticrystal'', a lattice of holes in an electron liquid. We discuss their experimental manifestations and possible connection to superconductivity.

</details>


### [144] [Shape of Wigner Crystals and Hole Self-Doping in a Mexican-Hat Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00170)
*Minho Luke Kim,Xiao-Gang Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究由墨西哥帽色散ε_k=c₂k²+c₄k⁴的环形费米面诱导的强库仑相互作用维格纳晶体（WC），通过设计轨道形状以最小化WC能量，并利用变分蒙特卡洛方法校准能带参数c₂和c₄，复现实验中随电子密度降低而观测到的两个转变：自旋-谷极化费米液体（盘状费米面）→环形费米面费米液体→维格纳晶体。研究发现，即使优化轨道形状使k=0处电子耗尽，在包含电子-空穴关联后，靠近WC转变处具有k=0空穴自掺杂的WC仍可能量上有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强库仑相互作用下由墨西哥帽色散形成的维格纳晶体的基态性质，特别是轨道形状设计对WC能量的影响，以及如何复现实验观测到的密度驱动的量子相变序列。

Method: 采用变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）计算方法，设计并优化单粒子轨道形状以最小化WC基态能量，包含Jastrow因子描述库仑关联以及描述电子与掺杂空穴之间关联的Jastrow因子。通过校准有效能带参数c₂和c₄来复现实验中的两个密度驱动相变。

Result: 低基态能量要求轨道形状在k=0附近具有电子耗尽（即空穴自掺杂）。即使采用优化轨道形状，在包含电子-空穴关联的情况下，靠近WC转变处带k=0空穴自掺杂的WC仍然能量有利。还估算了掺杂空穴的色散关系。

Conclusion: 墨西哥帽色散系统中，维格纳晶体的最优轨道形状需要在k=0处电子耗尽，且电子-空穴关联对描述WC转变附近的自掺杂状态至关重要，为理解实验观测到的相变序列提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study Wigner crystals (WCs) induced by a strong Coulomb interaction from the ring-like Fermi surface of a Mexican-hat dispersion $ε_k= c_2k^2+c_4k^4$. We design orbital shape in order to minimize the energy of the WC, and find that a low ground-state energy requires an orbital shape with a depletion of electrons near $k=0$. To capture the Coulomb-induced correlations, we include a Jastrow factor as well as a factor describing the correlation between electrons and doped vacancies. Using variational Monte Carlo calculations, we calibrate the effective band parameters $c_2$ and $c_4$ to reproduce the two transitions observed experimentally as the electron density is lowered: from a spin-valley-polarized Fermi liquid with a disk-like Fermi surface, to one with a ring-like Fermi surface, and finally to a WC. We find that, even with an optimized orbital shape that depletes electrons near $k=0$, a WC with hole self-doping near $k=0$ can still be energetically favorable near the WC transition, provided that the electron-vacancy correlation is included. We also estimate the dispersion of the doped hole.

</details>


### [145] [Chiral Magnon Mixing by Symmetry-Breaking in Collinear Ferrimagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00210)
*Dhurba R. Jaishi,Tyler J. Slade,S. X. M. Riberolles,Bing Li,Tianxiong Han,D. M. Pajerowski,D. L. Abernathy,Barry Winn,Melissa Graves-Brook,B. G. Ueland,R. J. McQueeney*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了RMn6Sn6（R = Tb, Er）铁磁材料中的手性磁振子，发现其左右手性磁振子带在基面内的有限动量处交叉，交叉性质可通过稀土磁各向异性或外加磁场调控，在间隙打开时产生动量依赖的混合手性激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁体中的磁振子具有由右手进动定义的自旋角动量，反铁磁体中无净磁化且左右手磁振子简并，而铁磁体具有未补偿的磁亚晶格，应天然存在由内部分子场劈裂的左右手磁振子。本研究旨在揭示铁磁体中手性磁振子带的交叉行为及其可调控性。

Method: 以RMn6Sn6（R = Tb, Er）铁磁体为研究对象，分析其左右手磁振子带在基面内有限动量处的交叉。通过改变稀土磁各向异性或施加外磁场来操纵铁磁有序的对称性，研究带交叉保持为节线或被打开能隙的不同情形。

Result: 发现RMn6Sn6铁磁体中存在左右手磁振子带在基面内有限动量处交叉，形成具有相反动力学手性的模式。交叉可能保持为节线或被打开能隙，取决于铁磁有序的对称性；能隙模式包含混合手性激发，其手性随动量变化。

Conclusion: 铁磁体RMn6Sn6中的手性磁振子带交叉提供了通过对称性调控（稀土磁各向异性或外加磁场）控制磁振子手性拓扑的平台，能隙中的混合手性激发具有动量依赖的手性特征，为手性磁振子物理及潜在自旋电子学应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Magnons in ferromagnets possess spin angular momentum defined by right-handed precession of the moment around the magnetization direction. In antiferromagnets with no net magnetization, left-and right-handed magnons are degenerate in the absence of an applied field. Ferrimagnets possess uncompensated magnetic sublattices, which should natively possess right-and left-handed magnons where their energy is split by the internal molecular field. Here, we show that RMn6Sn6 (R = Tb, Er) ferrimagnets possess right and left-handed magnon bands that cross at finite momentum (k) within the basal plane, defining modes with opposite dynamical chirality. Depending on the symmetry of the ferrimagnetic order, which may be manipulated by varying the rare-earth magnetic anisotropy or with applied field, the band crossing may remain a nodal line or may be gapped. The gapped modes contain hybridized chiral excitations whose chirality becomes k-dependent.

</details>


### [146] [Interplay of spin-orbit coupling, crystal field splitting and correlations: a ghost rotationally invariant slave boson treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00254)
*Xue Sun,Walber Hugo Brito,Andreas Gleis,Ran Adler,Tsung-Han Lee,Gabriel Kotliar,Corey Peters*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用鬼旋转不变滑移玻色子方法（GRISB）研究Sr₂RuO₄的t₂g Hubbard-Kanamori模型，探讨自旋轨道耦合（SOC）、晶体场劈裂与电子关联的相互作用。研究发现U增强SOC而J抑制SOC，GRISB方法随鬼数量增加快速收敛于DMFT和实验结果，并研究了应变驱动的Lifshitz转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Sr₂RuO₄中自旋轨道耦合、晶体场劈裂与电子关联之间的复杂相互作用，并验证GRISB方法能否在较宽参数范围内、低温条件下有效描述强关联体系中SOC相关的静态和动力学可观测量，同时检验其对DMFT和实验结果的收敛性。

Method: 采用鬼旋转不变滑移玻色子方法（GRISB）研究t₂g Hubbard-Kanamori模型；使用密度泛函理论（DFT）和线性化准粒子自洽GW（LQSGW）提取Sr₂RuO₄的紧束缚模型参数；通过增加鬼（ghost）数量考察GRISB对DMFT的收敛性；系统地扫描Hubbard U和Hund耦合J参数，在低温下研究SOC驱动的静态和动力学可观测量。

Result: GRISB对大多数可观测量快速收敛；发现Hubbard U增强自旋轨道耦合，而Hund耦合J抑制自旋轨道耦合；还研究了不同方法下的费米面形状，以及在该材料中由应变驱动的Lifshitz转变。

Conclusion: GRISB是一种高效且收敛良好的方法，能够在大参数范围内研究Sr₂RuO₄中的关联效应与SOC的相互作用，揭示了U和J对SOC的相反调控作用，并阐明了费米面形变与Lifshitz转变的行为。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay of spin-orbit coupling, crystal field splittings, and electronic correlations in the $t_{2g}$ Hubbard-Kanamori model within the recently formulated ghost rotationally invariant slave-boson method (GRISB). In particular, we study a tight binding model of Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ with parameters extracted from density functional theory and linearized quasiparticle self-consistent GW (LQSGW) calculations; we study the behavior of different physical quantities as the number of ghosts increases to examine the convergence of GRISB to dynamical mean field theory (DMFT) and experimental results; and we leverage the ability of GRISB to investigate the model over a wide range of parameters at low temperature. In particular, we examine both static and dynamical observables driven by the spin orbit coupling (SOC) and study how they vary as a function of the Hubbard $U$ and Hund's coupling $J$. GRISB converges quickly for most of these observables, and the calculations reveal the following: $U$ enhances the spin-orbit coupling while $J$ suppresses it. We also study the shape of the Fermi surface within different methodologies, and examine the Lifshitz transition which takes place as a function of strain in this material.

</details>


### [147] [Breakdown of Universal Whirling Order in a Heisenberg Tsai-Type Approximant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00467)
*Farid Labib,Guillaume Beutier,Alessandro Bombardi,Marc de Boissieu,Ryuji Tamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过X射线共振磁散射实验，发现非共面旋进磁序在Heisenberg型Tsai准晶近似物中并未表现出普遍性，Au-Al-Gd（Heisenberg型）与Au-Al-Tb（非Heisenberg型）尽管晶体结构几乎相同且磁性能量尺度相近，却稳定了不同的反铁磁基态，证明旋进磁序的普遍性需要自旋各向异性而非仅依赖Tsai簇几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期提出非共面旋进磁序是非Heisenberg型Tsai准晶近似物的通用磁性基态，但这一普遍性假设尚未在Heisenberg极限下得到验证，需要检验其是否仅源于Tsai簇的几何结构还是依赖于自旋各向异性。

Method: 对Heisenberg型Au-Al-Gd和非Heisenberg型Au-Al-Tb 1/1近似物进行X射线共振磁散射（XRMS）测量，比较两者的方位角依赖关系并对比旋进磁序理论模型。

Result: Au-Al-Tb体系与已知旋进磁结构高度吻合；而Au-Al-Gd体系的方位角依赖显示定性差异，与通用旋进模型不兼容。尽管二者晶体结构相似、磁性能量尺度相当，却分别稳定了传播矢量同为k=(1,0,0)但结构不同的反铁磁基态。

Conclusion: 旋进磁序的普遍性并非Tsai簇几何结构的必然结果，其稳定需要自旋各向异性条件；研究还揭示出在Heisenberg极限下存在一个近简并磁性态的竞争流形。

Abstract: Noncoplanar whirling magnetic order has recently been proposed as a universal magnetic state in non-Heisenberg Tsai-type quasicrystal approximants. Here, X-ray resonant magnetic scattering measurements on Heisenberg and non-Heisenberg Au-Al-R (R = Gd, Tb) 1/1 approximants reveal a clear breakdown of this universality in the Heisenberg limit. While the non-Heisenberg Au-Al-Tb system is well described by the previously identified whirling magnetic structure, the Heisenberg Au-Al-Gd counterpart exhibits qualitatively different azimuthal-angle dependences that are incompatible with the universal whirling-order model. Despite nearly identical crystal structures and comparable magnetic energy scales, the two systems stabilize distinct antiferromagnetic ground states with the same propagation vector k = (1, 0, 0), demonstrating that the apparent universality of whirling order is not a consequence of Tsai-cluster geometry alone but requires spin anisotropy. These results further reveal the emergence of a competing manifold of nearly degenerate magnetic states in the Heisenberg limit.

</details>


### [148] [Negative thermal expansion, lattice dynamics, and complex magnetism in TbFeO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00469)
*Shubham Farswan,Reshma Kumawat,Dipankar Sarkar,Deeksha Singh,Md. Atif Hasan,Devajyoti Mukherjee,Kaushik Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过X射线衍射、DC磁化率、拉曼散射和X射线光电子能谱对正交铁氧体TbFeO₃进行了变温系统研究，揭示了5–300 K范围内的负热膨胀、自旋-声子耦合、两种声子模式的高斯-洛伦兹线型转变、Fe亚晶格的双磁振子激发，以及~175 K以下出现的具有序参量式温度演化的宽拉曼模式，确立了该材料中晶格动力学、自旋关联与局域磁-晶格异常之间的显著相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解正交铁氧体TbFeO₃中晶格、自旋及其耦合行为，揭示其在低温下的负热膨胀、自旋-声子耦合以及磁-晶格异常之间的内在联系，为稀土正铁氧体类材料的物性研究提供实验基础。

Method: 综合运用变温X射线衍射、DC磁化率测量、变温拉曼散射光谱，并结合室温X射线光电子能谱，分析晶格参数变化、声子模式行为、磁振子激发及电子结构。

Result: 发现5–300 K范围内无结构相变但存在负热膨胀；拉曼声子频率偏离传统Klemens非谐衰减模型，表明存在自旋-声子耦合；A_g和B_1g模式在低温下呈高斯主导线型、高温下转为高斯-洛伦兹混合线型；高能拉曼谱中观察到与Fe亚晶格相关的双磁振子激发，其谱权重随温度变化微弱；~175 K以下出现一个宽拉曼模式，其温度演化呈序参量式特征并伴随声子异常，但双磁振子响应无显著对应异常。

Conclusion: TbFeO₃展现出晶格动力学、自旋关联与局域磁-晶格异常之间的显著耦合，是一种研究自旋-声子相互作用和相关磁学现象的重要模型材料。

Abstract: We report a temperature-dependent investigation of orthoferrite TbFeO$_3$ using x-ray diffraction, DC magnetization, and Raman scattering, complemented by room-temperature x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy. X-ray diffraction reveals negative thermal expansion over 5-300 K, with a small but systematic increase in unit-cell volume upon cooling in the absence of any structural phase transition. Raman scattering measurements identify the Raman-active phonon modes and show clear deviations from the conventional Klemens anharmonic decay model, particularly in phonon frequencies, indicating the presence of spin-phonon coupling. Two modes of $A_g$ and $B_{1g}$ symmetry exhibit a crossover from Gaussian-dominated line shapes at low temperatures to mixed Gaussian-Lorentzian profiles at higher temperatures, reflecting a transition from inhomogeneous broadening to lifetime-driven dynamics. High-energy Raman spectra reveal two-magnon excitations associated with the Fe sublattice, consistent with linear spin-wave theory, whose spectral weight shows only weak temperature dependence. In addition, a broad Raman mode emerging below $\sim 175$ K exhibits an order-parameter-like temperature evolution and coincides with the onset of phonon anomalies, while no corresponding strong anomaly is observed in the two-magnon response. Taken together, these results establish TbFeO$_3$ as a system with pronounced interplay among lattice dynamics, spin correlations, and emergent local magnetic-lattice anomalies.

</details>


### [149] [Spin-orbit-entangled frustrated magnetism in fcc Ba$_2$(Yb,Nd)NbO$_6$ double perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01022)
*S. M. Hossain,Sk. Soyeb Ali,S. Mohanty,R. Kolay,M. P. Saravanan,A. K. Yogi,Y. Tokiwa,R. Nath,S. K. Panda,M. Majumder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种稀土基双钙钛矿材料Ba₂YbNbO₆和Ba₂NdNbO₆，它们在面心立方结构中展现出由强自旋轨道耦合和晶体电场效应产生的孤立j_eff=1/2 Kramers双基态。毫开尔文温度下的热力学测量揭示了两种化合物中具有中等阻挫的长程磁有序，其出现可通过order-by-disorder机制理解，与有限Kitaev相互作用的稀土fcc晶格理论预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev材料候选物的搜索主要集中在4d和5d过渡金属化合物上，而稀土4f体系的Kitaev材料研究仅限于蜂窝和三角晶格结构。作者希望拓展稀土体系中Kitaev材料的搜索范围，探索面心立方结构中的稀土双钙钛矿材料。

Method: 通过磁化率和比热测量表征材料的磁性质；利用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算支持晶体电场效应和自旋轨道耦合影响下的j_eff=1/2 Kramers双基态；在毫开尔文温度下进行热力学测量以探测长程磁有序。

Result: 两种化合物均表现出孤立的j_eff=1/2 Kramers双基态，源自强SOC和晶体电场效应，DFT计算予以支持。在毫开尔文温度下两种化合物均表现出具有中等阻挫的长程磁有序，这一行为可通过order-by-disorder机制理解，与含有限Kitaev相互作用的稀土fcc晶格的理论预测一致。

Conclusion: Ba₂YbNbO₆和Ba₂NdNbO₆是很有前景的稀土自旋轨道纠缠磁性材料，需要进一步实验和理论研究来确定完整的交换张量，以阐明底层磁性相互作用的微观起源。

Abstract: The search for candidate Kitaev materials has largely focused on 4$d$ and 5$d$ transition-metal compounds with various lattice geometries. In contrast, investigations of rare-earth 4$f$ systems have thus far been restricted mainly to honeycomb and triangular lattices. In this work, we investigate the rare-earth-based double perovskites Ba$_2$YbNbO$_6$ and Ba$_2$NdNbO$_6$, which crystallize in a face-centered cubic structure. Magnetization and heat-capacity measurements establish isolated ${j_{\rm eff}} = 1/2$ Kramers doublet ground states arising from strong spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and crystal electric-field effects, which are further supported by density-functional theory calculations. Millikelvin-temperature thermodynamic measurements reveal long-range magnetic order with moderate frustration in both compounds. The emergence of magnetic order may be understood within an order-by-disorder scenario, as theoretically proposed for rare-earth fcc lattices with finite Kitaev interactions. Our results thus identify Ba$_2$YbNbO$_6$ and Ba$_2$NdNbO$_6$ as promising rare-earth spin-orbit-entangled magnets and motivate further experimental and theoretical investigations aimed at determining the complete exchange tensor to elucidate the microscopic origin of the underlying magnetic interactions.

</details>


### [150] [Magnetic circular dichroism of THz modes and selection rules of Raman-active optical phonons in the polar altermagnet candidate \ce{Mn2Mo3O8}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01062)
*F. Schilberth,K. Vasin,M. Knauft,M. Kondákor,M. Vuckovic,N. Herrmann,K. Penc,M. Minola,B. Keimer,V. A. Martinez,K. Panda,A. A. Sirenko,L. Prodan,V. Tsurkan,A. A. Tsirlin,I. Kézsmárki,J. Deisenhofer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过温度依赖的拉曼散射和磁光太赫兹时域透射光谱，结合第一性原理计算，系统研究了共线交替磁体候选材料Mn2Mo3O8的磁与振动激发。作者识别了全部光学声子（含此前未观测到的低声子A1和E2模式），分析了顺磁与磁有序相的光学选择定则与赝角动量守恒，未观测到E2声子的圆偏振劈裂（归因于Mn2+自旋轨道耦合较弱），但在磁有序态观测到强磁圆二色性（可能源于双磁振子激发），且该信号在自旋翻转转变（4T）以上消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体（altermagnet）是一种新兴的磁性材料类型，其磁性与振动激发之间的耦合对理解其物理性质至关重要。作者旨在通过高精度光谱与理论计算结合的方式，全面了解Mn2Mo3O8这一共线交替磁体候选材料中的声子谱、磁振子激发及其相互作用，特别是此前未被观测到的低频拉曼模式和磁有序导致的磁光效应。

Method: 本文采用实验与理论相结合的方法：1）温度依赖的拉曼散射光谱测量，用于识别振动模式并分析选择定则；2）磁光太赫兹时域透射光谱测量，用于研究磁激发与磁圆二色性；3）第一性原理（ab initio）计算精确捕捉振动本征模式的频率，辅助声子模式标定；4）基于赝角动量守恒分析拉曼选择定则，并对比顺磁相与磁有序相中光学活性声子的选择规则。

Result: 1）识别出包括此前未观测到的A1和E2型最低频拉曼模式在内的所有光学声子；2）在磁有序态下未观察到预期中简并E2声子因磁序引起的圆偏振分裂，推测与Mn2+弱自旋轨道耦合有关；3）在磁有序态观测到宽频太赫兹激发带的强磁圆二色性，该带可能源于双磁振子激发，仅具有电偶极活性，并具有随磁场变化的两组分精细结构；4）磁圆二色性在4T自旋翻转转变以上消失。

Conclusion: 本研究为交替磁体Mn2Mo3O8的声子谱和磁激发提供了全面而精确的表征。声子未发生磁序引起的圆偏振能量劈裂这一负结果，与弱自旋轨道耦合一致；而磁有序态下出现的电偶极活性双磁振子激发及其磁圆二色性，则揭示了该材料中磁光响应的独特来源。这些发现为理解交替磁体中磁振子-声子相互作用及磁光效应提供了重要实验依据。

Abstract: We investigated the magnetic and vibrational excitations in the collinear altermagnet candidate \ce{Mn2Mo3O8} by temperature dependent Raman scattering and magneto-optical THz time-domain transmission spectroscopy. By comparison to \textit{ab initio} calculations accurately capturing the eigenfrequencies of the vibrational eigenmodes, we identify all optical phonons, including the lowest-lying Raman modes of $A_1$ and $E_2$ type, which had remained elusive in a previous Raman study. Moreover, we compare the selection rules for optically active phonons in the paramagnetic and the magnetically ordered phases of \ce{Mn2Mo3O8} and analyze the Raman selection rules with respect to pseudo-angular momentum conservation. No evidence of the expected splitting of the degenerate paramagnetic $E_2$ optical phonons into modes with circular polarization upon magnetic ordering could be resolved, likely due to weak spin-orbit coupling typical for Mn$^{2+}$. In contrast, we observe strong magnetic circular dichroism at a broad THz excitation band, emerging in the magnetically ordered state. This band, potentially originating from two-magnon excitations, is only electric-dipole active and features a field-dependent two-component fine structure. Its magnetic circular dichroism vanishes above the spin-flop transition at 4~T.

</details>


### [151] [Pressure-induced self-doping and Fermi surface reconstruction in UAs2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01244)
*Zhenchao Wu,Yingying Cao,Yi-feng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过DFT+DMFT方法研究重费米子化合物UAs2在压力下的电子结构，发现压力诱导的轨道选择性电荷重分布（5f5/2向5f7/2转移电子）形成自掺杂效应，导致费米面重构，并在特定压力区间出现费米面嵌套增强与电荷涨落，从而解释了该材料中创纪录的超导转变温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAs2在压力下被报道表现出重费米子超导电性，其临界温度在铀基5f电子关联超导体中最高，但其微观起源尚不清楚，需要通过电子结构计算来阐明超导机理。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）计算UAs2在常压和不同压力下的电子结构，与角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）结果进行对比验证，并分析5f轨道占据、费米面拓扑及嵌套性质在压力下的演化。

Result: 常压下计算重现了近费米能处Γ和M点附近的平坦杂化带，与ARPES吻合。压力下，电子从局域的5f5/2轨道转移到巡游性更强的5f7/2轨道，总5f占据几乎不变，形成轨道选择性自掺杂效应。超导出现在费米面由两个不连通片层且嵌套增强的压力区间，而在费米面弯曲合并成三维褶皱圆柱体时超导消失。

Conclusion: UAs2的超导增强可能源于费米面嵌套和电荷涨落的协同作用，这与传统的重费米子超导体存在区别，为理解铀基超导体的超导机制提供了电子结构层面的依据。

Abstract: Superconductivity has recently been reported in the heavy-fermion compound UAs2 under pressure, with the highest Tc among uranium-based correlated 5f-electron superconductors. To elucidate its microscopic origin, we investigate its electronic structure using density functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT). At ambient pressure, our calculations reproduce the characteristic Kondo-lattice electronic structure, with flat hybridization bands near the Fermi energy around the Γ and M points, in good agreement with angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). Under pressure, we find a systematic transfer of electrons from the more localized 5f5/2 orbitals to the more itinerant 5f7/2 orbitals, while the total U-5f occupancy remains nearly unchanged. This orbital-selective charge redistribution constitutes a pressure-induced self-doping effect that drives the 5f5/2 electrons from a localized Kondo regime toward a mixed-valence regime with enhanced charge fluctuations, leading to a dramatic reconstruction of the low-energy electronic structure. Remarkably, superconductivity emerges in the pressure range where the Fermi surface consists of two disconnected sheets with enhanced nesting, but disappears when they bend and merge into a corrugated three-dimensional cylinder. Our results provide an electronic-structure basis for understanding superconductivity in UAs2 and suggest that Fermi-surface nesting and charge fluctuations may contribute to the enhanced superconducting Tc, pointing to a possible distinction from conventional heavy-fermion superconductors.

</details>


### [152] [All-Electric Topological Phase Transitions in Proximity-Coupled Bilayer MnBi2Te4 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01312)
*Basavaraja G,Mukul Kabir*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无外磁场的电场可调机制，通过在MnBi2Te4超薄膜表面与铁磁绝缘体界面引入磁邻近效应，利用栅极调控铁磁体的磁各向异性来动态调制邻近诱导的交换偏置，从而实现在轴向子绝缘体与量子反常霍尔态之间的可逆拓扑相变，同时通过封装显著提升磁有序温度，为高温拓扑电子学提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MnBi2Te4在二维极限下具有厚度依赖的轴向子绝缘体和量子反常霍尔态，但两者之间的可控相变通常需要超过9T的极端外磁场，严重限制了实际可调谐性，亟需一种无外磁场且可逆的相变调控机制。

Method: 采用互补的第一性原理计算和有效哈密顿模型，研究MnBi2Te4超薄膜与铁磁绝缘体界面处的磁邻近效应；通过栅极可调的磁各向异性动态调制邻近诱导的交换偏置，实现层间耦合和能带拓扑的全电学切换；并使用长程海森堡模型蒙特卡洛模拟验证封装后MnBi2Te4薄膜的磁有序温度提升。

Result: 实现了无外磁场的、可逆的拓扑相变工程机制，能够通过全电学方式切换超薄MnBi2Te4的层间耦合和能带拓扑；同时蒙特卡洛模拟显示封装后的MnBi2Te4薄膜磁有序温度被显著提升，消除了高场需求并改善了热稳定性。

Conclusion: 该栅极可调范式消除了拓扑相变对外加高磁场的依赖，同时改善了热稳定性，解决了拓扑物理中的关键瓶颈，为可扩展的高温拓扑电子学提供了可行的实现路径。

Abstract: The intrinsic magnetic topological insulator MnBi2Te4, in the two-dimensional limit, hosts thickness dependent axion and quantum anomalous Hal (QAH) insulating states governed by antiferromagnetic interlayer coupling. However, controlled interconversion between these phases typically requires extreme external magnetic fields exceeding 9 T, limiting practical tunability. Using complementary first-principles calculations and effective Hamiltonian modeling, we demonstrate a field-free, reversible mechanism to engineer topological phase transitions by exploiting magnetic proximity at the interfaces with a ferromagnetic insulator. Gate-tunable magnetic anisotropy within the ferromagnetic insulator dynamically modulates the proximity-induced exchange bias, enabling all-electric switching of interlayer coupling and band topology in ultrathin MnBi2Te4. Crucially, long-range Heisenberg Monte Carlo simulations reveal that the magnetic ordering temperature of the encapculated MnBi2Te4 film is dramatically elevated. By eliminating the high-field requirement and simultaneously improving thermal stability, this gate-tunable paradigm solves a critical bottleneck in topological physics and offers a viable route toward scalable, high-temperature topological electronics.

</details>


### [153] [Quantum geometry and RKKY in flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01363)
*Chang-geun Oh,Makoto Shimizu,Youichi Yanase,Shuichi Murakami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了平带极限下RKKY相互作用并非被消失的群速度所湮灭，而是由布洛赫态的量子几何（特别是布里渊区平均量子度量）所介导，量子度量控制静态磁化率的远程结构，决定磁关联长度和自旋刚度，并在有限/低维体系中调控有序温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RKKY理论基于色散驱动的物理图像，依赖传导电子的群速度作为有效交换相互作用的中介。平带系统群速度消失，按照传统观点RKKY相互作用应被熄灭，这挑战了现有的理解框架，因此亟需新的理论以解释平带系统中磁性相互作用的存在机制。

Method: 从微观RKKY推导出发，建立静态磁化率与布洛赫态量子几何之间的关系。证明布里渊区平均的量子度量控制静态磁化率的长波结构，从而决定磁关联长度与自旋刚度。进一步推导出有限和低维样品中有序温度由量子度量主导的普适原理。

Result: 1) 平带极限下RKKY相互作用依然有效，由量子几何而非群速度介导；2) Wannier函数的有限空间延展提供了无单粒子色散情况下的有效长程耦合通道；3) 增大量子度量可增强磁性刚度并提高有限尺寸系统中的临界温度；4) 量子度量可在有限/低维体系中绕过Mermin-Wagner定理的热力学极限约束。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子几何与磁性相互作用之间的深刻联系，表明量子度量是平带系统中控制磁性序的核心物理量，为理解和调控平带磁性材料提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Flat conduction bands quench the group velocity and thus challenge conventional, dispersion-driven pictures of the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interaction, where localized moments are coupled via an effective exchange mediated by conduction electrons. Here we show that RKKY interactions in the flat-band limit are not extinguished by the vanishing group velocity but are instead mediated by the quantum geometry of Bloch states. Starting from a microscopic RKKY derivation, we demonstrate that the Brillouin-zone-averaged quantum metric controls the long-wavelength structure of the static susceptibility, thereby determining the magnetic correlation length and the spin stiffness. As a result, the finite spatial spread of Wannier functions provides an effective long-range coupling channel even when single-particle dispersion is absent. Furthermore, we establish the general principle that the ordering temperature is governed by the quantum metric in finite and low-dimensional samples, effectively circumventing the thermodynamic-limit constraint of the Mermin-Wagner theorem. Specifically, our theoretical investigation reveals that increasing the quantum metric enhances magnetic rigidity and leads to a corresponding rise in the critical temperature within finite-sized systems.

</details>


### [154] [Emergent modular Luttinger liquid from spin-partitioned entanglement in the one-dimensional Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01817)
*Ádám Bácsi,Catalin Pascu Moca,Balázs Dóra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过玻色化与精确对角化相结合的方法，研究一维排斥性Hubbard模型的自旋分块纠缠哈密顿量，发现其表现为一种"模Luttinger液体"，其模谱完全无色散、纠缠隙与动量无关，但本征态具有由电荷和自旋Luttinger参数的几何平均值控制的代数关联，纠缠谱呈现普适的分支层级结构，且模基态与无自旋Luttinger液体几乎相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相互作用一维费米子系统中纠缠哈密顿量的普适结构，揭示自旋分块后系统的模结构与其物理系统之间的本质差异。

Method: 将玻色化（bosonization）与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）相结合，对一维排斥性Hubbard模型的自旋分块纠缠哈密顿量进行系统研究。

Result: 发现模谱完全无色散且纠缠隙与动量无关；本征态展现由电荷和自旋Luttinger参数几何平均值决定的代数关联；纠缠谱呈现与精确对角化高度一致的分支层级结构；模基态与无自旋Luttinger液体几乎相同。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了一维相互作用费米子系统中存在普适的模结构（modular structure），为理解超越物理系统的纠缠哈密顿量性质提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the spin-partitioned entanglement Hamiltonian of the one-dimensional repulsive Hubbard model. By combining bosonization with exact diagonalization, we show that tracing out one spin species produces a modular Luttinger liquid, whose properties fundamentally differ from those of the physical system. While the modular spectrum is fully dispersionless and possesses a momentum-independent entanglement gap, its eigenstates exhibit algebraic correlations governed by a single effective Luttinger parameter equal to the geometric mean of the charge and spin Luttinger parameters. The resulting entanglement spectrum displays a universal branching hierarchy in excellent agreement with exact diagonalization. We further demonstrate that the modular ground state is nearly identical to that of the spinless Luttinger liquid. These results uncover a universal modular structure in interacting one-dimensional fermionic systems.

</details>


### [155] [Topological Defects in Triple-$Q$ Magnetic Orders: A Fixed-Lattice Homotopy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01838)
*Jin-Tao Jin,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对具有(V4⋊D3)×O(N)对称性的M点三重-Q Ginzburg-Landau理论中N=2和3的七个稳定相进行了体缺陷分类，给出了自由内部缺陷、晶体畴壁和壁束缚复合缺陷的完整分类框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重-Q磁性序态将连续自旋旋转与离散晶体学扇区(平移和点群变换)结合，产生比传统单-Q磁体更丰富的缺陷结构。此前缺乏对这些系统缺陷的完整分类，特别是参考态稳定子H与连通分量的关系、分数绕数缺陷的拓扑约束等问题尚不明确。

Method: 利用M点三重-Q Ginzburg-Landau理论，将原子晶格视为预设背景(排除位错和向错)，保持三个傅里叶场的物理M点标签不变。基于G0={e}×SO(N)作为连通到恒等的母群变换，通过G0/(H∩G0)而非投影商来分析参考态稳定子H的连通分量，从而对七个稳定相进行分类。

Result: 正交三重-Q相具有完整流形O(3)，产生手性壁和Abelian Z2框架涡旋而非非Abelian二面体群涡旋；O(2)相的每个连通分量支持整数2π涡旋，而分数绕数需附着于离散畴壁且受到线性禁闭；平移对称性禁止交叉梯度双线性项，将二次弹性扇区简化为各向同性和M点锁定各向异性刚度两种。

Conclusion: 本文首次完整区分了三重-Q磁体中的自由内部缺陷、晶体畴壁和壁束缚复合缺陷，揭示了参考态连通分量的正确数学构造(G0/(H∩G0))，澄清了正交三重-Q相涡旋的Abelian性质，并指出分数绕数涡旋在非零壁张力下受到线性禁闭。

Abstract: Multiple-$Q$ magnetic orders combine continuous spin rotations with discrete crystalline sectors associated with translations and point-group transformations, producing a richer defect structure than conventional single-$Q$ magnets. We classify the bulk defects of all seven stable phases for $N=2$ and $3$ in the $M$-point triple-$Q$ Ginzburg--Landau theory with $(\Vfour\rtimes\Dthree)\times\OO(N)$ symmetry, where $\Vfour$ is the translation-generated Klein four-group. The atomic lattice is treated as a prescribed background, with lattice dislocations and disclinations excluded and the three Fourier fields retaining their physical $M$-point labels. The parent-group transformations continuously connected to the identity form $G_0=\{e\}\times\SO(N)$. For a reference-state stabilizer $H$, the connected component containing the reference state is $G_0/(H\cap G_0)$, not the quotient obtained by projecting $H$ onto spin space. This distinction gives the orthogonal triple-$Q$ phase the full manifold $\OO(3)$, with chirality walls and Abelian $\ZZ_2$ frame vortices rather than non-Abelian binary-polyhedral vortices. Every connected component of the $\OO(2)$ phases supports an integer $2π$ vortex, whereas fractional windings close only when attached to a discrete-domain wall and are linearly confined at nonzero wall tension. Translation symmetry further forbids cross-gradient bilinears, reducing the quadratic elastic sector to an isotropic and an $M$-point-locked anisotropic stiffness. The classification separates free internal defects, crystalline domain walls, and wall-bound composites in triple-$Q$ magnets.

</details>


### [156] [Diodes and capacitors for the transport of monopoles in fragmented spin ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01883)
*Anoop Raj,Sumiran Pujari,Ludovic D. C. Jaubert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了利用磁性碎片化自旋冰系统设计磁单极子二极管和电容器的理论概念验证。通过磁性有序带来的畴壁不对称过滤特性，实现正负磁单极子的分离和定向输运，并通过超过百万自旋的模拟验证了该概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋冰材料中的磁单极子可以传导交流磁流（即"磁电性"），但此前研究主要集中在单极子动力学本身，几乎没有涉及设计器件来控制磁电性。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出在碎片化自旋冰系统中利用磁有序和畴壁来设计控制磁单极子输运的功能器件。

Method: 利用磁场碎片化系统中自旋冰物理与长程反铁磁有序共存的性质。磁有序允许畴壁存在，在特定制备条件下，畴壁作为单极子的不对称过滤器：一个方向上正电荷通过而负电荷被排斥，反方向则相反，形成二极管效应。多个连续畴壁将正负电荷分离并产生电荷真空区域，构成电容器。通过超过一百万自旋的模拟验证所有微观机制。

Result: 成功证明碎片化自旋冰中的畴壁可作为磁单极子二极管和电容器的功能元件，并通过大规模自旋模拟（超过一百万个自旋）验证了概念。该器件在充电后原则上可作为磁单极子电池使用。应用前景涵盖稀土烧绿石氧化物和人工自旋冰体系。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁单极子器件的实际设计提供了理论概念验证，并揭示了碎片化自旋冰中的畴壁可视为分隔两个由时间反演对称性联系的镜像"世界"的涌现边界。超越自旋冰范围，为手性和向列自旋液体中涌现准粒子跨越畴壁的动力学研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Spin-ice materials are famous for their quasi-particle excitations that behave like magnetic monopoles. Magnetricity is the concept that these monopoles can conduct an AC magnetic current, in analogy with conduction electrons. While monopole dynamics has been intensively studied and is reasonably well understood, very little has been done to design devices in order to control magnetricity. Here we develop a theoretical proof of concept for the design of diodes and capacitors for the transport of monopoles. We use the property of systems with magnetic fragmentation, where spin-ice physics co-exists with long-range antiferromagnetic order. The key point is that magnetic order allows for the existence of domain walls. Under certain conditions of preparation, this domain wall is equivalent to an asymmetric filter for monopoles. In a given direction, positive charges can go through while negative ones are repelled; the opposite applies in the opposite direction. This asymmetry effectively functions like a diode for monopole current. Successive domain walls separate positive from negative charges with a vacuum of charge in between, producing a capacitor for monopoles. Once the capacitor is charged, it can in principle be used as a battery for monopoles. All microscopic mechanisms are explained and our proof of concept is validated by simulations of more than a million spins. Application to experiments are discussed for rare-earth pyrochlore oxides and artificial spin ice. Finally, we discuss in general terms how a domain wall in fragmented spin ice can also be seen as an emergent boundary separating two mirror "worlds" separated by time-reversal symmetry. Beyond spin ice, our work opens a promising direction of investigation for the dynamics of emergent quasi-particles crossing domain walls in chiral and nematic spin liquids, which also possess a broken symmetry.

</details>


### [157] [Microscopic magnetic phase evolution in the Weyl semimetal Mn$_3$Sn revealed by $μ^+$SR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01892)
*U. Miniotaite,O. K. Forslund,H. Luetkens,V. Rai,J. Persson,G. D. Morris,D. Zuhair,D. W. Tam,S. Nandi,Y. Sassa,M. Månsson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过μ^+SR和体磁化测量，系统研究了Weyl半金属Mn₃Sn（Mn₂.₉₉Sn）的复杂磁性，确定了三个磁相转变温度（T_N=418K, T_t≈275K, T_f=21K），并发现：低温区不存在静态自旋玻璃态而是存在铁磁分量与局域自旋涨落减慢；非公度螺旋反铁磁相中磁场分布呈现非对称性，表明磁结构受非谐性显著影响；在螺旋与三角形反铁磁相之间存在宽泛的共存区域；共度三角形反铁磁相中存在约20%的未解析超快去极化信号；325K以上观察到μ⁺占位随温度的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn₃Sn作为一种具有非平凡拓扑性质的反铁磁Weyl半金属，其丰富的磁相（共度三角形反铁磁、非公度螺旋反铁磁及可能的低温柔玻璃态）对其拓扑电子性质有重要影响。然而，这些复杂磁序之间的转变机理、共存关系以及低温态的真实本质尚未明确，特别是对低温自旋玻璃态的存在性存在争议。本文旨在通过高灵敏的μ^+SR和体磁化测量，厘清Mn₃Sn各磁相的本质、转变温度及磁结构演变细节。

Method: 采用零场/纵场μ^+SR（μ子自旋弛豫）技术结合体磁化率测量，对Mn₃Sn单晶/多晶样品进行宽温区（低温至418K以上）的系统表征。通过拟合ZF谱分析内部磁场分布与μ子退极化行为，利用初始非对称性缺失和谱权重重分布追踪各磁相演变，并刻画不同温度下μ子占位的变化。

Result: 确定了三个转变温度：T_N=418K（奈尔温度）、T_t≈275K（IT-AFM到IC螺旋相转变）、T_f=21K。低温下未观察到静态自旋玻璃态，而是铁磁分量增强伴随局域自旋涨落减慢。IC螺旋相中ZF谱拟合揭示非对称内磁场分布（非谐性效应）。150K以上出现IC螺旋与IT-AFM共存区。IT-AFM相中约20%的μ子经历超快未解析退极化。325K以上观测到μ子占位随温度的迁移。

Conclusion: Mn₃Sn的磁结构高度动态，各磁序之间并非简单离散相变，而是随温度连续演变并频繁共存。低温行为不支持静态自旋玻璃态模型，铁磁分量与自旋涨落减慢可能源于不同机制。该研究为理解拓扑反铁磁体中磁序与拓扑性质的耦合提供了重要实验证据。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive muon spin relaxation ($μ^+$SR) and bulk magnetization study of the antiferromagnetic (AFM) Weyl semimetal Mn$_3$Sn (composition Mn$_{2.99}$Sn). Mn$_3$Sn is reported to exhibit a commensurate inverse triangular (IT) AFM phase, an incommensurate (IC) helical AFM phase, and a proposed low-temperature spin-glass-like state. In our sample, we establish the respective transition temperatures for these phases to be $T_\mathrm{N} = 418$ K, $T_\mathrm{t} \approx 275$ K, and $T_\mathrm{f} = 21$ K. Investigating the low-temperature regime below $T_\mathrm{f}$, we find no evidence of a static spin-glass state. Instead, the sample exhibits an increasing ferromagnetic (FM) component accompanied by a localized slowing of spin fluctuations, indicating that these phenomena may be decoupled. In the IC helical AFM phase, fitting the zero-field (ZF) spectra reveals an asymmetric internal magnetic-field distribution, indicating that the magnetic structure is heavily modified by anharmonicity. Upon warming above 150 K, a continuous redistribution of muon spectral weight reveals a broad regime of coexisting local magnetic environments associated with the IC helical and IT-AFM phases. In the commensurate IT-AFM phase above $T_\mathrm{t}$, a persistent missing fraction in the initial asymmetry indicates that a subset of implanted muons, corresponding to roughly 20% of the sample-related asymmetry, undergoes unresolved ultrafast depolarization. Finally, we observe temperature-driven shifts in muon site populations above 325 K. Ultimately, our results show a highly dynamic magnetic landscape in Mn$_3$Sn, demonstrating how its complex magnetic orders often coexist and evolve continuously with temperature.

</details>


### [158] [Pressure-Driven Evolution of Electronic and Magnetic Correlations in Bilayer Nickelate La3Ni2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02261)
*Jian Zhou,Rui Song,Haiyan Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过DFT、cRPA和DMFT方法系统研究了高压下双层La3Ni2O7的电子与磁关联性质，揭示出压力驱动的轨道选择性演化：Ni dx2-y2态变得更具巡游性，而dz2轨道保持局域特征，导致Kondo屏蔽效应增强并压制超交换耦合，从而解释了Tc在高压区单调下降的圆顶形超导相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压双层La3Ni2O7中发现的高温超导电性引发了广泛研究兴趣，但其压力依赖的超导转变温度(Tc)的微观机制尚不清楚，需要深入理解压力如何调控电子关联与磁相互作用。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)、约束随机相位近似(cRPA)和动力学平均场理论(DMFT)相结合的第一性原理方法，系统研究了高压下La3Ni2O7的电子结构和磁关联性质。通过分析层间跳跃参数(t)、超交换能标(4t2/U)、相对关联强度(U/W)以及轨道选择性演化等关键物理量来刻画压力效应。

Result: 发现静水压力虽增强层间跳跃和裸超交换能标，但同时通过降低U/W将系统推向更巡游的电子态。关键发现是存在明显的轨道选择性演化：Ni dx2-y2态趋于巡游，而dz2轨道保持局域性。这种压力诱导的巡游性增强了两种轨道间的杂化，极大放大了巡游dx2-y2电子对局域dz2矩的Kondo-like屏蔽效应，导致有效磁交换耦合(Jeff)在高压区被显著抑制。

Conclusion: 高压下Tc的单调下降源于Kondo屏蔽对超交换相互作用的压制，该机制为La3Ni2O7中圆顶形超导相图提供了连贯的微观解释。

Abstract: The recent discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in pressurized bilayer La3Ni2O7 has sparked intense research interest, yet the microscopic mechanism governing its pressure-dependent superconducting transition temperature (Tc) remains elusive. In this work, we investigate the electronic and magnetic correlations of La3Ni2O7 under high pressure using a combination of density-functional theory (DFT), constrained random phase approximation (cRPA), and dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). We find that while hydrostatic pressure enhances the interlayer hopping and the bare superexchange energy scale (4t2/U), it simultaneously drives the system toward a more itinerant regime by reducing the relative correlation strength (U/W). Crucially, our results reveal a distinct orbital-selective evolution: the Ni dx2-y2 states become increasingly itinerant, whereas the Ni dz2 orbitals retain a more localized character. This pressure-induced itinerancy significantly enhances the hybridization between the two, leading to a dramatic amplification of the Kondo-like screening of the local dz2 moments by the itinerant dx2-y2 electrons. Consequently, the effective magnetic exchange coupling (Jeff), which serves as the pairing glue, is suppressed in the high-pressure regime. Our findings suggest that the monotonic decrease of Tc at high pressures is driven by the dominance of Kondo screening over superexchange interactions, providing a coherent microscopic explanation for the dome-shaped superconducting phase diagram in La3Ni2O7.

</details>


### [159] [Long and short time linear response of metals: a geometric approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02298)
*Nishchhal Verma,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了金属中时间相关量子几何张量的作用，指出费米面附近波函数的量子几何在低能描述中并非总是可忽略的。作者计算了金属中的时间相关量子几何张量并解释了其发散性，与Dirac和Weyl半金属的奇异几何张量进行对比，并提出D/𝒮₁比值作为格点尺度上束缚电荷与巡游电荷的探针，在kagome金属中以相同的费米面但不同的范霍夫填充验证了该方法的区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间相关量子几何张量能捕获束缚电子的偶极涨落，对理解绝缘体、超导体和平带至关重要，但通常被认为在由带内过程主导的金属低能描述中是次主导的。作者旨在挑战这一观点，揭示费米面附近波函数量子几何在金属中的重要作用。

Method: 作者计算了金属中的时间相关量子几何张量，解释其发散行为；将其与Dirac和Weyl半金属中奇异的几何张量进行对比；并定义Drude权重与总谱权重之比D/𝒮₁作为格点尺度的探针，在kagome金属中量化束缚电荷与巡游电荷的贡献。

Result: 研究发现金属中时间相关量子几何张量可以显著且发散，打破了其总为次主导的传统认知；D/𝒮₁在kagome金属中表现出两个范霍夫填充之间的不同响应，尽管它们的费米面完全相同，表明该比值能区分束缚与巡游电荷的微观差异。

Conclusion: 量子几何在金属中并非总是可忽略的，费米面附近的量子几何对金属的电子性质具有重要影响。D/𝒮₁比值是一个有效的格点尺度探针，可用于区分金属中束缚电荷与巡游电荷的贡献，即使费米面相同，不同的范霍夫填充也可能导致截然不同的量子几何响应。

Abstract: The time-dependent quantum geometric tensor, which captures dipole fluctuations of bound electrons, is essential for understanding the electronic properties of insulators, superconductors, and flat bands. It is often considered subleading for low-energy descriptions of metals that are dominated by intra-band processes. Here, we revisit this perspective and highlight scenarios where the quantum geometry of the wavefunctions close to the Fermi surface plays a significant role. We compute the time-dependent quantum geometric tensor for metals, explain its divergence, and contrast it against singular geometric tensors of Dirac and Weyl semi-metals. We identify the ratio of Drude to total spectral weight, $D/\mathcal{S}_1$, as a lattice-scale probe of bound versus itinerant charge, and quantify it in the kagome metal, where the two van Hove fillings respond differently despite identical Fermi surfaces.

</details>


### [160] [Optimized Tensor-Network Renormalization for Quantum Dynamics: Resolving the Spectral Function of $\mathrm{K_2Co(SeO_3)_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02473)
*Jiahang Hu,Runze Chi,B. Normand,Hai-Jun Liao,T. Xiang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的iPEPS方法在计算二维量子系统动力学谱时，重正化张量仅从基态环境构造，导致严重的数值不稳定性，限制了动力学性质研究的可靠性和精度。

Method: 作者识别了数值不稳定性的根源，并提出激发态定制的角转移矩阵重正化群（ET-CTMRG）方法：将激发张量纳入重正化程序，构造更准确的有效哈密顿量矩阵，从而获得可靠且良好收敛的激发谱。

Result: 对于Heisenberg反铁磁体，截断误差降低了数个数量级；对于三角晶格XXZ磁体K₂Co(SeO₃)₂的超固相这一特别复杂的情况，实现了与非弹性中子散射实验测量的出色定量一致。

Conclusion: ET-CTMRG为研究强关联量子系统的动力学性质提供了一个鲁棒的框架，有效解决了传统iPEPS方法的数值不稳定问题。

Abstract: Tensor-network methods have opened a powerful route for the study of dynamical spectral functions in two-dimensional quantum systems. However, existing approaches within the framework of infinite projected entangled-pair states construct the required renormalization tensors solely from the ground-state environment and can suffer from severe numerical instability. We identify the origin of this instability and introduce an excitation-tailored corner-transfer-matrix renormalization-group (ET-CTMRG) method to resolve it. By incorporating excitation tensors into the renormalization procedure, the method constructs a substantially more accurate effective Hamiltonian matrix and thereby yields reliable and well-converged excitation spectra. For Heisenberg antiferromagnets, it reduces truncation errors by orders of magnitude and for the particularly complex case of the supersolid phase in the triangular-lattice XXZ magnet $\mathrm{K_2Co(SeO_3)_2}$, it achieves excellent quantitative agreement with inelastic neutron-scattering measurements. ET-CTMRG therefore provides a robust framework for investigating the dynamical properties of strongly correlated quantum systems.

</details>
