  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，光照射绝缘体通常不会使其转变为金属，除非涉及强超快激发引发的瞬态结构相变。作者旨在寻找一种例外情况，即弱光也可以在关联平带体系中驱动绝缘体-金属转变，并探索其在超灵敏长波长辐射探测中的应用潜力。

Method: （1）选择魔角扭曲双层石墨烯，将其调至莫尔带半填充的关联绝缘态；（2）利用能量与平带宽度相当的弱长波长光子束照射样品；（3）通过光子选择性加热低热容的电子子系统，抑制关联能隙；（4）测量电阻变化、光响应率、带宽、噪声及磁场依赖等关键性能指标。

Result: 发现弱光束即可显著抑制关联能隙并产生巨大电阻变化，实现光子驱动的绝缘体-金属转变。该光响应是宽带低噪声的，电压响应率超过毫伏/纳瓦量级，且对数特斯拉的磁场具有鲁棒性，展现出锐利的绝缘体-金属电阻对比。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了关联平带系统作为超灵敏微弱长波长辐射探测平台的巨大潜力。其工作机制与超导热点子响应相对偶：辐射加热电子抑制多体有序，但在此反向过程中关联绝缘体熔化为金属。这一发现为长波长光子探测提供了全新的物理机制和材料平台。

Abstract: Light incident on an insulator is generally not expected to turn it into a metal without invoking intense ultrafast excitation that leads to transient structural transitions. Here we show that magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene tuned to half filling of the moiré band provides a notable exception to this expectation. We find that weak beam of long-wavelength photons, with energies comparable to the flat-band width, selectively heat the low-heat-capacity electronic subsystem, thereby suppressing the correlated gap. This produces a giant resistance change governed not by a persistent photocarrier population, but by the extreme sensitivity of a many-body correlated gap to weak electronic heating. The resulting photon-driven insulator-to-metal transition produces a broadband low-noise photoresponse with voltage responsivity exceeding millivolts per nW of absorbed power. The mechanism is dual to superconducting hot-electron response: radiation-heated electrons suppress a many-body order, but in reverse the correlated insulator melts into a metal, providing robustness to magnetic fields of several tesla and a sharp insulator-to-metal resistive contrast. Our results establish correlated flat-band systems as a platform for ultra-sensitive detection of faint long-wavelength radiation.

</details>


### [162] [{\it Ab initio} prediction of $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave superconductivity in infinite-layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00512)
*Guang-Yu Guo,Ren-Guo Guo,Yun-Chen Liao,Yang-hao Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过完全从头算（ab initio）的超导密度泛函理论，研究了最优掺杂的无限层镍酸盐Re₀.₈Sr₀.₂NiO₂（Re=La, Pr, Nd）的超导性质，发现其是双带超导体，具有符号反转的dₓ²₋ᵧ²(±)波能隙，并证明超导由自旋涨落相互作用驱动，而非电子-声子耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍酸盐作为新发现的高温超导家族，其超导配对机制和能隙对称性存在激烈争议，亟需从头算理论研究来澄清根本问题。

Method: 基于超导密度泛函理论，将电子-声子耦合（EPC）、屏蔽库仑排斥和自旋涨落（SF）相互作用置于同等地位进行从头算计算，分析费米面、能隙结构、临界温度及Lindhard响应函数。

Result: 发现Re₀.₈Sr₀.₂NiO₂为双带超导体，具有dₓ²₋ᵧ²(±)波能隙；关闭SF相互作用后T_c降至约0.01 K，证明超导由SF驱动；SF强度比EPC和库仑排斥强一个数量级；节点d波能隙源于布里渊区角部Lindhard响应的显著峰；计算结果与大多数实验一致。

Conclusion: 无限层镍酸盐Re₀.₈Sr₀.₂NiO₂的超导由自旋涨落介导，配对对称性为节点dₓ²₋ᵧ²(±)波；预测的扫描隧道谱等非常规超导特性可直接通过实验验证。

Abstract: Infinite-layer nickelates have recently emerged as a new family of potential unconventional high critical temperature ($T_c$) superconductors. However, fundamental questions such as their superconducting (SC) pairing mechanism and gap symmetry remain under intense debate. Here we present a fully {\it ab initio} theoretical study on the SC properties of optimally doped nickelates $Re$$_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ ($Re=$ La, Pr, Nd), based on the density functional theory for superconductors calculations with electron-phonon coupling (EPC), screened Coulomb repulsion and spin fluctuation (SF) interaction treated on an equal footing. We find that $Re_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ are two-band superconductors with sign reversal $d_{x^2-y^2}(\pm)$-wave gap functions on the different Fermi surface (FS) pockets. Interestingly, when the SF interaction is turned off, $T_c$ becomes negligibly small ($\sim$0.01 K), thus demonstrating that the superconductivity in $Re_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ is driven by SF interaction. Moreover, our {\it ab initio} calculations reveal that the SF interaction is an order of magnitude stronger than both EPC and Coulomb repulsion on the large quasi-two-dimensional FS pocket around the Brillouin zone (BZ) center, thus leading to the SF-mediated pairing mechanism, although the EPC dominates on the small three-dimensional electron FS pockets at the BZ corners. The emergence of nodal $d_{x^2-y^2}(\pm)$-wave gap structure is traced to the pronounced peaks in the Lindhard response function at the BZ corners. Our calculated FS, SC critical temperature, nodal gap structure and SC quasiparticle density of states are consistent with most available experiments. Furthermore, predicted unconventional SC properties such as scanning tunneling spectra of La$_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ and Pr$_{0.8}$Sr$_{0.2}$NiO$_2$ are ready for immediate experimental verifications.

</details>


### [163] [$d$-spacing distributions as a probe of nematoelastic response in iron-based superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01264)
*Wenting Zhang,Ruixian Liu,Tingjun Zhang,Weiliang Yao,Xüe Fu,Hanqing Xie,Ziye Mo,Ting Guo,Kuo-Feng Tseng,Thomas Keller,Jitae T. Park,Fankang Li,Masaaki Matsuda,Avishek Maity,Long Tian,Pengcheng Dai,Xingye Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用中子Larmor衍射技术，测量了多种铁基超导体中温度依赖的相对d间距分布，观察到四方相中随冷却增宽的面内分布宽度可用居里-外斯形式描述，拟合尺度T*与掺杂演化趋势同向列相图一致。通过平均场模型，将这种增宽解释为双线性向列-弹性耦合下重整化的正交晶格柔量，从而提供了体敏感且互补于电子和弹性测量的向列相关晶格增宽探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁基超导体中的电子向列性与正交应变双线性耦合，使得向列关联能出现在晶格响应中。此前的弹性电阻率等测量主要探测电子响应，而缺乏一种体敏感的、直接探测向列相关晶格增宽的实验手段。作者旨在利用中子Larmor衍射这一高分辨率技术，系统地研究多种铁基超导体的晶格间距分布随温度的演化，以揭示向列关联在晶格响应中的普适表现。

Method: 使用中子Larmor衍射测量温度依赖的相对d间距分布宽度（ε_FWHM），研究对象包括电子掺杂Ba(Fe₁₋ₓCoₓ)₂As₂、空穴掺杂Ba₀.₈₃K₀.₁₇Fe₂As₂、FeSe以及Fe₁.₀₇Te。在无外加单轴应力的样品中观测四方相的分布增宽，并用居里-外斯形式的唯象公式拟合。对Fe₁.₀₇Te施加单轴压力以研究应力诱导的各向异性增宽。最后构建了一个含双线性向列-弹性耦合及空间变化对称性破缺应力的平均场模型来解释实验现象。

Result: 在Ba(Fe₁₋ₓCoₓ)₂As₂中，无外加应力的晶体在四方相冷却时，面内分布宽度ε_FWHM增大且符合居里-外斯形式；拟合尺度T*随Co掺杂降低，其演化趋势与弹性电阻率推断的向列相图相似。类似增宽在Ba₀.₈₃K₀.₁₇Fe₂As₂和FeSe中也被观察到。相比之下，Fe₁.₀₇Te在无外加应力时没有延伸的居里-外斯型行为，而施加单轴压力后产生强烈各向异性增宽。平均场模型成功解释了居里-外斯型增宽源于重整化的正交晶格柔量。

Conclusion: 中子Larmor衍射提供了一种体敏感的、探测向列相关晶格增宽的方法，与电子和弹性测量互补。该技术适用于多种铁基超导体体系，为深入研究向列关联在晶格响应中的表现提供了新工具，也验证了平均场理论框架下向列-弹性耦合模型的合理性。

Abstract: Electronic nematicity in iron-based superconductors (FeSCs) couples bilinearly to orthorhombic strain, allowing nematic correlations to appear in the lattice response. Here we use neutron Larmor diffraction to measure the temperature-dependent distribution of relative $d$ spacings in electron-doped Ba(Fe$_{1-x}$Co$_x$)$_2$As$_2$, hole-doped Ba$_{0.83}$K$_{0.17}$Fe$_2$As$_2$, FeSe, and Fe$_{1.07}$Te. In Ba(Fe$_{1-x}$Co$_x$)$_2$As$_2$ crystals without intentionally applied uniaxial stress, the in-plane distribution width, $\varepsilon_{\rm FWHM}$, increases on cooling in the tetragonal phase and can be described phenomenologically by a Curie--Weiss-like form. The fitted scale $T^*$ decreases with Co doping and evolves similarly to the nematic phase diagram inferred from elastoresistance, although the two experiments probe different response functions. Related broadening in Ba$_{0.83}$K$_{0.17}$Fe$_2$As$_2$ and FeSe supports extending this interpretation beyond electron-doped BaFe$_2$As$_2$. By contrast, Fe$_{1.07}$Te shows no extended Curie--Weiss-like regime without applied stress, whereas uniaxial pressure produces a strongly anisotropic broadening that can contain contributions from both the field-biased lattice response and inhomogeneous loading. A mean-field model with bilinear nematoelastic coupling and spatially varying symmetry-breaking stress explains the Curie--Weiss-like broadening in terms of the renormalized orthorhombic compliance. Neutron Larmor diffraction therefore provides a bulk-sensitive probe of nematic-related lattice broadening that complements electronic and elastic measurements.

</details>


### [164] [Electron-like high-temperature superconductivity induced by compressive strain in La2PrNi2O7 thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01295)
*Zhiwei Wang,Zhengjie Wang,Huiyu Wang,Mingyi Zhu,Mengzu Shi,Zongyao Huang,Houpu Li,Shoucong Ning,Jing Tao,Tao Wu,Xianhui Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过在-2.14%至+0.91%的宽应变范围内系统研究双层镍氧化物超导体，在La2PrNi2O7薄膜上通过臭氧辅助原子层外延实现了Tc_onset=60 K、零电阻温度33 K的超导电性。研究发现外延应变与高压虽都能抑制自旋密度波从而诱导超导，但两者在c轴晶格响应和电子特性上存在本质差异：最优超导薄膜呈电子型（负霍尔系数），而高压块材呈空穴型（正霍尔系数），表明调控电子关联景观而非费米面拓扑才是实现超导的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解外延压应变是否等同于高压效应是双层镍氧化物超导体研究中的核心问题。此前普遍认为外延压应变模拟静水压效应，但这一等价性从未被严格验证。通过在最大压应变下实现超导并在宽应变范围内系统对比，可揭示两种调控机制的本质异同。

Method: 采用臭氧辅助原子层外延技术制备La2PrNi2O7薄膜，覆盖从压应变(-2.14%)到张应变(+0.91%)的连续应变谱。通过磁输运测量确认准二维超导本质，结合霍尔系数测量表征电子性质，并将相图与高压块材实验数据系统对比。

Result: 在NdAlO3衬底上实现-2.14%极端压应变下60 K的Tc_onset、33 K零电阻和20 K抗磁响应。相图对比显示：外延薄膜的c轴晶格参数响应与高压块材显著发散，但面内参数ap与块材一致。霍尔测量揭示本质差异：最优超导薄膜呈电子型（负霍尔系数），而高压块材和非超导张应变薄膜呈空穴型（正霍尔系数）。

Conclusion: 外延压应变与高压并非简单等价机制：两者虽都通过抑制自旋密度波诱导超导，但电子性质（霍尔系数）截然相反。超导性的真正驱动力是关联景观的调控而非特定的费米面拓扑。该工作为多轨道镍氧化物物理提供了宏观研究平台，拓展了高温超导研究的新维度。

Abstract: The realization of high-temperature superconductivity in bilayer nickelates under epitaxial compressive strain is widely interpreted as mimicking the effects of high hydrostatic pressure. To test the equivalence of these mechanisms, we investigated a comprehensive strain continuum ranging from compressive (-2.14%) to tensile (+0.91%). Crucially, via ozone-assisted atomic-layer epitaxy, we realized high-temperature superconductivity in as-grown La2PrNi2O7 films on NdAlO3 substrates, which induce the most extreme compressive strain in this material system. Under extreme compression (-2.14%), these films exhibit a Tc_onset of 60 K, zero resistance at 33 K, and a diamagnetic response at 20 K, with magnetotransport measurements confirming a quasi-two-dimensional superconducting nature. Comparing our phase diagram with reported data reveals distinct lattice responses: unlike in pressurized crystals, the superconducting window in epitaxial films diverges significantly in the out-of-plane parameter c (or c/ap ratio) but remains consistent with the bulk regarding the in-plane parameter ap. Crucially, while superconductivity in both systems emerges from the suppression of spin-density waves (SDW), Hall measurements reveal a fundamental electronic dichotomy: optimal superconducting films are intrinsically electron-like (exhibiting a negative Hall coefficient), in stark contrast to the hole-like nature (positive Hall coefficient) of high-pressure bulk crystals and non-superconducting tensile films. Ultimately, both tuning strategies effectively modulate the underlying correlation landscape - the true driver of superconductivity - transcending the constraints of specific Fermi surface topologies. This work establishes a macroscopic platform for probing the multi-orbital physics of nickelates, offering a new dimension for investigating high-temperature superconductivity.

</details>


### [165] [Synthesis and structural validation of close-to-stoichiometric NiTe$_2$ single crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02334)
*T. Tirasutt,L. H. Tjeng,A. C. Komarek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了通过化学气相输运法合成化学计量比NiTe₂单晶，并结合粉末XRD与单晶XRD进行精确结构表征，确认了接近化学计量比的组分，为后续物理性质研究提供了可靠的结构基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiTe₂的物理性质与其化学计量比和晶体结构密切相关，但此前缺少对化学计量比NiTe₂单晶结构的精确表征和可靠基准，阻碍了对其物理性质的深入研究。

Method: 采用化学气相输运（CVT）法合成NiTe₂单晶；通过粉末X射线衍射（XRD）与单晶XRD联合分析确定晶格参数和结构细节；利用高分辨率单晶XRD检测是否存在间隙镍原子以确认化学计量比。

Result: 成功合成了化学计量比NiTe₂单晶；高分辨率单晶XRD未发现间隙镍的迹象，证实样品接近化学计量比组成；建立了化学计量比NiTe₂晶体结构的可靠基准。

Conclusion: 该工作为化学计量比NiTe₂的结构提供了明确的参考基准，可作为未来研究其物理性质（如电学、磁学等）的重要基础。

Abstract: We report the synthesis of stoichiometric NiTe$_{2}$ single crystals via chemical vapor transport together with precise structural characterization. A combined analysis using powder X-ray diffraction (XRD) and single crystal XRD was performed to determine lattice parameters and structural details. High-resolution single crystal XRD reveals no evidence of interstitial nickel, confirming a close-to-stoichiometric composition. This work establishes a reliable benchmark for the crystal structure of stoichiometric NiTe$_{2}$, offering a reference for future studies on its physical properties.

</details>


### [166] [Gradient expansion approximation of the inhomogeneous electron-gas revisited: Higher-order corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02524)
*Mario Benites,Angel Rosado,Efstratios Manousakis*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在之前工作的基础上，将梯度展开近似（GEA）中交换关联系数$B_{xc}[n]$的计算扩展到次领头阶（相对领头阶缩放为$r_s \ln r_s$的项），并证明了该系数不受高阶图表达式的修正，从而为广义梯度近似（GGA）泛函开发提供了精确、确定性的约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前已重新计算了高密度缓变极限下$B_{xc}[n]$的领头阶贡献，解决了历史争议并纠正了GGA泛函中被错误施加的约束。本文旨在将计算推广到次领头阶（$r_s \ln r_s$项），以提供更完整的精确约束，指导未来GGA泛函的发展。该工作的意义在于证明了此系数不受高阶图修正，因此所得值是精确的、确定性的约束。

Method: 作者建立了一个系统性的框架来计算密度-密度响应函数在长波长极限（$q\to0$）下的$b_{xc}$系数（$\sim q^2$项）的积分表达式，这是计算$B_{xc}[n]$的前提。通过这一框架，将计算扩展到相对领头阶缩放为$r_s\ln r_s$的次领头阶项。关键的证明在于该系数不受更高阶图表达式的修正。

Result: 成功获得了$B_{xc}[n]$中次领头阶（$r_s\ln r_s$项）的系数的精确值，并证明了该值不受高阶图表达式的修正。所得结果与之前工作中建立的领头阶约束一起，构成了高密度缓变极限下精确、确定性的GGA泛函约束条件。

Conclusion: 本文所提供的次领头阶系数构成了对未来GGA泛函开发的精确约束。在高密度缓变极限下，任何有效的泛函都必须复现此前工作及本文所确立的精确约束。这为密度泛函理论的泛函开发提供了坚实的理论依据。

Abstract: In our recently published work (our Ref. 1) we revisited the gradient expansion approximation (GEA) of the interacting electron gas, and recalculated the leading-order contribution$-$with respect to the Wigner-Seitz radius $r_s$$-$to the coefficient $B_{xc}[n]$ of the square of the gradient of the electron density in the high-density and slowly varying limits. That work resolved historical controversies regarding these coefficients and demonstrated that serious misconceptions have led to incorrect constraints being imposed on popular functionals within the generalized gradient approximation (GGA). In the present paper, we extend this calculation to obtain the coefficient of the next-to-leading term, which scales as $r_s \ln(r_s)$ relative to the leading order. First, we establish a systematic framework to evaluate the integral expressions for the $b_{xc}$ coefficient of the leading term ($\sim q^2$) of the density-density response function in the long-wavelength limit ($q \to 0$)$-$a prerequisite for computing $B_{xc}[n]$. The significance of the calculation stems from the proof that the coefficient of this $r_s \ln(r_s)$ term receives no corrections from higher-order diagrammatic expressions. Consequently, our derived value serves as an exact, definitive constraint for future GGA functional development; in the high-density slowly-varying limit, any valid functional must reproduce the exact constraints established in both our previous work and the present paper.

</details>

### [167] [Memory with Onsager-Casimir symmetry: Rotating particle in a viscoelastic fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00344)
*Debankur Das,Niloyendu Roy,Niklas Windbacher,Clemens Bechinger,Matthias Krüger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了非马尔可夫流体中旋转布朗粒子的随机动力学，实验发现旋转增强了粒子的长时间扩散率，并产生了与旋转轴垂直平面内正交位移分量之间的时间反对称互关联。通过引入最小线性模型（示踪粒子耦合到慢浴自由度，旋转以平流耦合形式进入），消除浴变量得到具有非互易记忆核的广义Langevin方程。该记忆核随时间旋转形成对数螺旋，满足旋转矢量反转下的Onsager-Casimir对称性及对应的涨落-响应关系，由此得到将二时间互关联与粒子横向响应联系起来的几何构造，与普通Einstein关系不同，该关系涉及响应的反对称部分。实验与理论定性一致，确立了粘弹性流体中的旋转胶体作为时间非局域随机动力学中Onsager-Casimir对称性的最小实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在非马尔可夫（粘弹性）流体中，旋转自由度如何影响布朗粒子的输运性质，特别是旋转对扩散率增强的作用以及时间反对称互关联的起源。该研究旨在为时间非局域随机动力学中的Onsager-Casimir对称性提供一个最小实验实现，填补了非平衡随机热力学在该领域的实验空白。

Method: 实验上观测旋转布朗粒子在粘弹性流体中的两维运动轨迹，测量长时间扩散率与正交位移分量的互相关；理论上构建最小线性模型，将示踪粒子耦合到慢浴自由度，旋转通过平流耦合（advective coupling）进入，据此推导含非互易记忆核的广义Langevin方程，并从涨落-响应关系出发给出互关联与横向响应的几何联系。

Result: 旋转增强粒子长时间扩散率；正交位移分量间出现时间反对称互关联；记忆核在时间上演化为对数螺旋形态；该核满足Onsager-Casimir对称性和对应的涨落-响应关系；新得到的几何关系将二时间互关联连接到响应的反对称（横向）部分，而非普通的Einstein关系。实验与理论定性一致。

Conclusion: 旋转胶体在粘弹性流体中是时间非局域随机动力学中Onsager-Casimir对称性的最小实验实现。所建立的广义涨落-响应关系推广了普通Einstein关系，揭示了旋转诱导的非互易记忆效应对扩散输运的影响机制。

Abstract: We study the stochastic dynamics of a rotating Brownian particle in a non-Markovian fluid. Experimentally, we find that rotation enhances the long-time diffusivity of the particle and generates time-antisymmetric cross-correlations between orthogonal displacement components in the plane perpendicular to the rotation axis. To rationalize these observations, we introduce a minimal linear model in which a tracer is coupled to a slow bath degree of freedom and rotation enters through an advective coupling. Eliminating the bath variable yields a generalized Langevin equation with a non-reciprocal memory kernel. This kernel rotates in time, forming a logarithmic spiral, and it obeys Onsager-Casimir symmetry under reversal of the rotation vector, and the corresponding fluctuation-response relation. From the latter we obtain a geometric construction that links two-time cross-correlations to the transverse response of the particle in bulk. Unlike the ordinary Einstein relation, this relation involves the antisymmetric sector of the response. Our experiments and theory are in qualitative agreement, establishing rotating colloids in viscoelastic fluids as a minimal realization of Onsager-Casimir symmetry in time-nonlocal stochastic dynamics

</details>


### [168] [Noise-enhanced temporal boundary states in non-Hermitian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01179)
*Jielong Zhang,Bozheng Xue,Xianfeng Man,Baizhan Xia*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现时间噪声在二维周期驱动非厄米系统中反而能指数增强时间界面处的边界态响应强度，并通过平均超算符分析揭示噪声不破坏无噪声能带结构，但增加增长模式的增长率同时抑制衰减模式的衰减率，最终在机器人超材料网络中实验验证了噪声增强的时间边界态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为噪声是拓扑保护的威胁，会削弱甚至破坏拓扑态。本文旨在挑战这一认知，探索时间噪声是否能在非厄米周期驱动系统中成为构建性因素，从而增强而非破坏拓扑边界态，为拓扑器件的鲁棒性提供新思路。

Method: 将周期性重复时间噪声和完全随机时间噪声引入二维周期驱动非厄米系统；采用系综平均下的平均超算符分析来研究噪声对能带结构和模式增长/衰减率的影响；通过机器人超材料网络进行实验验证。

Result: 系综平均下，时间噪声导致时间界面响应强度发生指数级增强；平均超算符分析表明两种噪声类型下无噪声能带结构均被保留；噪声增加增长模式的增长率同时抑制衰减模式的衰减率；在机器人超材料网络中实验实现了噪声增强的时间边界态。

Conclusion: 时间噪声被确立为一种构建性要素，能够促进拓扑态的明确涌现，并为拓扑器件赋予卓越的鲁棒性，颠覆了噪声必然破坏拓扑保护的既有认知。

Abstract: Time-periodic modulation introduces a synthetic degree of freedom to manipulate topological phases. This synthetic dimension can trigger a phase transition that localizes a boundary state at the temporal interface. Noise is widely deemed a fundamental threat to topological protection, universally anticipated to weaken or even destroy topological states. Here, we introduce periodically repeated temporal noise and fully random temporal noise into a 2D periodically driven non-Hermitian system. Paradoxically, under ensemble averaging, such temporal noise drives an exponential enhancement of the response intensity at the temporal interface. An averaged superoperator analysis shows that the noiseless band structure is preserved under both types of noise. Yet the noise increases the growth rate of growing modes while suppressing the decay rate of decaying ones. Finally, we experimentally realize the noise-enhanced temporal boundary state in a robotic metamaterial network. This finding establishes temporal noise as a constructive ingredient, enabling the unambiguous emergence of topological states and conferring exceptional robustness upon topological devices.

</details>


### [169] [Algorithmic overlaps in the Baxter-Wu model: cluster dynamics under Novotny-Evertz updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01280)
*Ian Pilé,Lev Shchur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过Novotny-Evertz亚晶格冻结单簇更新算法，研究Baxter-Wu模型中马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛模拟中连续自旋构型的空间重叠。发现重叠均值作为算法可观测量，其临界行为类似序参量，且有限尺寸幂律衰减指数ψ=0.378(4)，小于Ising和Potts模型在标准Fortuin-Kasteleyn簇动力学下的值≈0.42，表明该指数反映的是算法动力学特性而非模型的静态性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在Baxter-Wu模型（三自旋相互作用模型）中，自旋构型空间重叠是否也能像Ising和Potts模型那样作为算法可观测量，反映临界热力学行为，并检验其有限尺寸标度行为是否普适。

Method: 使用Novotny-Evertz亚晶格冻结单簇更新算法对Baxter-Wu模型进行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛模拟，追踪连续自旋构型重叠的均值和方差，在临界区域跨越11个尺寸尺度进行分析，并提取有限尺寸幂律衰减指数。

Result: 单簇重叠均值在有序相呈现有限平台值，跨过T_c后趋于零，表现出类似序参量的行为；重叠在临界点不发散，保持有限值且随尺寸按幂律U_2(T_c)~L^(-ψ)衰减，指数ψ=0.378(4)，明显小于Ising和Potts模型下的值≈0.42。

Conclusion: Baxter-Wu模型中自旋构型重叠确实可作为算法可观测量并跟随相变热力学，但其临界指数并非普适常数，而是取决于所采用的簇更新动力学方案，揭示了算法可观测量反映的是动力学性质而非静态属性。

Abstract: We study the spatial overlap of successive spin configurations generated by Markov chain Monte Carlo simulations of the Baxter-Wu model. Using the Novotny-Evertz sublattice-freezing single-cluster update, we track the mean and variance of the algorithmic overlap across the critical region. We show that, even in this three-spin model, the overlap acts as an algorithmic observable that follows the thermodynamics of the transition: the single-cluster overlap mean behaves like an order parameter, dropping from a finite ordered-phase plateau toward zero across $T_c$. The overlap does not diverge at criticality, instead it remains finite and its finite-size value decays as a clean power law, $U_2(T_c)\sim L^{-ψ}$, over eleven sizes with an exponent $ψ{=}0.378(4)$ smaller than the value $\approx0.42$ found for the Ising and Potts models under standard Fortuin-Kasteleyn cluster dynamics, indicating that it reflects the Novotny-Evertz sublattice-freezing dynamics rather than any static property of the model.

</details>


### [170] [LieStoNet: Learning Lie Symmetries from Spatiotemporal Data for Stochastic Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01582)
*Shida Liu,Abhishek Gupta,Sumit Sinha,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: LieStoNet是一个端到端、无需模板的框架，用于直接从时空轨迹中发现随机微分方程（SDE）的Lie点对称性，无需预设对称群、模板或正则坐标。它通过神经网络学习漂移和扩散的代理，并通过强制SDE决定方程、Lie括号封闭性、Lie代数公理及非冗余独立基来学习可投影的生成元，同时可并行发现相关的Fokker-Planck对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性在现代机器学习和物理学中至关重要，但随机动力系统的连续对称性通常未知，且SDE的对称性发现此前几乎未被探索。现有方法需要预设对称群或模板，限制了其适用性。

Method: LieStoNet基于Gaeta和Quintero（1999）的SDE Lie对称性理论，首先从增量中学习漂移和扩散的神经代理，然后通过学习可投影生成元并强制执行SDE决定方程来实现对称性发现。训练中分别正则化Lie括号封闭性、Lie代数公理（双线性、反对称、Jacobi恒等式）以及非冗余独立基。代理还定义了对应的Fokker-Planck方程，支持并行发现其Lie点对称性。

Result: 在多个具有已知解析对称性的经典SDE上，LieStoNet能够恢复与真实对称代数一致的生成元，实现了对噪声动力学的可解释对称性发现。

Conclusion: LieStoNet提供了首个端到端、无需模板的SDE对称性发现框架，能够直接从数据中恢复真实的对称代数，为随机动力系统的可解释建模开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Symmetry is central to modern machine learning and physics: invariances and equivariances improve sample efficiency, robustness, and out-of-distribution generalization, while symmetry principles guide scientific modeling. Yet for stochastic dynamical systems the relevant continuous symmetries are rarely known, and symmetry discovery for SDEs has remained essentially unexplored. We introduce \textit{LieStoNet}, an end-to-end, \emph{template-free} framework for discovering Lie-point symmetries of SDEs directly from spatiotemporal trajectories, without prespecifying symmetry groups, templates, or canonical coordinates. Building on the seminal SDE Lie-symmetry theory of Gaeta and Quintero (1999), which formalizes Lie-point SDE symmetries and their relation to Fokker-Planck symmetries, LieStoNet learns neural surrogates for drift and diffusion from increments, then learns projectable generators by enforcing the SDE determining equations, separately regularizing for closure under Lie brackets, adherence to the Lie algebra axioms (bilinearity, antisymmetry, Jacobi), and a non-redundant independent basis. The surrogate also defines an associated Fokker-Planck equation, enabling optional discovery of its Lie-point symmetries in parallel. Across multiple canonical SDEs with known analytic symmetries, LieStoNet recovers generators consistent with the ground-truth symmetry algebra, providing interpretable symmetry discovery for noisy dynamics. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/sumit-sinha-seas/LieStoNet_Final.git}{this link}.

</details>


### [171] [Universal Scaling of the Magnetocaloric Effect in 2D Ising Monolayers and Bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01811)
*Basit Iqbal,Kingshuk Sarkar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过Monte Carlo模拟研究了二维Ising铁磁模型（方、蜂窝、三角晶格，单层和双层）的磁热效应，发现临界温度随配位数增加而升高，而磁熵变随配位数增加而降低；在所有晶格和磁场下归一化后的磁熵变和场指数n呈现普适标度行为，并系统分析了绝热温变、Grüneisen参数、相对制冷功率等量及其幂律行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁性材料在磁制冷领域具有重要应用前景，但配位数和层数如何影响磁热效应仍缺乏系统理解。理解二维晶格结构对磁热效应的调控规律，可为设计高效的磁制冷材料提供理论指导。

Method: 采用Monte Carlo模拟研究方、蜂窝、三角三种二维Ising铁磁晶格（每种含单层和双层构型）的磁热效应。用Binder累积量确定临界温度Tc；计算磁熵变-ΔSM、绝热温变ΔTad、磁场指数n、Grüneisen参数ΓM、相对制冷功率RCP和冷却容量；通过归一化和温度标度进行普适性分析；并进行磁滞回线和幂律场依赖分析。

Result: （1）Tc随配位数增大而升高（蜂窝r=3最小，三角r=6最大）；（2）磁熵变峰值随配位数增大而减小，蜂窝晶格最大；（3）归一化的-ΔSM和场指数n在所有六种结构及不同磁场下塌缩为普适主曲线；（4）ΔTad随配位数增大而增大，与-ΔSM趋势相反；ΓM与-ΔSM趋势一致；（5）RCP和冷却容量基本不随配位数变化，因峰展宽补偿了峰高下降；（6）各量随磁场服从幂律直到约2.6T；（7）低配位数晶格磁滞回线宽度随温度减小的速率更快。

Conclusion: 二维单层和双层磁晶格的磁热效应展现出独立于配位数和层数的普适标度行为，这种普适性为设计二维磁制冷材料提供了重要指导原则。

Abstract: We report a Monte Carlo study of the magnetocaloric effect (MCE) in two-dimensional ferromagnetic Ising models on square, honeycomb, and triangular lattices with monolayer and bilayer configurations. Using Binder cumulant analysis, we determine the critical temperature ($T_c$) of each structure and find that $T_c$ increases with coordination number, from the honeycomb ($r=3$) to the triangular ($r=6$) lattice. In contrast, the magnetic entropy change ($-ΔS_M$) decreases with coordination number, reaching its maximum for the honeycomb lattice. After normalization by their peak values and appropriate temperature scaling, both $-ΔS_M$ and the field exponent $n$ collapse onto universal master curves for different magnetic fields and across all six lattice structures at a fixed low field. This demonstrates universal MCE scaling independent of coordination number and layer count. Critical scaling analysis further supports the observed universality and power-law behavior. Unlike $-ΔS_M$, the adiabatic temperature change ($ΔT_{ad}$) increases with coordination number, whereas the magnetic Grüneisen parameter ($Γ_M$) follows the same trend as $-ΔS_M$. Although the peak value of $-ΔS_M$ decreases with coordination number, the relative cooling power and cooling capacity remain nearly unchanged due to compensating broadening of the $-ΔS_M$ curves. The field dependence of $-ΔS_M$, relative cooling power, and cooling capacity follows power laws up to $\sim2.6$ T (assuming $J\approx1$ meV). Hysteresis analysis shows that lattices with lower coordination numbers exhibit a faster reduction in loop width with increasing temperature. These results establish the universal scaling behavior of the magnetocaloric effect in two-dimensional monolayer and bilayer magnetic lattices and provide guidelines for designing magnetic refrigerants.

</details>


### [172] [Gap distributions between successive personal bests in cricket: Data and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01882)
*Priyanka D. Bhoyar,Prashant M. Gade*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过分析板球运动员职业生涯中的连续个人最佳成绩之间的间隔（inter-record gaps），发现其分布服从截断幂律，指数α在0.799–0.843之间。当打乱时间顺序后，指数显著增大至约0.939–0.979，表明职业生涯的时间演化对纪录出现有重要影响，简单的独立同分布随机过程无法完全解释个人最佳成绩的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典纪录理论对独立同分布序列预测了普适的间隔分布$P(g)\sim 1/g$，但体育职业生涯受到学习、老化、能力变化和外部影响等因素的制约，违反了独立同分布假设。作者希望探究在非平稳和路径依赖系统中纪录统计如何被改变，以更深入地理解个人最佳成绩的进展机制。

Method: 作者从ESPN Cricinfo收集了领先的Test、ODI和T20板球运动员的职业生涯数据，定义连续个人最佳得分之间的局数间隔（inter-record gaps），统计其经验分布并用截断幂律$P(g) \propto g^{-α} e^{-λg}$拟合。此外，通过bootstrap洗牌打乱局数的时间顺序（保留个体得分分布和职业生涯长度），比较洗牌前后的幂律指数差异。

Result: 经验分布很好地由截断幂律描述，指数在0.799 ≤ α ≤ 0.843范围内。打乱时间顺序后，指数显著增大至约0.939–0.979，表明大部分与经典纪录理论的偏差源于职业生涯的时间演化。

Conclusion: 个人最佳成绩的进展保留了职业生涯时间组织的信息，不能仅由简单随机纪录过程解释。该研究更广泛地揭示了纪录统计在非平稳和路径依赖系统中的改变方式。

Abstract: Successive personal best performances provide a natural measure of progression in an athlete's career. Classical record theory predicts a universal gap distribution, $P(g)\sim 1/g$, for independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) sequences. However, sporting careers are shaped by learning, aging, changes in ability, and external influences that violate these assumptions. We investigate the statistics of inter-record gaps, defined as the number of innings between successive personal best scores, in cricket. Using career records of leading Test, ODI, and T20 players obtained from ESPN Cricinfo. We find that the empirical distributions are well described by truncated power law $P(g) \propto g^{-α} e^{-λg}$ with exponents in the range (0.799 $\leq α\leq$ 0.843). Much of this deviation disappears when the temporal ordering of innings is destroyed, indicating that career evolution plays a key role in shaping record occurrence. Bootstrap-shuffled careers, which preserve individual score distributions and career lengths while removing temporal ordering, yield significantly larger exponents ($α\approx 0.939\text{--}0.979$). These findings show that the progression of personal best performances retains information about the temporal organization of a player's career and cannot be fully explained by simple stochastic record processes. More generally, they illustrate how record statistics are altered in nonstationary and path-dependent systems.

</details>


### [173] [Inhomogeneous Ising Model on 2D kagomé Lattice: Fermionic field approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02156)
*Shahane A. Khachatryan,Zhidong Zhang,Ara G. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了kagomé晶格上的二维非均匀Ising模型（2DIM），通过将其映射到特定的非对称八顶点模型并构造相应的R-矩阵，利用费米子表示计算了配分函数和主要热力学量。在热力学极限下，导出了确定相变临界曲面的精确方程，并计算了铁磁情形下的自由能、比热和自发磁化强度。当一或两个耦合常数消失时，模型分别退化为方格和一维Ising模型，解析结果均与已知精确解一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在kagomé晶格上研究二维非均匀Ising模型，旨在通过将其映射到非对称八顶点模型来获得精确解。这类非均匀模型的精确热力学性质和临界行为十分复杂，通过R-矩阵和费米子表示的方法希望推导出精确的临界曲面方程以及主要热力学量的闭合表达式。

Method: 将kagomé晶格上的二维非均匀Ising模型映射到特定的非对称八顶点模型，并构造对应的R-矩阵。利用费米子表示来评估配分函数，并在热力学极限下推导临界曲面的精确方程；同时计算自由能、比热和自发磁化强度。通过令某些耦合常数趋近于零，验证模型退化为方格和线性Ising模型时的精确结果。

Result: 获得了kagomé晶格二维非均匀Ising模型的精确临界曲面方程，以及铁磁情形下自由能、比热和自发磁化强度的显式表达式。在退化极限下，一或两个耦合常数消失时，模型分别还原为方格和一维Ising模型，所得临界耦合常数和自由能与已知精确结果完全吻合。

Conclusion: 通过将2DIM映射到非对称八顶点模型并利用费米子表示，成功获得了kagomé晶格上非均匀Ising模型的精确解。所得结果在退化极限下与方格和一维Ising模型的已知精确解一致，验证了方法的正确性与普适性。

Abstract: We investigate the two-dimensional inhomogeneous Ising model (2DIM) on the kagom'e lattice by mapping it onto a particular non-symmetric eight-vertex model and constructing the corresponding $R$-matrix. Using a fermionic representation, we evaluate the partition function and derive explicit expressions for the main thermodynamic quantities. In the thermodynamic limit, we obtain an exact equation for the critical surface determining the phase transition of the model. We also calculate the free energy, specific heat, and spontaneous magnetization in the ferromagnetic case. Furthermore, we show that when one or two coupling constants vanish, the model reduces, respectively, to the square-lattice and one-dimensional Ising models. In both limits, our results reproduce the corresponding exact critical couplings and free energies.

</details>


### [174] [Front Selection Is Not Determined by Renormalization-Group Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02211)
*Ko Okumura*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文通过统一的重整化群（RG）框架，研究了Fisher-KPP方程的行波解，发现旅行波解构成连续的RG不动点族，所有v≥2的前沿都是RG稳定的，但渐近选择的波前并非由RG稳定性决定，从而证明RG不动点稳定性与渐近态选择是两个不同的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fisher-KPP方程是波前传播和渐近态选择的经典范例，以往研究中RG稳定性常被视为与渐近态选择相关。本文旨在通过该方程检验RG稳定性与渐近态选择之间的关系，揭示二者可能是相互独立的概念。

Method: 采用统一的重整化群（RG）框架分析Fisher-KPP方程的行波解，将行波解映射为RG不动点，并系统分析各不动点的稳定性。

Result: 证明Fisher-KPP方程的行波解构成一个连续族的RG不动点，所有波速v≥2的前沿均为RG稳定的；然而，RG稳定性并不能决定实际被选取的渐近波前。

Conclusion: FKPP方程为RG不动点稳定性与渐近态选择可以互相独立提供了明确反例，说明在动力系统分析中不能将RG稳定性等同于物理上渐近态的选择判据。

Abstract: The Fisher--Kolmogorov--Petrovsky--Piskunov equation provides a paradigmatic example of front propagation and asymptotic-state selection. Using a unified renormalization-group (RG) framework, we show that its traveling-wave solutions form a continuous family of RG fixed points and that all fronts with $v \ge 2$ are RG-stable. Nevertheless, the asymptotically selected front is not determined by RG stability. The FKPP equation therefore provides an explicit example in which RG fixed-point stability and asymptotic-state selection are distinct concepts.

</details>


### [175] [Quantum resetting with memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02297)
*Gabriele de Mauro,Manas Kulkarni,Satya N. Majumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有均匀记忆的量子随机重置协议，每次重置将系统恢复到其历史中均匀随机选取的一个先前状态。该动力学是非幺正、非马尔可夫的，是经典偏好重定位模型的直接量子推广。研究发现哈密顿量仅通过玻尔频率进入动力学，据此区分了有隙（离散能谱）和无隙（连续能谱）两类量子系统：有隙系统中密度矩阵非对角元以代数方式衰减并伴随对数时间的周期振荡；无隙系统中位置分布以超慢的log(rt)/r尺度扩散，与初态和哈密顿量细节无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机重置协议已被广泛研究并在多个领域取得应用，但其量子对应版本——特别是具有历史记忆（非马尔可夫）的重置协议——尚未被系统探索。作者旨在将经典的偏好重定位模型推广到量子领域，建立一种具有均匀记忆的量子随机重置协议，并全面分析其对任意时间无关哈密顿量下量子系统演化的影响。

Method: 作者在能量本征基下工作，对任意时间无关哈密顿量推导了密度矩阵每个矩阵元的精确演化方程。关键发现是哈密顿量仅通过对应的玻尔频率（能量本征值之差）进入动力学，从而自然地引出有隙系统（离散能谱）与无隙系统（连续能谱）的区分。通过分别分析这两类系统中密度矩阵元的行为，作者得出了统一的演化规律。

Result: 对于有隙系统：对角元保持不变，非对角元在能量本征基下以代数方式衰减（衰减指数连续变化），且振幅在log t上具有周期性振荡；系统最终达到与重置率无关的稳态，但保留了对初态的强记忆。对于无隙系统：任意小的玻尔频率阻止了稳态的达到，位置分布以普遍的（超慢的）log(rt)/r尺度扩散，与初态和哈密顿量细节无关。作者以两能级系统、谐振子和自由量子粒子为例进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子随机重置与经典偏好重定位模型之间的桥梁，揭示了量子记忆重置协议中因能谱结构（有隙/无隙）而截然不同的动力学行为。这一理论框架为理解带记忆的量子重置过程提供了普适工具，并为后续在更复杂量子系统中研究非马尔可夫重置协议奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce a quantum stochastic resetting protocol with uniform memory, in which each resetting event returns the system to a state visited at a time chosen uniformly from its entire history. The resulting dynamics is nonunitary, non-Markovian and a direct quantum generalization of the classical preferential relocation model. Working in the energy eigenbasis, we derive the exact evolution of every density-matrix element for an arbitrary time-independent Hamiltonian and show that the Hamiltonian enters the dynamics only through the corresponding Bohr frequencies. This leads to a natural distinction between two classes of quantum systems: gapped and gapless. In \emph{gapped systems} (systems with a discrete energy spectrum), while the diagonal elements remain unchanged, the off-diagonal elements of the density matrix in the energy eigenbasis decay algebraically with a continuously varying exponent and with an amplitude that oscillates periodically in $\log t$. The system therefore approaches a stationary state that is independent of the resetting rate and retains a strong memory of the initial state. In \emph{gapless systems} (systems with a continuous energy spectrum), arbitrarily small Bohr frequencies prevent stationarity. Instead, the position distribution spreads on the universal (ultra-slow) scale $\log(rt)/r$, independently of the initial state and of the details of the Hamiltonian. We illustrate these results with a two-level system, a harmonic oscillator, and a free quantum particle, and contrast them with their classical counterparts.

</details>


### [176] [Channel selection at identically vanishing dissipation difference: isolating the frenetic sector of the overdamped path measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00041)
*Shlomo Segal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明仅依赖鞍点局部数据（过渡态理论/Kramers-Langer理论）无法确定反应通道选择。作者构造了一个双通道梯度势垒系统，其中两通道共享端点、势垒高度相等、鞍点Hessian矩阵完全相同，所有局部鞍点理论预测50:50，但Langevin模拟实际给出P(+)=0.4568。作者将选择机制归因于时对称的"frenesy"部分，提出了协变几何量(1/2)ln det(D⊥H⊥)作为通道选择的度量，并给出无参数的解析预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统过渡态理论和Kramers-Langer理论仅利用鞍点处的局部信息（如Hessian矩阵）来预测反应速率和通道分支比。本文质疑这种做法的充分性：是否存在这样一个系统，其鞍点局部数据完全相同，但通道选择却不同？作者在精确构造的意义上证明这确实不足够，并揭示frenesy（时对称路径权重部分）在通道选择中的关键作用。

Method: 作者首先利用Onsager-Machlup路径权重的标准分解（时间反对称部分由熵通量决定，时间对称部分即frenesy），从理论上证明熵差等于β乘以非保守力沿闭环的环量，且在梯度流中恒为零。然后构造一个双通道梯度景观，其通道端点共享、势垒高度恒等、鞍点Hessian为同一矩阵，从而关闭熵部分，使所有局部鞍点理论预测50:50。通过Langevin模拟测量实际分支比，并提出(1/2)ln det(D⊥H⊥)这一协变几何量来量化frenetic通道选择，该量具有坐标协变性且对刚性旁观模不变。

Result: Langevin模拟得到P(+)=0.4568±0.0012，与局部鞍点理论预测的50:50显著偏离。所提出的协变量(1/2)ln det(D⊥H⊥)在无拟合参数的情况下与测量分支比吻合（在0.5个标准差内）。此外给出无参数预测：螺线管力强度ε对ln[P(+)/P(-)]的贡献恰好为2βε/π。

Conclusion: 局部鞍点数据不足以确定反应通道选择。熵（耗散）部分在梯度流中恒为零，而frenetic部分携带了通道选择的关键信息。作者提出的协变几何量(1/2)ln det(D⊥H⊥)是通道选择的有效度量，且优于此前提出的divergence-of-drift可观测量（后者不具坐标不变性）。这构成了对局部鞍点理论充分性的精确受控反例。

Abstract: Transition-state and Kramers-Langer theory determine reaction channel selection from data at a single point: the saddle. We show this is insufficient in a precise, constructive sense. Using the standard split of the Onsager-Machlup path weight into a time-antisymmetric part fixed by the entropy flux and a time-symmetric part, the frenesy, we first settle when a dissipation-based criterion can distinguish two forward histories at all: the entropy difference between two histories sharing endpoints equals beta times the circulation of the non-conservative force around the loop they enclose, and vanishes identically for every gradient flow. We then construct a two-channel gradient landscape whose channels share both endpoints, whose barrier heights are equal identically, and whose saddle Hessians are the same matrix. The entropy sector is switched off by construction, all local saddle-point theories predict 50:50 exactly, and the branching ratio isolates the frenetic sector. Langevin simulation gives P(+) = 0.4568 +/- 0.0012. We identify a covariant geometric contribution to pathway selection within the frenetic sector, (1/2) ln det(D_perp H_perp), show that the divergence-of-drift observable proposed for this role is not coordinate-invariant whereas this one is, and find that it reproduces the measured branching to within 0.5 sigma with no fitted parameters. Stiff spectator modes cancel identically, since selection depends only on a ratio of determinants. We also give a parameter-free prediction: a solenoidal force of strength epsilon must contribute exactly 2*beta*epsilon/pi to ln[P(+)/P(-)]. These results are an exactly controlled counterexample to the sufficiency of local saddle-point data for channel selection.

</details>


### [177] [Phase transitions in quantum-circuit compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00189)
*Andrea De Girolamo,Davide Rattacaso,Simone Notarnicola,Ilaria Siloi,Simone Montangero*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文档（论文摘要）将量子电路编译过程类比为多体自旋系统的热力学过程，揭示了在串扰约束下编译问题中存在从无序相到反铁磁砖墙相的相变（符合伊辛普适类），并在更强串扰下出现 Z₃ 有序相。当移除幺正等价约束时这些有序相消失，表明电路间的幺正等价性是涌现临界性的根源，也是编译复杂性的来源。此外，电路的柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度会增强有序相的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路编译旨在在给定约束（如最小化硬件诱导误差和保持电路幺正等价性）下找到目标电路的优化实现。该工作旨在探索编译过程中的固有复杂性来源，特别是理解幺正等价约束在编译问题中所扮演的角色，以及编译优化为何具有计算难度。

Method: 作者将编译过程与多体自旋系统的热力学建立映射：以电路保真度（infidelity）作为能量函数，低温态对应编译后的电路。在并行门间存在串扰（crosstalk）的典型场景下，通过统计力学和相变理论分析编译问题的相结构，并研究柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度对有序相涌现的影响。

Result: 发现编译问题存在无序相与反铁磁砖墙相之间的相变，符合伊辛普适类；在更强串扰下出现 Z₃ 有序相，并推测更强的串扰会导致 Zₙ 有序相；移除幺正等价约束后有序相消失；电路的柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度增强有序相的出现。

Conclusion: 幺正等价约束是量子电路编译中涌现临界性和复杂性的根源；编译问题本质上是一种受等价约束的优化问题，其复杂性具有统计物理机理；柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度与有序相的出现呈正相关，为理解编译难度提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Quantum-circuit compilation aims at finding an optimized realization of a target circuit under given constraints, e.g., the minimization of hardware-induced errors and the unitary-equivalence of the circuit. We connect the compilation process with the thermodynamics of a many-body spin system: circuit infidelity plays the role of the energy function and low-temperature states correspond to compiled circuits. In the paradigmatic case where crosstalk between parallel gates is present, we find a phase transition between a disordered phase and an antiferromagnetic brick-wall phase, compatible with the Ising universality class. At larger crosstalk, we observe a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-ordered regime, suggesting that increasingly serial compiled circuits are associated with emergent $\mathbb{Z}_n$-ordered phases. When the unitary-equivalence constraint is removed, these phases disappear, showing that the equivalence between circuits underlies the emergent criticality and constitutes a source of complexity in quantum-circuit compilation and, more generally, in equivalence-constrained optimization. Finally, we observe that the Kolmogorov complexity of the circuit enhances the emergence of ordered phases.

</details>


### [178] [Dissipation in Periodically Driven Quantum Systems: Partial Secularization and Thermodynamic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00225)
*Luísa T. Tude,Carlos Ortega-Taberner,Roberta Zambrini,Gonzalo Manzano*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了周期性驱动开放量子系统中全世俗近似（full secular approximation）可能导致的稳态能量流非物理预测问题，并提出了一种粗粒化主方程公式来修复这些问题，确保完全正定动力学和一致的能量流。通过驱动二能级系统和三能级受激辐射器（maser）两个典型案例，与精确非马尔可夫模拟对比验证了该方法的有效性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动开放量子系统在量子热力学和量子控制中至关重要，通常使用基于全世俗近似的Floquet-Born-Markov主方程描述，但该近似的热力学含义常常被忽视。作者发现，这种强世俗近似可能导致稳态能量流的非物理预测，因此需要一种能够修正此问题同时保持完全正定动力学和一致能量流的替代方案。

Method: 作者提出了一种粗粒化（coarse-grained）主方程公式，利用粗粒化时间作为物理上有意义的参数，定义马尔可夫主方程能够描述周期性驱动系统演化的时间分辨率。该方法在保持完全正定（CP）动力学的同时，正则化全世俗近似带来的能量流问题。验证方法是将该方案与精确的非马尔可夫模拟进行对比，应用于两个范例系统：驱动二能级系统和耦合热、冷热库的三能级受激辐射器。

Result: 结果表明，全世俗近似确实可导致稳态能量流的非物理预测，而粗粒化公式能够有效解决此问题，产生完全正定动力学和一致的能量流。通过与精确非马尔可夫模拟的对比，证实了该方法的有效性和可靠性，表明粗粒化时间为物理上可解释的正则化参数。

Conclusion: 该工作为在周期性驱动-耗散系统中正确应用世俗近似提供了实用框架，并可作为评估GKSL形式主方程准确性的工具，对量子热力学和量子控制领域的理论建模具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Periodically driven open quantum systems are central to quantum thermodynamics and quantum control. These systems are typically described using Floquet-Born-Markov master equations, derived with the use of a full secular approximation, and whose thermodynamic implications are often overlooked. In this context, we show that such a strong secular approximation may lead to unphysical predictions for steady state energy currents. We then demonstrate that a coarse-grained formulation of the master equation can regularize these issues while yielding completely positive dynamics and consistent energy currents. The coarse-graining time has a clear physical interpretation, as it defines the temporal resolution at which a Markovian master equation can describe the evolution of the periodically driven system. We show the consistency and validity of our approach by comparing to an exact non-Markovian simulation in paradigmatic examples: a driven two-level system and a three-level maser coupled to hot and cold thermal reservoirs. Our work provides a practical framework for correctly applying the secular approximation in periodically driven-dissipative systems and for assessing the accuracy of master equations of the GKSL form.

</details>


### [179] [From Flight Logs to Atmospheric Science: Paragliders as Convection Sensors for Identifying Thermal Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00241)
*César Hernández-Aguayo,Matthieu Cristelli,Michael Benzaquen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于法国滑翔伞飞行日志（2017-2024年，110,730次飞行，147万个爬升段），构建了一个高分辨率的大气热对流观测数据集，并通过与ERA5再分析资料结合的正交化回归分析，揭示了边界层高度是热对流云顶高度和热力强度的主要预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气热对流驱动边界层动力学及热量、水汽和动量的垂直交换，但受限于现场观测覆盖不足，热力结构的基础问题仍未解决。传统观测手段（如探空、再分析）在空间和时间分辨率上存在局限，亟需新的高分辨率观测资源。

Method: 利用滑翔伞飞行日志中GPS变高仪记录的盘旋爬升段观测热上升气流，提取三种物理可辨观测量，并与ERA5（0.25度、逐小时）再分析资料耦合，通过包含118个可解释预测因子的正交化回归框架分析各观测量的主导大气预测因子。

Result: 云顶高度与温度-露点差的经验关系与理论抬升凝结高度（LCL）标度精确匹配；边界层高度是云顶高度和热力强度的首要独立预测因子（在所有地形和季节中均成立），而垂直速度变率由地表热通量和风变量控制。

Conclusion: 众包飞行日志数据在研究中尺度大气对流方面具有重要价值，为大气边界层热对流研究提供了前所未有的空间覆盖和时间分辨率。

Abstract: Atmospheric thermal convection drives boundary-layer dynamics and vertical exchange of heat, moisture, and momentum, yet fundamental questions about thermal structure remain open due to limited in situ observational coverage. We introduce a novel high-resolution observational dataset for atmospheric convection based on paragliding flight logs collected over metropolitan France during 2017-2024. Paragliders probe thermal updrafts by circling within them while carrying GPS variometers that record position and altitude; each climbing segment samples the vertical velocity field within a thermal column. Aggregated across 1.47 million climbing segments from 110,730 flights, this dataset provides unprecedented spatial coverage and temporal resolution. To demonstrate the value of this observational resource, we extract three physically distinct observables from climbing segments and characterize their dependence on terrain, season, time of day, cloud state, and soil moisture. Coupling the paragliding observations with global atmospheric reanalysis data (ERA5, 0.25 degrees hourly) through an orthogonalized regression framework against 118 physically interpretable predictors, we identify leading atmospheric predictors of these observables. The empirical relationship between ceiling height and temperature-dewpoint depression matches the theoretical lifting condensation level scaling with striking precision supporting our methodology. Boundary-layer height emerges as the leading independent predictor of both ceiling height and thermal strength across all terrains and seasons, while vertical-velocity variability is controlled by surface heat-flux and wind variables. Taken together, our results highlight the utility of crowdsourced flight-log data for investigating atmospheric convection.

</details>


### [180] [Duck hunting with quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00579)
*Artem Alexandrov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将经典奇异摄动理论中的慢-快系统与量子系统的半经典方法联系起来，证明经典系统特有的canard解（奇异解）是量子系统中瞬子（instanton）的阴影/映射。通过过阻尼约瑟夫森结这一具体物理例子，展示了canard解存在的参数域边界由瞬子作用量确定，并数值验证了理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典奇异摄动理论（慢-快系统）与量子半经典方法长期被视为两个独立的领域。本文的动机是建立这两者之间的桥梁，揭示经典的canard解与量子瞬子之间存在深层联系，从而为理解多尺度动力学系统提供统一的视角。

Method: 选取过阻尼约瑟夫森结这一物理相关系统作为研究对象，将经典慢-快系统中的canard解与量子系统的瞬子（instanton）进行对应分析。利用瞬子作用量确定canard解存在的参数域边界，并分别通过解析推导和数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 证明了canard解存在的参数域边界由瞬子作用量决定。对于约瑟夫森结，canard窗口恰好对应相邻Shapiro台阶之间指数级狭窄的间隙，且分析结果与数值模拟吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了经典canard解与量子瞬子之间的数学对应关系，表明经典奇异摄动与量子半经典两种方法在深层结构上是统一的，约瑟夫森结为这一联系提供了具体的物理例证。

Abstract: We bridge two sides of singular perturbation theory: the classical theory of slow-fast systems and the semi-classical approach to quantum mechanical systems. For a specific but physically important class of dynamical systems, we show that purely classical and exotic objects, so-called canard solutions, are shadows of instantons in the corresponding quantum system. We demonstrate that canard solutions exist in a domain of parameter space whose boundaries are determined by an instanton action. We illustrate our statements analytically for the relevant example, the overdamped Josephson junction, and confirm them numerically. For the Josephson junction, the canard window is the exponentially narrow gap between consecutive Shapiro steps.

</details>


### [181] [Work Statistics of Autonomous Quantum Energy Pumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00638)
*Yang Peng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为自主量子能量泵建立了基于终端的功统计理论，通过将供能与受能系统建模为显式量子终端，直接从哈密顿量变化定义能量交换。理想时钟终端可在泵希尔伯特空间获得精确表示，并恢复周期/准周期驱动系统的常规相位导数电流。框架解析了泵中积累能量与传输功，纳入任意初始泵-终端关联，并通过可解双终端量子比特展示噪声匹配现象，最后引入正功方差间隙量化超出泵描述之外的涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主量子能量泵的功统计缺乏终端分辨的统一描述，传统处理将泵当作驱动系统但未显式建模能量终端，导致无法清晰区分传输功与泵内累积能量，也无法处理初始关联及超越理想时钟极限的情形。

Method: 将能量供应与接收系统建模为显式量子终端，以各终端哈密顿量变化定义交换能量；理想时钟终端下功可观测量可在泵希尔伯特空间获得精确表示，恢复传统的相位导数电流公式；引入终端分辨的工作统计框架，支持任意初始泵-终端关联，推导周期泵Floquet本征态的有限周期功统计，并通过可解双终端量子比特验证。

Result: 在周期泵中推导了Floquet本征态的有限周期功统计，将其涨落关联到Floquet量子几何；长时终端电流趋于相互兼容。两终端量子比特展现噪声匹配，即通过公共模式终端涨落抵消使传输变尖锐。对超越理想时钟极限的物理终端，定义了正功方差间隙量化泵唯象描述的缺失涨落；相干腔基准表明随占据数增加趋于理想时钟，但平均电流一致并不意味着功统计准确。

Conclusion: 该终端分辨的理论为自主量子能量泵的功统计提供了严格框架，统一了理想时钟极限下的常规结果与更广泛的物理终端情形，揭示功统计不仅取决于平均电流，还与终端关联和量子几何密切相关。

Abstract: We develop a terminal-resolved theory of work statistics for autonomous quantum energy pumps. By modeling the systems that supply and receive energy as explicit quantum terminals, the energy exchanged with each terminal is defined directly from its Hamiltonian change. When the terminals are modeled by ideal clocks, these full-space observables admit exact representations on the pump Hilbert space and recover the conventional phase-derivative currents of periodically and quasiperiodically driven systems. The framework resolves transported work from energy accumulated in the pump. It also incorporates arbitrary initial pump--terminal correlations and identifies when correlations can enhance directional energy transfer under uncertain driving phases. For periodic pumps, we derive finite-cycle work statistics in Floquet eigenstates, relate their fluctuations to Floquet quantum geometry, and show that the long-time terminal currents become mutually compatible. An exactly solvable two-terminal qubit exhibits noise matching, in which transport becomes sharp through cancellation of common-mode terminal fluctuations even though the individual terminal energies remain noisy. Finally, for physical terminals beyond the ideal-clock limit, we introduce a positive work-variance gap that quantifies the fluctuations missed by a pump-only description. A coherent-cavity benchmark shows systematic convergence toward the ideal-clock regime with increasing occupation while demonstrating that agreement of the mean current alone does not guarantee accurate work statistics.

</details>


### [182] [Analysis of Conjectural Improvements to Minkowski's Lower Bound on the Sphere Packing Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00920)
*Carlo Vanoni,Alan Guo,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对Torquato-Stillinger猜想（高维欧氏空间球堆积密度的指数级改进下界），证明了一族超均匀对相关函数在可实现性猜想成立条件下，能在充分高维中给出形式为φ_max ≳ d^β 2^{-d}的多项式改进下界；同时从Cohn-Elkies对偶线性规划上界框架推导出相同的指数渐近速率，两种独立方法的一致性为高维异常致密无序球堆积的存在性提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Minkowski经典下界只给出了高维球堆积密度的2^{-d}量级下限，Torquato和Stillinger基于对相关函数优化框架猜想存在指数级改进。验证这一猜想对理解高维空间中的几何与无序结构具有重要意义，但直接证明困难，需要从不同视角寻求证据。

Method: 论文采用两种互补方法：(1)在Torquato-Stillinger可实现性猜想条件下，构造一族简单的超均匀对相关函数并分析其对应的堆积密度下界，得到d^β 2^{-d}的多项式改进；(2)从Cohn-Elkies对偶线性规划上界公式出发，分析径向目标检验函数的渐近行为，证明其无法排除具有Torquato-Stillinger密度量级的堆积。

Result: 在可实现性猜想成立的前提下，对于充分高的维数d%，一族超均匀对相关函数给出φ_max ≳ d^β 2^{-d}的下界，其中β>1可任意固定；当多项式指数β随维数增长时，该结果连续逼近指数的Torquato-Stillinger改进。同时，Cohn-Elkies框架的分析得到相同的指数渐近速率，两种方法结论一致。

Conclusion: 两种独立方法（超均匀对相关函数构造与Cohn-Elkies对偶线性规划）的一致性，为Torquato-Stillinger猜想提供了有力支持，暗示高维空间中可能确实存在异常致密的无序球堆积，强化了该猜想作为高维球堆积理论中核心开放问题的地位。

Abstract: Torquato and Stillinger conjectured an exponential improvement of Minkowski's classical lower bound on the maximal density of sphere packings in high-dimensional Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^d$ using a pair-correlation-function optimization framework. Conditional on their realizability conjecture, we show that a simple family of hyperuniform pair correlation functions yields polynomial improvements over Minkowski's lower bound of the form $φ_{\mathrm{max}} \gtrsim d^β2^{-d}$ for every fixed $β>1$ in sufficiently high dimensions. As the polynomial exponent is allowed to increase with dimension, this family continuously approaches the previously conjectured exponential improvement. We further derive the same exponential asymptotic rate independently from the Cohn--Elkies dual linear programming upper bound formulation, demonstrating that its radial objective test functions cannot asymptotically exclude packings with the Torquato--Stillinger density scalings. The agreement between these alternative approaches provides new evidence that exceptionally dense disordered sphere packings may exist in high dimensions and strengthens the case for the Torquato--Stillinger conjectural lower bound.

</details>


### [183] [Exactly solvable model for the diffusive price-dynamics paradox under long-range correlated market-order flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00988)
*Yuki Sato,Shunta Fujiwara,Kiyoshi Kanazawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过将平方根价格冲击定律引入Lillo-Mike-Farmer（LMF）模型，构建了一个可精确求解的非线性时间序列模型，解决了在可预测订单流下价格动态的矛盾——即长期来看价格呈布朗运动（扩散动力学），但订单流本身是长期相关且可预测的。该模型可映射到Lévy游走框架（统计物理中可精确求解的非马尔可夫随机过程），并证明了在平方根定律下，即使订单流可预测，价格动态在长期仍然是扩散的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场微观结构中存在一个悖论：一方面，公认价格动态在长时间尺度上近似为布朗运动；另一方面，市场订单流因长期相关性而明显可预测（由LMF模型数学化表述）。由于市场订单通常具有正向价格冲击，可预测的订单流似乎与布朗价格动态相矛盾。作者旨在解决这一"扩散价格动态悖论"。

Method: 将平方根价格冲击定律（square-root price-impact law）纳入LMF模型，构建非线性时间序列模型，将其推广为一般化的模型族。利用数学映射将时间序列模型转化为Lévy-walk框架——一种统计物理中可精确求解的非马尔可夫随机过程——从而进行精确的数学分析。

Result: 作者证明了在平方根定律下，即使存在可预测的市场订单流，价格动态在长时间尺度上仍然是扩散的（即布朗运动行为得以保留），从而解决了可预测订单流与扩散价格动态之间的矛盾。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了平方根价格冲击定律在理解有效市场假说的微观结构基础方面的关键实践重要性，为连接微观订单流结构、价格冲击非线性和宏观价格扩散行为提供了理论桥梁。

Abstract: We develop an exactly solvable nonlinear time-series model by incorporating the square-root price-impact law into the Lillo--Mike--Farmer (LMF) model to resolve the diffusive price-dynamics paradox under predictable market-order flow. In financial market microstructure, it is well established that the price dynamics are approximately described by Brownian motion at long times. However, it is also well-known that market-order flow is clearly predictable due to long-range correlations, as mathematically formulated by the LMF model. Since market orders have a positive price impact in general, predictable market-order flow seems to contradict Brownian price dynamics. In this work, we resolve this diffusive price-dynamics paradox by developing nonlinear time-series models that generalize the LMF model based on the square-root price-impact law. Our time-series models can be mathematically mapped onto the Lévy-walk framework---an exactly solvable class of non-Markovian stochastic processes developed in statistical physics. We prove that the price dynamics are diffusive at long times under the square-root law even under predictable market-order flow. Our work highlights the crucial practical importance of the square-root law in understanding the microstructural foundation of the Efficient Market Hypothesis.

</details>


### [184] [On Identification of Heat and Work in Quantum Many-Body Systems with Local Operations and Classical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00993)
*Hiroaki Matsueda*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在量子能量隐形传态（QET）协议下基于LOCC优化的明确定义热与功的方法。当LOCC优化时，可提取能量归属为功（daemonic ergotropy）；当LOCC未优化时，系统中残留的不可控能量归属为热。作者推导了两种广义Clausius不等式，并在Kitaev-like模型中验证了该框架及其有效温度的物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子热力学中，热与功分别归因于密度矩阵和哈密顿量的变化，但这种识别存在歧义。此外，涉及量子测量的量子热力学仍在发展中。本文旨在通过量子能量隐形传态（QET）与LOCC优化的视角，为热和功提供无歧义的定义，并深入理解热作为不可控能量的本质。

Method: 作者考虑一个量子多体系统，通过局部操作和经典通信（LOCC）按照QET协议提取能量。核心方法是对LOCC的优化条件进行深入分析：当LOCC被优化时，QET的可提取能量等于daemonic ergotropy，归属为功；当LOCC未优化时，系统中存在经由幺正操作无法全部挤出的不可控能量，在仔细处理多体相互作用后将此能量归属为热。热项包含远距离参与者之间通信产生的非局域关联，这些关联对子系统中的参与者无法直接观测。在此基础上推导了两个版本的广义Clausius不等式，并在一维Kitaev-like模型中进行验证，讨论有效温度的物理意义。

Result: 作者成功建立了基于LOCC优化条件的量子热力学框架，明确了功与热的无歧义定义；推导出两种广义Clausius不等式并讨论了不等式方向；通过一维Kitaev-like模型验证了该框架的合理性，并阐释了有效温度的物理意义。热作为不可控能量的视角得到了非局域关联这一微观基础的支持。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子测量参与下的量子热力学提供了一套自洽的理论框架。通过LOCC优化的视角，热和功的区分不再依赖于约定性的分配（密度矩阵vs哈密顿量），而是基于控制能力（是否完全优化、是否能否完全提取能量）的物理判据。热的概念与非局域关联和不可直接观测性相关联，与传统热力学的"不可控能量"视角保持一致。

Abstract: How we identify heat and work is a fundamental question in modern quantum thermodynamics. Usually, heat and work are attributed to changes in the density matrix and the Hamiltonian, respectively, during time-evolution processes in quantum systems. Recently, it has been recognized that this identification is ambiguous. Furthermore, quantum thermodynamics involving quantum measurement is still under development. Motivated by these on-going works, we consider a quantum many-body system from which we extract energy by local operations and classical communication (LOCC) according to the quantum energy teleportation (QET) protocol. The central idea to define heat and work unambiguously is based on a sharp insight into the optimization condition of LOCC. When LOCC is optimized, the extractable energy by QET becomes a daemonic ergotropy; thus, it can be attributed as work. On the other hand, when LOCC is not optimized, we have not squeezed out all the energy with the unitary operation. It means there is uncontrollable energy left in the system. The uncontrollable energy can be attributed as heat after careful treatment of many-body interactions. The heat term consists of nonlocal correlation due to communication between remote participants, and the correlation cannot be directly observed for the participant in the subsystem. Thus, this feature is consistent with the traditional perspective of heat as an uncontrollable energy. To deeply understand the nature of heat, we derive two types of generalized Clausius inequality in our effective quantum thermodynamics, and discuss the direction of the inequality. To justify our perspective, we examine a one-dimensional Kitaev-like model and discuss the physical meaning of effective temperature in our thermodynamics.

</details>


### [185] [Funnel-like protein energy landscapes emerge from functional evolution under thermal fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01143)
*Norifumi Maruyama,Macoto Kikuchi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过二维格子蛋白质模型，探讨了仅凭功能选择（不直接选择可折叠性）能否演化出具有漏斗状能量景观的可折叠蛋白质。研究发现只有在适当的环境温度范围内，高适应度序列才会自发产生漏斗状能量景观，且仅支持有限数量的天然构象；而在低温下则呈现玻璃态粗糙能量景观和更大的结构多样性。这表明可折叠蛋白质并非演化的直接目标，而是维持功能的热力学副产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质通过折叠成特定天然结构来执行生物学功能，其能量景观呈漏斗状。一个根本性问题是：这种可折叠性是否是演化的直接选择目标，还是仅通过功能选择就能自发出涌现？作者旨在探究功能选择（而非折叠性选择）是否足以产生具有漏斗状能量景观的可折叠蛋白质。

Method: 使用二维格子蛋白质模型（四种氨基酸类型），将蛋白质功能定义为环境温度T下指定活性位点局部结构实现的平衡概率，并以该概率作为演化适应度。采用多正则蒙特卡洛方法对覆盖整个适应度范围的氨基酸序列进行采样，并从平衡构象系综构建其能量景观和自由能景观。

Result: 研究发现只有在一个恰当的环境温度范围内，高适应度序列才会自发获得漏斗状能量景观，尽管适应度定义中从未包含折叠性。高适应度序列仅由有限数量的天然构象所支持。相反，在低温条件下，高适应度序列表现出粗糙的玻璃态能量景观和更大的结构多样性。

Conclusion: 可折叠蛋白质不需要是演化的直接选择目标，而可以作为一种热力学结果涌现：在热涨落条件下维持功能的需求，使得局部功能约束组织起整体折叠结构，从而产生漏斗状能量景观。这一发现揭示了功能与折叠之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Proteins perform biological functions by folding into specific native structures that are stabilized by funnel-like energy landscapes shaped through evolution. We investigate whether such foldable proteins can emerge solely from selection for function, without any direct selection for foldability. Using a two-dimensional lattice protein model with four amino-acid types, we define protein function as the equilibrium probability that a prescribed local structure forming an active site is realized at environmental temperature $T$, and use this probability as the evolutionary fitness. Amino-acid sequences spanning the entire fitness range are sampled by multicanonical Monte Carlo method and their energy and free-energy landscapes are constructed from the equilibrium conformational ensemble. We find that only within an appropriate range of environmental temperatures do high-fitness sequences spontaneously acquire funnel-like energy landscapes, despite foldability never being included in the fitness definition. High-fitness sequences are furthermore supported by only a limited number of native conformations. At low temperatures, in contrast, high-fitness sequences exhibit rugged, glass-like energy landscapes and much greater structural diversity. These results suggest that foldable proteins need not be direct targets of evolution, but can emerge as a thermodynamic consequence of maintaining function under thermal fluctuations, whereby local functional constraints organize the global fold and give rise to funnel-like energy landscapes.

</details>


### [186] [Validation and calibration of quantum hardware through the many-body quantum Mpemba effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01788)
*Francesco Campaioli,Marco Avesani,Oren Raz,Roderich Moessner,Gianluca Teza*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用工程化多体弛豫来验证和校准量子硬件的方案，并在两台独立的中性原子处理器上首次实现了开放系统中的多体量子Mpemba效应。通过初始态工程创建快慢两条弛豫路径，分别用于访问目标动力学和放大隐藏误差，从而直接评估处理器作为多体模拟器的实际工作窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件日益复杂，缺乏一种既能验证其动力学可靠性又能校准控制误差的通用工具。现有基准方法往往脱离处理器实际运行的物理过程，无法直接反映其作为多体模拟器的真实性能。

Method: 利用多体量子Mpemba效应：通过初始态工程构造快弛豫路径（快速进入难以到达的混合态物理区域，赶在硬件噪声破坏之前）和慢弛豫路径（放大制备与控制中的隐藏缺陷）。在计算基下进行直接测量以独立基准测试动力学可靠性，并通过互补响应区分不同类型的控制误差，驱动自适应、时间分辨的校准方案。

Result: 在两台独立开发的中性原子处理器上成功实现了开放系统中的多体量子Mpemba效应，揭示了每台处理器作为多体模拟器的实际工作窗口，并给出了可直接执行的硬件校正方案，提高了目标动力学的忠实再现能力。

Conclusion: 多体弛豫被确立为一种可迁移的量子处理器验证与校准工具，证明处理器可以通过其被设计用于复现的动力学本身来被评判和改进。

Abstract: We introduce a validation process that harnesses engineered many-body relaxation to control and calibrate quantum hardware. On two independently developed neutral-atom processors, we realize the many-body quantum Mpemba effect in an open system for the first time. Initial-state engineering creates fast and slow relaxation pathways: the fast pathway opens access to unreachable mixed-state physics before hardware noise obscures the target dynamics, whereas the slow pathway amplifies hidden imperfections in preparation and control. Computational-basis measurements directly and independently benchmark dynamical reliability, revealing each processor's actual operating window as a many-body simulator. The processors are thus judged by the very dynamics they are built to reproduce. Complementary responses disentangle control errors and drive an adaptive, time-resolved scheme that supplies hardware developers directly actionable corrections, enhancing faithful reproduction of the target dynamics. These results establish many-body relaxation as a transferable validation and calibration tool for programmable quantum processors.

</details>


### [187] [Optimality of Gaussian Entanglement of Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01909)
*Gerardo Adesso*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明任意两模高斯态的纠缠形成（EOF）与其高斯限制相等，解决了连续变量量子信息理论中长期悬而未决的问题。关键结果是纠缠与广义EPR可观测量之间的严格仿射关系，对任意纯两模态（包括非高斯态）均成立。该结果可扩展到双对称多模高斯态，并为任意非高斯态的EOF提供可测量的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子信息理论中，高斯态的纠缠形成是否等于其高斯限制是一个长期悬而未决的问题。这个问题对于理解高斯态纠缠资源的本质以及量子信息处理的可行性具有重要意义。

Method: 证明任意纯两模态（包括非高斯态）的纠缠与广义EPR可观测量之间存在严格的仿射关系，以此作为关键工具，进而证明两模高斯态的EOF与其高斯限制相等，并将结果推广到双对称多模高斯态。

Result: 证明了两模高斯态的纠缠形成等于其高斯限制；建立了纠缠与广义EPR可观测量间的仿射关系；结果扩展到双对称多模高斯态；为任意非高斯态的纠缠形成提供了可测量的下界。

Conclusion: 解决了连续变量量子信息理论中关于高斯态纠缠形成与高斯限制等价性的长期开放问题，为任意非高斯态的纠缠下界提供了可测量的定量工具。

Abstract: We prove that the entanglement of formation of every two-mode Gaussian state coincides with its Gaussian restriction, solving a longstanding open problem in continuous variable quantum information theory. The key result is a sharp affine relation between entanglement and a generalized Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen observable, valid for arbitrary pure two-mode states, including non-Gaussian ones. Our result extends to bisymmetric multimode Gaussian states, and also provides a measurable lower bound on the entanglement of formation of arbitrary non-Gaussian states.

</details>


### [188] [Microscopic dynamics of consensus formation in multi-agent LLM Naming Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02178)
*Cristiano De Nobili,Vijayasri Iyer,Alessandro Codello,Raffaella Burioni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过最小化LLM命名博弈实验，揭示了解码温度如何通过控制LLM智能体的内部随机性来塑造去中心化群体共识形成的宏观有序化行为，并从统计物理视角给出了定量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化的LLM智能体群体能够自发形成共享约定（共识），但其内部随机性（如解码温度）如何从微观层面影响宏观有序化（共识形成）的机制尚未被探索。作者希望填补这一空白，从统计物理的角度理解LLM群体的集体行为。

Method: 设计了一个最小LLM命名博弈模型，其中听者的决策是单token的LLM调用（受解码温度T控制），替代了确定性命名博弈中的库存检查。将每次交互分解为库存内与库存外两个通道，分别具有条件速率π(T)和φ(T)，两者的平衡控制有序化-无序化漂移。基于两速率动力学建立平均场理论，推导出解析的有序化条件，将随机命名博弈的共识阈值推广为(π,φ)平面上的临界线。

Result: 在三种开放权重架构上，共识总能达成，但表现出三种不同的听者行为模式：宽容型（重绘噪声主导）、近确定型和保守型（漏检坍塌主导）。有效有限尺寸指数β(T)（t_conv~N^β）随温度变化，温度敏感度α（t_c~e^{αT}）在不同架构间从约0.67变化到约0。

Conclusion: 解码温度作为架构相关的控制参数，对去中心化LLM群体的共识形成起着关键调节作用，统计物理工具能够对其效应进行定量表征。这一发现为理解和控制多智能体LLM系统的集体行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Decentralized populations of Large Language Model (LLM) agents can spontaneously reach consensus on shared conventions, yet the microscopic mechanisms by which their internal stochasticity shapes macroscopic ordering remain unexplored. We study a minimal LLM Naming Game in which the listener's decision is a single-token LLM call at decoding temperature $T$, replacing the inventory check of the deterministic Naming Game. Each interaction decomposes into an in-inventory and an out-inventory channel with conditional rates $π(T)\!\equiv\!P(\text{YES}\mid w\in P_j)$ and $φ(T)\!\equiv\!P(\text{YES}\mid w\notin P_j)$, whose balance controls an ordering-disordering drift. A mean-field theory of the two-rate dynamics yields an analytical ordering condition that generalizes the consensus threshold of the stochastic Naming Game to a critical line in the $(π,φ)$ plane. Across three open-weight architectures, consensus is always reached, but through three distinct listener regimes: permissive (repaint-noise dominated), near-deterministic, and conservative (missed-collapse dominated). The effective finite-size exponent $β(T)$ in $t_{\rm conv}\!\sim\!N^β$ shifts with temperature, and the temperature-sensitivity $α$ in $t_c\!\sim\!e^{αT}$ ranges from ${\approx}\,0.67$ to ${\approx}\,0$ across architectures. Decoding temperature thus emerges as an architecture-dependent control parameter for decentralized LLM populations, quantitatively characterized by the statistical-physics toolkit.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Mitigating ray effects in rarefied flow simulations using an ensemble-of-subproblems strategy with stochastic discrete velocities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00514)
*Shuyang Zhang,Weidong Li,Ming Fang,Zhaoli Guo*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子问题集成与随机离散速度的策略（SDV-DUGKS），通过执行多个独立子问题并对结果求平均，在无需增加单个子问题内存的前提下近似高精细速度空间分辨率，从而缓解稀薄流模拟中的射线效应。与原始DUGKS相比，在无碰撞极限下，一维问题内存减少约2/3，二维问题内存减少1~2个数量级，同时保持良好的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀薄流模拟中存在射线效应问题，传统确定性方法需要高精细的速度空间离散来抑制该效应，但这会显著增加内存开销。作者希望找到一种在缓解射线效应的同时不大幅增加内存负担的策略。

Method: 将"子问题集成与随机离散速度"策略扩展到确定性方法中：执行多个独立子问题，每个子问题仅使用一小部分随机采样的速度点，然后对各个子问题的解进行平均得到最终结果。将该策略集成到DUGKS框架中，形成SDV-DUGKS方法。

Result: 在Sod激波管问题、一维Riemann问题、二维顶盖驱动空腔流动和二维Riemann问题等算例中，与原始DUGKS比较：在无碰撞极限下，(1) 一维可压缩流动内存降低约2/3且精度良好；(2) 二维可压缩流动内存降低1~2个数量级且精度良好。

Conclusion: SDV-DUGKS是一种可靠且有效的工具，可用于缓解稀薄流模拟中的射线效应。

Abstract: In this work, a ensemble-of-subproblems strategy with stochastic discrete velocities is extended to deterministic methods for mitigating ray effects in rarefied flow simulations. The strategy involves performing multiple independent subproblems, each using a small set of randomly sampled velocity points, and then averaging their solutions to obtain the final result. The core idea is to ensure that the distribution function at any velocity can contribute to the final result, approximating highly refined velocity-space resolution without increasing the memory requirement in any single subproblem. We incorporate this strategy within the DUGKS framework, and the resulting method is denoted as SDV-DUGKS. To evaluate the performance of the proposed method, we compare SDV-DUGKS with the original DUGKS on several test cases: (a) the Sod shock tube problem, (b) the one-dimensional Riemann problem, (c) the two-dimensional lid-driven cavity flow, and (d) the two-dimensional Riemann problem. The results show that, in the collisionless limit $\mathrm{Kn} \to \infty$: (1) for one-dimensional compressible flows, SDV-DUGKS reduces memory usage by approximately 2/3 compared with that of the original DUGKS while achieving good agreement; (2) for two-dimensional compressible flows, SDV-DUGKS requires one to two orders of magnitude less memory than the original DUGKS while achieving good agreement. Based on these results, it can be concluded that the proposed method serves as a reliable and effective tool for mitigating ray effects in rarefied flow simulations.

</details>


### [190] [A memory-efficient deterministic method for multiscale gas flows using an ensemble-of-subproblems strategy with stochastic discrete velocities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00521)
*Shuyang Zhang,Weidong Li,Ming Fang,Zhaoli Guo*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机离散速度的集合-子问题策略（SDV-DUGKS），通过将大量确定性离散速度替换为多个小型随机速度集合，将计算问题分解为一系列独立可解的子问题，并通过蒙特卡洛积分计算宏观矩，最终算术平均得到流场。该方法在二维算例中相比确定性方法节省了超过80%的内存，同时保持相当的精度，有效缓解了多尺度气体流动模拟中的维度灾难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性多尺度气体流动模拟长期受困于维度灾难：随着速度空间维度和马赫数增加，离散速度数量急剧增长，导致内存和计算资源被迅速耗尽，阻碍了确定性多尺度数值方法在工程问题中的应用。

Method: 提出基于集合-子问题策略的存储高效确定性方法。具体做法是：（1）用多个小型随机速度集替代传统的大型确定性速度集；（2）每个随机集定义一个子问题；（3）采用确定性多尺度数值格式（离散统一气体动理学格式，DUGKS）求解每个子问题，并通过蒙特卡洛积分计算宏观矩；（4）对所有子问题的结果进行算术平均得到最终流场。该方法命名为SDV-DUGKS。

Result: 通过一维激波结构、二维方腔流动以及方柱绕流超声速流动三个数值测试验证了所提方法。一维激波结构结果证实了方法的可行性；二维算例表明，与确定性对应方法相比，所提方法节省超过80%的内存使用，同时保持相当的精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的SDV-DUGKS方法显著降低了多尺度流动模拟的内存需求，展现出缓解当前阻碍确定性多尺度数值方案应用于工程问题的维度灾难的强大潜力。

Abstract: Deterministic multiscale gas flow simulations have long suffered from the curse of dimensionality: the number of discrete velocities increases dramatically with the velocity space dimension and the Mach number, exhausting available memory and computational resources. To address this issue, this paper proposes a memory-efficient deterministic method based on an ensemble-of-subproblems strategy using stochastic discrete velocities. This strategy transforms the originally computationally expensive problem into a series of independently and efficiently solvable subproblems. To be concrete, the proposed method replaces the conventional large deterministic velocity set with multiple small random velocity sets. Each random set defines a subproblem, which is solved by a deterministic multiscale numerical scheme that computes macroscopic moments via Monte Carlo integration. The final flow field is obtained by arithmetic averaging over all sub-problems. In this work, we employ the discrete unified gas kinetic scheme (DUGKS) for spatial discretization and term the resulting method SDV-DUGKS. To validate the proposed method, several numerical test cases are conducted, including (a) the one-dimensional shock structure, (b) the two-dimensional cavity flow, and (c) supersonic flow around a square cylinder. The results of the one-dimensional shock structure confirm the feasibility of the proposed method. The two-dimensional cases demonstrate that, compared to its deterministic counterpart, the proposed method saves more than 80% of memory usage while maintaining comparable accuracy. These results indicate that the proposed method markedly reduces memory demand for multiscale flow simulations and exhibits strong potential to alleviate the curse of dimensionality that currently hinders deterministic multiscale numerical schemes from being applied to engineering problems.

</details>


### [191] [Consistent and bound-preserving finite-volume WENO scheme for compressible two-/$N$-phase flows with Phase-Field mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00746)
*Ziyang Huang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种一致且有界保持的有限体积WENO格式，用于基于相场机制的压缩多相流模拟。通过基于模板间相对光滑度计算WENO权重，以及对质量和体积分数进行耦合重构，该格式满足一致性、守恒性、平衡态保持和有界性要求，并已在多种基准问题和冲击诱导空泡塌陷等问题中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩多相流中传统WENO格式往往难以同时满足一致性、守恒性和边界保持的要求，容易在数值上产生虚假相、局部空洞或过度填充等问题，尤其是在N≥1种不混溶相存在的情况下，需要开发一种同时满足这些性质的可靠高阶格式。

Method: 提出基于模板间相对光滑度计算WENO权重的新方法，并对质量与体积分数进行耦合重构；在格式开发中考虑还原一致性和体积分数求和为1的约束，避免产生虚假相、局部空洞或过度填充；将该格式应用于一致且守恒的相场方法，并启用自适应网格细化。

Result: 通过多种压缩两相和N相流基准算例验证了所提WENO格式及其带一致性限制器变体的各项性质；展示了该格式在冲击诱导空泡塌陷和冲击-容器-气泡相互作用问题中的能力，并讨论了对高阶格式进行边界保持的必要性及不同压缩多相流模型的比较。

Conclusion: 所提出的WENO格式能够同时满足一致性、守恒性、平衡态保持和有界性要求，适用于压缩多相流数值模拟，并在复杂冲击流相互作用问题中展现出良好性能。

Abstract: In the present study, we propose a consistent and bound-preserving finite-volume WENO scheme that satisfies the requirements of consistency, conservation, equilibrium, and bound preservation for compressible multiphase flows with the Phase-Field mechanism. The proposed WENO scheme is developed based on a new calculation of WENO weights determined by relative smoothness between stencils and on a coupled reconstruction of the masses and volume fractions. Consistency of reduction and volume fraction summation to unity are considered during the development so that fictitious phases, local voids, or overfilling are not produced numerically when there are $N$ ($N \geqslant 1$) different immiscible phases. The proposed WENO scheme is applied to the consistent and conservative Phase-Field method with adaptive mesh refinement enabled. Various benchmark compressible two- and $N$-phase flows are performed to verify the properties of the proposed WENO scheme as well as its variant with the consistent limiter. We finally demonstrate the capability of the proposed WENO scheme in shock-induced cavity collapse and shock-vessel-bubble interaction problems, with discussion of the necessity of bound preservation for high-order schemes and comparison of different compressible multiphase flow models.

</details>


### [192] [Multiscale passive scalar turbulence in a compressed subspace via tensor trains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00194)
*Stefano Pisoni,Egor Tiunov,Chiara Calascibetta*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合张量列（Tensor Train, TT）方法，用于压缩表示高间歇性被动标量的多尺度湍流统计，在结构函数上与Galerkin、小波和标准TT分解相当，同时更优地再现了间歇性非高斯涨落，为在压缩张量形式中直接演化被动标量线性动力学开辟了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以压缩形式捕捉湍流的多尺度统计特性是降阶建模的核心挑战，尤其是对于高间歇性被动标量，传统的Galerkin、小波和标准TT分解在表征间歇性非高斯涨落方面存在不足。

Method: 引入一种混合张量列（hybrid Tensor Train, TT）方法，将TT分解与其他手段结合，以在保持结构函数精度的同时改善对间歇性非高斯涨落的表示。

Result: 混合TT方法在结构函数上与Galerkin、小波和标准TT分解相当，但在表示间歇性非高斯涨落方面更为优越，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该结果指向在压缩张量形式中直接演化被动标量线性动力学的可行路径，并可能应用于流体输运的量子算法。

Abstract: Capturing the multiscale statistics of turbulence in compressed form remains a central challenge for reduced-order modeling. We introduce a hybrid Tensor Train (TT) approach for a highly intermittent passive scalar. The hybrid TT matches Galerkin, wavelet, and standard TT decompositions for the structure functions while improving the representation of intermittent, non-Gaussian fluctuations. These results open a route toward evolving the linear dynamics of passive scalars directly in compressed tensor form, with potential applications to quantum algorithms for fluid transport.

</details>


### [193] [A Physics-Chemistry-Informed Neural Network (PCINN) for Real-Time Spatial-ALD Coverage Prediction and Reliable Kinetics Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00212)
*Ning Hu,Chang Liu,Yunlei Jiang,Yuan Dong*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理-化学信息神经网络（PCINN），作为空间原子层沉积（SALD）的混合代理模型，兼具CFD精度的实时速度（约7ms/查询，比CFD快5×10^4倍，R²_log=0.998）。该架构通过单标量瓶颈层将可训练的表面反应动力学硬编码嵌入，仅用小网络学习操作条件到近壁浓度的映射，保持可解释性和可逆性。论文还给出了完整的可辨识性分析（Fisher信息、剖面似然），发现E_ads和k_des可稳健辨识，而k_ads在单一温度下不可单独辨识，并推导了跨温度下nu与E_ads之间斜率为0.065 eV/decade的退化谷，作为未建模位点异质性的可靠性诊断标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SALD是工业化的常压高通量ALD路线，但其设计和控制受限于表面覆盖率的预测成本：高保真CFD过于缓慢无法用于操作窗口扫描，而解析模型又遗漏了气帘等传输调制效应。需要一个兼顾精度与速度、且可解释的代理模型。

Method: 提出PCINN混合代理架构：小型神经网络仅学习操作条件到近壁浓度的闭式映射，已知的表面动力学作为硬编码但可训练的化学层沿基底轨迹积分。这种单标量瓶颈结构在稀疏数据下保持准确、可解释且可逆。随后通过Fisher信息和剖面似然进行完整可辨识性分析，并推导温度关联参数间的解析退化关系。

Result: PCINN在约7ms内返回覆盖率查询，比CFD快约5×10^4倍，测试R²_log=0.998，留一法R²_raw=0.974，仅需30个训练案例即可覆盖四个数量级的覆盖率范围。可辨识性分析显示E_ads和k_des可稳健辨识，k_ads在单一温度下不可单独辨识；跨四温度时nu与E_ads沿斜率为0.065 eV/decade（=k_B T_eff ln(10)）的退化谷绑定。七化学失配矩阵验证该斜率对任何单一阿伦尼乌斯失配不变，仅当第二热活化过程出现时才偏移。

Conclusion: PCINN为SALD提供了一个结合CFD精度与实时速度的可解释、可逆混合代理，并通过可辨识性分析揭示了参数辨识的边界与约束。退化谷斜率可作为检测未建模位点异质性的诊断工具。数据来源为已知真值的模拟并通过相同动力学形式反演，因此该研究验证的是管道的自洽性和可辨识性边界，而非真实工艺参数。

Abstract: Spatial atomic layer deposition (SALD) is a leading atmospheric-pressure, high-throughput route to industrial ALD, but design and control are limited by the cost of predicting surface coverage: high-fidelity CFD is far too slow for operating-window scans, while analytic models miss transport modulation such as the gas curtain. We present a physics-chemistry-informed neural network (PCINN), a hybrid surrogate with CFD-level accuracy at real-time speed: a query returns coverage in about 7 ms, roughly 5x10^4 times faster than a CFD solve, reaching a test R^2_log = 0.998 (leave-one-out R^2_raw = 0.974) from only 30 training cases spanning four orders of magnitude in coverage.
  The architecture is not a black box: a small network learns only the operating-condition to near-wall concentration closure, while the known surface kinetics is a hard-coded, trainable chemistry layer integrated along the substrate trajectory. This single-scalar bottleneck keeps it accurate under sparse data, interpretable and invertible.
  We add a full identifiability analysis (Fisher information, profile likelihood). The adsorption energy E_ads and desorption rate k_des are robustly identifiable; k_ads is not separately identifiable at a single temperature (only k_ads*c_wall is). Across four temperatures the prefactor nu and E_ads bind along a weakly identifiable degeneracy valley of slope 0.065 eV/decade, derived analytically as k_B T_eff ln(10) and turned into a reliability diagnostic: a seven-chemistry mismatch matrix shows it is invariant under any single-Arrhenius mismatch and shifts only when a second thermally activated process appears, so a slope departure flags unmodelled site heterogeneity.
  Data come from simulation with known ground truth inverted by the same kinetic form, so the study verifies pipeline self-consistency and the identifiability boundary, not real parameters.

</details>


### [194] [Adaptive operator-generated subspaces for effective many-body Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00560)
*Ginanjar Utama,Hermawan Kresno Dipojono*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应Clifford-代数子空间本征求解器（A-CASE），一种基于算符生成的单参考Rayleigh-Ritz方法。它从共享Pauli期望值库重建重叠、哈密顿量、可观测量和响应矩阵，利用自适应增长评分来选择局部铅笔并抑制对称性泄漏。通过严格FCIDUMP适配器界定活性空间。在线性H4/STO-3G的CAS(4e,4o)体系中，映射扇区与独立行列式FCI一致到3.1×10^-15 Ha；九向量预算下A-CASE误差为3.019 mHa，改用双算符ADAPT-VQE参考后降至0.342 mHa。与ADAPT-VQE相比，固定参考路径用一次态制备但测量约多一个数量级的Pauli词。分组自助法传播有限shot涨落。该方法建立了从交换哈密顿量到能量、关联和响应的可执行路径，但不声称材料精度、有利标度或量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子结构和嵌入工作流最终止于有效的多体哈密顿量，而量子本征求解器研究常从手工搭建的qubit模型开始，两者之间存在脱节。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，提供一个从算符生成哈密顿量到量子本征求解的完整可执行路径。

Method: A-CASE采用单参考、算符生成的Rayleigh-Ritz框架：从一份共享的Pauli期望值池中重建重叠矩阵、哈密顿量、可观测量和响应矩阵；自适应增长评分基于重叠感知的局部铅笔并排除对称性泄漏或近似线性相关；严格FCIDUMP适配器提供活性空间边界。对比实验中比较了固定参考与ADAPT-VQE参考、ADAPT-GCIM、固定Krylov基等多种策略，并使用分组自助法传播有限shot统计涨落。

Result: 在线性H4/STO-3G的CAS(4e,4o)体系中，A-CASE与独立行列式FCI一致至3.1×10^-15 Ha。九向量预算下误差3.019 mHa，用双算符ADAPT-VQE态替代行列式参考后降至0.342 mHa。固定参考路径使用一次态制备（vs ADAPT-VQE的九十次）但测量约一个数量级更多的Pauli词。固定角度ADAPT-GCIM在最近尺寸匹配处误差13.364 mHa、迭代匹配处10.674 mHa。基准阶梯测试显示固定Krylov基更准确、更窄但条件数较差。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从交换哈密顿量到能量、关联函数和响应的完整可执行路径，验证了A-CASE的精确性和自适应策略的有效性。作者明确不声称材料级精度、有利的可扩展性或量子优势，展现了诚实的工作边界。

Abstract: Electronic-structure and embedding workflows terminate in effective many-body Hamiltonians, whereas quantum eigensolver studies often start from hand-built qubit models. We present the Adaptive Clifford-Algebra Subspace Eigensolver (A-CASE), a single-reference, operator-generated Rayleigh--Ritz method. Overlap, Hamiltonian, observable, and response matrices are reconstructed from one shared Pauli-expectation bank, while adaptive growth scores overlap-aware local pencils and rejects symmetry leakage or near-linear dependence. A strict FCIDUMP adapter supplies the active-space boundary. For linear H$_4$ in STO-3G with CAS(4e,4o), the mapped sector agrees with independent determinant FCI to $3.1\times10^{-15}$ Ha. At a nine-vector budget A-CASE has a $3.019$ mHa error; replacing the determinant reference by a two-operator ADAPT-VQE state reduces it to $0.342$ mHa, without implying a matched total-cost advantage. Under a matched contract, the fixed-reference route uses one state preparation versus ADAPT-VQE's ninety but measures roughly an order of magnitude more Pauli words. Exact fixed-angle ADAPT-GCIM gives $13.364$ mHa at the nearest size match and $10.674$ mHa at the iteration match, with its transition-pair burden reported separately. Across a broader benchmark ladder, fixed Krylov bases are generally more accurate and often narrower but substantially less well conditioned. A grouped bootstrap propagates finite-shot variability through thresholding, diagonalization, root matching, spectral weights, susceptibility, and broadening; its bands are explicitly heuristic and conditional, not finite-sample confidence certificates. The work establishes an executable path from an interchange Hamiltonian to energies, correlations, and response, without claiming materials accuracy, favorable scaling, or quantum advantage.

</details>


### [195] [Round-Trip Consistency: Bidirectional Diffusion Models Can Predict Their Own Rollout Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00675)
*Alexander Scheinker*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用单一条件潜在扩散模型,通过方向标志在时间上前向/后向逐步推进行动力系统,利用"前向i步再后向i步必须回到起点"的往返一致性(C_i)作为无需测量的测试时误差信号。在可压缩磁流体动力学(MHD)、天体物理湍流辐射混合层和人脸视频(CelebV-HQ)上验证,该信号与rollout误差显著相关(Spearman最高0.98),能检测分布外样本(AUROC 0.98),且双向训练无额外代价甚至优于单向专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归类生成模型在长rollout中会累积误差,但部署时没有ground truth可供度量。现有方法需要集成、保留数据或已知控制方程,成本高昂。作者希望寻找一种无需上述资源的、测量免费的测试时误差信号。
