Method: 训练一个条件潜在扩散模型,通过方向标志决定系统向前或向后演化。利用往返一致性(round-trip discrepancy):前向i步再后向i步应回到起点,偏差C_i作为自监督的rollout误差代理。该方法无需集成、无需保留数据、无需控制方程,只需一次额外rollout。并在上采样过程中训练一个简单校准器预测误差大小。

Result: 在MHD数据上,C_i与rollout误差排名相关性高(Spearman 0.91-0.98);校准器预测误差倍数在1.14x(68%)和1.29x(95%)内。检测分布外Orszag-Tang涡流AUROC达0.98(深度10时为1.0),而采样离散化基线在此失效。在LE-PDE-UQ湍流Navier-Stokes基准上,单模型达到10模型集成的1.3倍精度,训练成本仅为十分之一。

Conclusion: 往返一致性将动力系统的可逆性转化为生成模型的实用信任信号,为长rollout误差估计提供了测量免费的、自监督的新范式。双向训练本身也有益,互为有益的附带效果。

Abstract: Autoregressive models accumulate error over long rollouts, yet at deployment there is no ground truth to measure it against. We train a single conditional latent diffusion model that steps a dynamical system forward or backward in time via a direction flag, and show that this bidirectionality supplies a measurement-free test-time error signal: rolling forward $i$ steps and then backward $i$ steps must return the model to its start, so the round-trip discrepancy $\mathcal{C}_i$ is a self-supervised proxy for the unobservable rollout error: no ensembles, no held-out data, no governing equations, for one extra rollout. We validate on compressible magnetohydrodynamics (MHD), an astrophysical turbulent radiative mixing layer, and natural face videos (CelebV-HQ). On held-out MHD trajectories, $\mathcal{C}_i$ ranks rollout error (Spearman $0.91$-$0.98$ at fixed depth; $0.69 \pm 0.16$ within trajectories), and a simple calibrator fit on training rollouts predicts its magnitude to within $1.14\times$ ($68\%$) and $1.29\times$ ($95\%$) with near-nominal coverage - one nat beyond a depth-only predictor, transferring to all six decoded physical fields. The same signal flags the out-of-distribution Orszag-Tang vortex (AUROC $0.98$; $1.0$ by depth $10$) exactly where sampling-dispersion baselines invert, and it cuts incurred error by $15\%$ at $80\%$ coverage - three times the depth-only baseline. Bidirectional training comes at negative cost, beating direction specialists in both directions, and the backward direction doubles as a fast inverse solver. On LE-PDE-UQ's turbulent Navier-Stokes benchmark, a single bidirectional model reaches accuracy within $1.3\times$ of their ten-model ensemble at a tenth of the training cost, with the best training-free pixel-level calibration. Round-trip consistency turns reversibility into a practical trust signal for generative models.

</details>


### [196] [Physics-informed neural networks for two-dimensional wall-reactive solute dispersion in canonical shear flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00856)
*Nanda Poddar,Subham Dhar*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息神经网络(PINN)的无网格框架，用于模拟剪切流（Couette、Poiseuille、Couette-Poiseuille）中二维边界反应溶质分散问题，通过嵌入对流-扩散方程和Robin边界条件构建统一损失函数，数值结果与ADI有限差分基准高度吻合，并提取了轴向分散系数、壁面累积去除动力学和局部摄取通量等壁面分辨率输运诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典解析方法和网格数值求解器在表征剪切流中非对称反应环境下的溶质羽流时空演化时计算成本高昂，亟需一种计算效率更高且能精确解析边界耦合反应输运的新方法。

Method: 构建PINN框架，将控制方程（对流-扩散方程）和Robin边界条件嵌入统一损失函数中；在Pe=10条件下，对不可渗透壁、对称吸收(β1,β2)=(1,1)以及十倍非对称反应对比度(β1,β2)=(0.2,2)和(2,0.2)三种情景进行训练；利用网络的自动微分特性提取壁面分辨输运诊断量；以ADI有限差分解为基准验证。

Result: PINN预测与ADI基准解在非反应、对称反应和非对称反应区间均高度一致；结果显示剪切剖面主导了反应摄取在流向的组织方式，而不等的壁面反应性引入横向非对称性，从而改变了宏观扩散速率。

Conclusion: 该框架确立了PINN作为可解释的无网格工具在剪切流边界耦合反应输运分析中的有效性，为求解此类计算密集型问题提供了高效且精确的替代方案。

Abstract: The dispersion of reactive solutes in shear flows is governed by the interplay between advective stretching, transverse diffusion, and boundary exchange kinetics. While classical analytical methods and grid-based numerical solvers have extensively characterised these transport mechanisms, accurately resolving the spatiotemporal evolution of solute plumes in asymmetric reactive environments remains computationally demanding. In this study, we introduce a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework to simulate two-dimensional wall-reactive solute dispersion in canonical shear flows (Couette, Poiseuille, and Couette-Poiseuille) bounded by absorbing walls. By embedding the governing convection-diffusion equation and Robin boundary conditions into a unified loss function, the mesh-free PINN reconstructs the spatiotemporal concentration field. The network predictions are validated against an alternating-direction implicit (ADI) finite-difference benchmark, showing close agreement across non-reactive, symmetric, and asymmetric reactive regimes. The computations are carried out at $\mathrm{Pe}=10$ for impermeable walls, symmetric absorption $(β_1,β_2)=(1,1)$, and tenfold asymmetric wall-reactivity contrasts $(β_1,β_2)=(0.2,2)$ and (2,0.2). Leveraging the differentiable nature of the trained PINN, we extract wall-resolved transport diagnostics, including the apparent axial dispersion coefficient, cumulative wall-removal dynamics, and localised uptake fluxes. The results show that the imposed shear profile governs the streamwise organisation of reactive uptake, while unequal wall reactivities induce transverse asymmetry that modifies the macroscopic spreading rate. Overall, this framework establishes PINNs as an interpretable mesh-free tool for analysing boundary-coupled reactive transport in shear flows.

</details>


### [197] [Spatial-Order Hierarchy of Time-Dependent Exchange-Correlation Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01483)
*Naoki Negishi*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于时间依赖Hartree-Fock参考下的一电子约化密度矩阵非对角展开，建立了交换关联势中相互作用分量与动力学关联分量之间的可表示性约束，并在一维双电子关联猝灭系统中验证了该约束的物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间依赖密度泛函理论（TDDFT）将交换关联势分解为相互作用和动力学关联两个分量，但二者之间的结构关系尚不明确。作者试图建立二者之间的精确约束，以指导非绝热泛函的构建。

Method: 以时间依赖Hartree-Fock（TDHF）参考为基准，对一电子约化密度矩阵进行非对角展开，分析线性项与高阶项对电流密度和动力学关联分量的影响。在一维双电子关联猝灭体系中，通过保留精确相互作用分量而置零动力学分量，对比精确演化的密度展宽行为。

Result: 非零的线性项会产生非HF电流密度，但动力学关联分量仍保持HF可表示性；更高阶的非对角结构才会激活动力学分量。在一维两电子猝灭实验中，去除动力学关联分量会抑制精确演化中的密度展宽。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了密度运动方程的层级结构，为非绝热密度泛函构建提供了精确的约束条件。

Abstract: Exact time-dependent density-functional theory separates the exchange-correlation potential into interaction and kinetic-correlation components, but the structural relation between them remains unknown. We establish a representability constraint based on the off-diagonal expansion of the one-electron reduced density matrix relative to a time-dependent Hartree-Fock (TDHF) reference. A non-zero linear term generates a non-HF current density while the kinetic-correlation component remains HF representable; higher-order off-diagonal structure activates the kinetic component. In a one-dimensional two-electron correlation quench, retaining the exact interaction component while setting the kinetic component to zero suppresses the density broadening of the exact evolution. These results establish a hierarchy of density equations of motion and provide an exact constraint for non-adiabatic functional construction.

</details>


### [198] [Supersonic jet impingement on concave surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01542)
*Hemanth Chandravamsi,Dhanush Vittal Shenoy,Steven H. Frankel*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过大涡模拟、涡面模型和Powell反馈环分析，研究了超音速圆射流冲击凹曲面的气动声学共振，发现凹陷收缩使主音调振幅相对平壁参考增加高达23dB，并识别出螺旋与轴对称截击声不同的频率选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超音速射流冲击凹曲面在实际工程中（如火箭发射台、垂直起降飞行器）会产生强烈的气动声学共振和表面载荷，但壁面曲率对截击声（screech）的放大机制尚不清楚。理解凹壁几何如何影响声学反馈回路和频率选择，对于预测和控制超音速冲击噪声具有重要意义。

Method: 采用可压缩大涡模拟（LES）模拟M=1.56、Re=6×10^4的壅塞喷流冲击六种几何构型（两块平壁L/D=2.08和2.58，四种不同高斯凹面σ∈{0.4,0.8,1.6,4.0}）；结合涡面（vortex-sheet）模型预测导向射流模式；利用Powell反馈环分析和Powell-Tam源传输预算进行机理诊断。

Result: 凹陷越窄，主音调振幅相对平壁参考（L/D=2.6）增加最多23dB，壁面压力脉动和力矩也随之增大。Powell-Tam预算表明放大源于马赫盘声源幅值增强及上游反馈波更高效地返回喷口。螺旋模态的频率和径向特征函数与涡面模型预测的导向射流模式吻合，且该选择对平壁和凹壁一致，表明由等效理想膨胀射流剪切层剖面而非壁面几何决定；轴对称频率则不符合导向模式分支，遵循Powell经典环长准则。

Conclusion: 壁面曲率能有效控制截击声振幅和表面载荷；螺旋与轴对称截击声具有截然不同的频率选择机制（前者由导向射流模式决定，后者遵循Powell环长准则）；凹壁的声聚焦效应增强了上游传播波，是振幅放大的关键机理。

Abstract: The aeroacoustic resonance of round supersonic jets impinging on concave surfaces is investigated using compressible large-eddy simulations, vortex-sheet modelling, and Powell's feedback-loop analysis. The choked jets operate at an ideally expanded Mach number of $1.56$ and a Reynolds number of $6\times10^4$. Six geometries are considered: two flat plates at $L/D=2.08$ and $2.58$, where $L$ is the nozzle-to-wall distance and $D$ the nozzle exit diameter, and four Gaussian concave surfaces of fixed depth and indentation spread $σ\in\{0.4,0.8,1.6,4.0\}$. As the indentation narrows, the primary-tone amplitude increases by up to $23\,\mathrm{dB}$ relative to the flat-wall reference at $L/D=2.6$, together with larger wall-pressure fluctuations and moments. A Powell-Tam source-transfer budget attributes this amplification to increased Mach-disk source amplitude and more efficient return of the upstream feedback wave to the nozzle. The stronger upstream-propagating waves are consistent with acoustic focusing by the concave wall. For the helical cases, the measured frequencies and radial eigenfunctions agree closely with the guided jet mode predicted by the vortex-sheet model, supporting its role in closing the upstream feedback path. The same selection is recovered for concave and flat walls alike, so this tone is governed by the shear-layer profile of the equivalent ideally expanded jet rather than by the wall geometry. The axisymmetric frequencies, by contrast, coincide with no guided-mode branch and appear instead to follow Powell's classical loop-length criterion. The results identify distinct frequency-selection mechanisms for helical and axisymmetric screech and demonstrate that wall curvature provides effective control of screech amplitude and surface loading.

</details>


### [199] [FESOM2-JAX v1.0: a differentiable shadow of the ocean-sea-ice model FESOM2, cast onto GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01546)
*Nikolay V. Koldunov,Sergey Danilov,Suvarchal Cheedela,Dmitry Sidorenko,Sebastian Beyer,Patrick Scholz,Ivan Kuznetsov,Jan Streffing,Aleksei Koldunov,Dmitrii Pantiukhin,Svetlana N. Loza,Thomas Jung*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: FESOM2-JAX是一个用JAX重新实现FESOM2海洋-海冰模型的Python版本，保留了原有的非结构化网格有限体积格式，可在从笔记本电脑到256个GPU的平台上运行，且端到端可微。该模型作为Fortran原版代码的"影子"版本，通过大语言模型翻译并逐核验证。在1°分辨率下与Fortran版本进行了1958-2019年的后报实验对比，两者均态差异比与观测的差异小两个数量级，并在全球温度、盐度、热含量和海冰方面六个十年一致。单GPU即可运行完整1°配置，4×GH200节点每天可积分约113个模拟年，高达740万表面顶点（约5km）的网格可扩展至128个GPU。这是首个以可微分框架原生编写的CMIP级复杂度的全球海洋-海冰模型，也是首个在非结构化网格上的此类模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低海洋-海冰建模实验的门槛，包括新数值方法、参数化、基于梯度的校准以及混合物理-机器学习组件的开发，同时保持与原Fortran模型足够接近以便双向迁移研究成果。

Method: 使用大语言模型将FESOM2的Fortran代码翻译为JAX/Python实现，逐核验证与原版一致性；保留非结构化网格、cell-vertex有限体积格式；利用JAX的自动微分实现端到端可微；采用影子代码策略确保与原模型的兼容性。

Result: 1°分辨率下与Fortran版本均态差异比与观测的差异小两个数量级；全球温度等指标六十年一致；单GPU可运行完整1°配置，4×GH200节点日积分约113模拟年，740万顶点网格扩展至128 GPU；反向模式单次遍历完整时间循环可计算每个网格顶点参数的敏感性，并经有限差分验证。

Conclusion: FESOM2-JAX是首个以可微分框架原生编写的CMIP级全球海洋-海冰模型，也是首个非结构化网格上的此类模型。限制因素从算力转变为通信带宽，且梯度能力的引入为参数校准和混合物理-ML建模开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We present FESOM2-JAX, a Python re-implementation of the Finite-volumE Sea ice-Ocean Model (FESOM2) in JAX. The model retains the unstructured-mesh, cell-vertex finite-volume formulation of the original, runs unchanged from a laptop CPU to 256 GPUs, and is end-to-end differentiable. FESOM2-JAX is a code shadow of the Fortran model: a projection onto the Python ecosystem, translated with large language models and verified kernel by kernel against the original. It is built to lower the barrier to experimentation, from new numerics and parameterizations to gradient-based calibration and hybrid physics-machine-learning components, while remaining close enough to the original so that what is developed in the shadow can be transferred back. In a 1958-2019 hindcast at 1$^{\circ}$ equivalent resolution with identical physics and forcing, the mean states of the JAX and Fortran versions differ from each other by two orders of magnitude less than either differs from observations, and the two runs agree for six decades in global temperature, salinity, heat content, and sea ice. The complete 1$^{\circ}$ configuration fits on a single GPU, a node of four GH200 superchips integrates $\sim$113 simulated years per wall-clock day, and meshes of up to 7.4 million surface vertices ($\sim$5 km) scale to 128 GPUs. What limits the model is communication rather than arithmetic. What the shadow adds to the original is the gradient: a single reverse-mode pass through the full time loop returns the sensitivity of a model diagnostic to a parameter at every mesh vertex, verified against finite differences. To our knowledge, FESOM2-JAX is the first global ocean-sea-ice model of CMIP-class complexity written natively in a differentiable framework, and the first on an unstructured mesh.

</details>


### [200] [Machine-Learning-Accelerated Metallene Stabilization from High-Throughput Sandwich Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01779)
*Mohammad Bagheri,Pekka Koskinen*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 利用通用机器学习原子间势，构建了包含6种夹层和45种金属的1620种金属烯三明治异质结构，通过声子计算发现1208种动态稳定结构，揭示MoSe2等过渡金属二硫属化物是最有效的稳定夹层，并为金属烯的合成提供系统性指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属烯具有诱人的性质，但单层相稳定化是合成难点。实验上已证明范德华挤压法可以在MoS2三明治结构中稳定某些金属烯，但系统性的实验研究不切实际，第一性原理计算成本又过于高昂。

Method: 采用通用机器学习原子间势（universal machine-learning interatomic potentials），构建了1620种金属烯三明治异质结构（含6种夹层材料和45种金属），通过声子计算筛选动态稳定结构，并利用密度泛函理论分子动力学（DFT-MD）模拟评估选定结构的室温热稳定性。

Result: 发现1208种动态稳定结构；过渡金属二硫属化物（尤其MoSe2）在稳定金属烯方面效果显著；褶皱六边形和蜂窝状晶格稳定性最高；部分结构经DFT-MD证实具有室温热稳定性。

Conclusion: 揭示了金属烯稳定化的物理和化学调控因素，为未来金属烯的合成与应用提供了系统性的理论指导和加速方案。

Abstract: Metallenes have appealing properties, but stabilizing them in a monolayer phase poses challenges for their synthesis. A recent experiment showed that the van der Waals squeezing method can stabilize certain metallenes in a MoS2 sandwich. This pioneering work motivates systematic studies, but such studies are experimentally impractical, while first-principles modeling remains prohibitive. Here, armed with universal machine-learning interatomic potentials, we constructed 1620 metallene sandwich heterostructures containing 6 different sandwich layers and 45 metals. We performed phonon calculations, which revealed 1208 dynamically stable structures. We found that transition-metal dichalcogenides, particularly MoSe2, are highly effective in stabilizing metallenes. Specifically, buckled hexagonal and honeycomb crystal lattices exhibit the greatest stability. We further evaluated the thermal stability of selected heterostructures with density-functional theory molecular dynamics simulations at room temperature. By uncovering the physical and chemical factors governing the stabilization of metallenes, our results provide systematic insights to guide and accelerate synthesis for future applications.

</details>


### [201] [Aerodynamic Drag and Heat Transfer Corrections for Dehydrated Pollen Particles: CFD-Based Modeling of Airborne Allergen Transport in Smart Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02360)
*Omar Hamad,Samer Ali,Mahmoud Khaled,Talib Dbouk*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本研究首次通过CFD模拟，在颗粒尺度（Re_p 0.1-15）下评估了真实脱水（干燥）花粉颗粒的气动阻力和对流换热特性。研究发现干花粉的阻力系数比水合球形花粉高8%-15%，而努塞尔数低5%-15%，表明传统球形相关式无法准确模拟干燥花粉的拉格朗日输运和蒸发过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气花粉输运对城市空气质量评估、过敏风险预测和智慧城市规划至关重要，但传统弥散模型通常将花粉假设为光滑球形颗粒，忽略了花粉脱水后颗粒形态变化对空气动力学和热行为的影响，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 采用先进的计算流体动力学（CFD）模拟，对真实脱水（干燥）花粉颗粒在雷诺数0.1≤Re_p≤15范围内（对应0.27-30 km/h的大气风速）评估气动阻力系数和对流换热（努塞尔数）。

Result: 干花粉颗粒的阻力系数比水合球形花粉预测值高8%-15%，而努塞尔数低5%-15%，两者均存在显著偏差。

Conclusion: 传统球形相关式不足以用于模拟干燥花粉的拉格朗日输运和蒸发，建模城市环境中的空气过敏原输运时必须考虑真实的脱水花粉形态。

Abstract: Airborne pollen transport is a key concern for urban air-quality assessment, allergy-risk forecasting, and smart-city planning. However, conventional dispersion models generally assume smooth spherical particles, neglecting how pollen dehydration alters particle morphology and impacts aerodynamic and thermal behavior. To address this gap, this study presents, for the first time, advanced CFD simulations evaluating the aerodynamic drag forces and convective heat transfer of realistically dehydrated (dry) pollen particles. Investigations are conducted at Reynolds numbers ($0.1 \leq Re_p \leq 15$) at the particle's scale corresponding to realistic atmospheric wind speeds ranging from 0.27 to 30 km/h. The findings reveal that dry pollen particles exhibit drag coefficients 8% to 15% higher than those predicted for hydrated pollen spherical particles. Conversely, their Nusselt numbers are 5% to 15% lower than those for hydrated pollen particles. These considerable deviations confirm that conventional spherical correlations are inadequate for simulating dry pollen Lagrangian transport and evaporation. These findings highlight the need to account for realistic dehydrated shapes when modeling airborne allergen transport in urban environments.

</details>


### [202] [Thermalization of open quantum systems with pseudomodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02517)
*R. Kevin Kessing,Thibaut Lacroix,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了赝模方法中系统是否满足细致平衡条件并热化到吉布斯态的问题，探讨了在何种参数条件下赝模模型能实现（或仅能实现受限频率范围内的"弱"细致平衡），并提出了一种结合厄米与非厄米赝模的方案以获得平坦的有效温度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赝模方法可以精确且无近似地描述量子系统与强耦合浴的相互作用，但一般情况下系统耦合到赝模后并不会热化到系统的吉布斯态。当系统-浴耦合非微扰时这符合预期，但在渐近弱耦合情形下与常见的热力学直觉相冲突。因此需要厘清在什么条件下赝模模型能保证热力学一致性。

Method: 作者系统研究了赝模模型满足细致平衡条件的参数条件，分析了如何通过特定参数选择强制实现仅限有限频率范围的"弱"细致平衡，并提出结合厄米与非厄米赝模来构造平坦有效温度分布的方法。

Result: 建立了赝模模型满足细致平衡（进而热化到系统吉布斯态）的判据，并展示了如何通过参数选择实现受限频率范围的弱细致平衡，以及如何利用厄米/非厄米赝模组合获得平坦的有效温度分布。

Conclusion: 本文所建立的结果和判据对需要在热力学一致性约束下构建赝模模型的应用场景具有重要的指导意义。

Abstract: Pseudomode approaches allow for an exact and unapproximated description of a quantum system interacting arbitrarily strongly with a bath. In general, a system coupled to pseudomodes will not thermalize to the system's Gibbs state: This is to be expected when the system-bath coupling is non-perturbative, but conflicts with common thermodynamic intuition when the system-bath coupling is asymptotically weak. We explore under which circumstances pseudomode models satisfy detailed balance (and subsequently thermalize to the system Gibbs state) and how specific choices of parameters can force "weak" detailed balance that is restricted to a limited frequency range. A combination of Hermitian and non-Hermitian pseudomodes that yields a flat effective-temperature profile is also considered. The results and criteria established here are relevant for the construction of pseudomode models in contexts where thermodynamic consistency is required.

</details>
